## <ruby><rb><u>约书亚</u></rb><rt>IAH-SHÜ-ÜÔ</rt><rb>记</rb><rt>KYI</rt></ruby>

### Is. I.

<ruby><rb>耶和华个</rb><rt>YIÆ-ʽO-WÔ-GO</rt><rb>奴仆</rb><rt>nu-boh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>死</rb><rt>si</rt><rb>之后</rb><rt>ts-ʽeo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>耶和华</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>吩咐</rb><rt>feng-fu</rt><rb>侍值</rb><rt>z-dzih</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb><u>嫩</u>个</rb><rt>Neng-go</rt><rb>儿子</rb><rt>ng-ts</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>约书亚</rb><rt>Iah-shü-üô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：<sup>2</sup> 「<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>奴仆</rb><rt>nu-boh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>已经</rb><rt>yi-kying</rt><rb>死了</rb><rt>si-de</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>现在</rb><rt>yin-dzæ</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>众</rb><rt>cong</rt><rb>百姓</rb><rt>pah-sing</rt><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>起来</rb><rt>kyʽi-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>过</rb><rt>ko</rt><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>约但</rb><rt>Iah-dæn</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>河</rb><rt>ʽo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>赐拨</rb><rt>s-peh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>子孙个</rb><rt>ts-seng-go</rt><rb>地</rb><rt>di</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>。<sup>3</sup> <ruby><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>Ng-lah</rt><rb>脚底</rb><rt>kyiah-ti</rt><rb>凡百</rb><rt>væn-pah</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>踏个</rb><rt>dah-go</rt><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>已经</rb><rt>yi-kying</rt><rb>赐拨</rb><rt>s-peh</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>像</rb><rt>ziang</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>话过</rb><rt>wô-ko</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt></ruby>。<sup>4</sup> <ruby><rb>从</rb><rt>Dzong</rt><rb>旷野</rb><rt>kwʽông-iæ</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>黎巴嫩</rb><rt>Li-pô-neng</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>直到</rb><rt>dzih-tao</rt><rb>大</rb><rt>do</rt><rb>河</rb><rt>ʽo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>伯拉</rb><rt>Pah-lah</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>河</rb><rt>ʽo</rt></ruby>，<u><ruby><rb>赫</rb><rt>Hah</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人个</rb><rt>nying-go</rt><rb>全地</rb><rt>djün-di</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>还有</rb><rt>wa-yiu</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>向</rb><rt>hyiang</rt><rb>日头</rb><rt>nyih-deo</rt><rb>落山个</rb><rt>loh-sæn-go</rt><rb>大</rb><rt>do</rt><rb>海</rb><rt>hæ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>尔拉个</rb><rt>ng-lah-go</rt><rb>地界</rb><rt>di-ka</rt></ruby>。<sup>5</sup> <ruby><rb>尔</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>在生</rb><rt>dzæ-seng</rt><rb>日子</rb><rt>nyih-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>呒人</rb><rt>m-nying</rt><rb>能够</rb><rt>neng-keo</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>面前</rb><rt>min-zin</rt><rb>立勒住</rb><rt>lih-leh-djü</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>必定</rb><rt>pih-ding</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>来东</rb><rt>læ-tong</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>正像</rb><rt>tsing-ziang</rt><rb>前头</rb><rt>zin-deo</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>来间</rb><rt>læ-kæn</rt><rb>一样</rb><rt>ih-yiang</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>弗会</rb><rt>feh-we</rt><rb>离开</rb><rt>li-kʽæ</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>弗会</rb><rt>feh-we</rt><rb>弃掉</rb><rt>kyʽi-diao</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt></ruby>。<sup>6</sup> <ruby><rb>尔</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>刚强</rb><rt>kông-gyiang</rt><rb>放</rb><rt>fông</rt><rb>大</rb><rt>do</rt><rb>胆子</rb><rt>tæn-ts</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>因为</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>必定</rb><rt>pih-ding</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>箇个</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>百姓</rb><rt>pah-sing</rt><rb>得着</rb><rt>teh-djoh</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>向</rb><rt>hyiang</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>列祖</rb><rt>lih-tsu</rt><rb>罚咒</rb><rt>vah-tsiu</rt><rb>赐拨</rb><rt>s-peh</rt><rb>其拉个</rb><rt>gyi-lah-go</rt><rb>地</rb><rt>di</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>产业</rb><rt>tsʽæn-nyih</rt></ruby>。<sup>7</sup> <ruby><rb>只要</rb><rt>Tsih-iao</rt><rb>刚强</rb><rt>kông-gyiang</rt><rb>放</rb><rt>fông</rt><rb>大</rb><rt>do</rt><rb>胆子</rb><rt>tæn-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>遵依</rb><rt>tseng-i</rt><rb>全</rb><rt>djün</rt><rb>律法</rb><rt>lih-fah</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>奴仆</rb><rt>nu-boh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>吩咐</rb><rt>feng-fu</rt><rb>尔个</rb><rt>ng-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>弗可</rb><rt>feh-kʽo</rt><rb>转向</rb><rt>cün-hyiang</rt><rb>顺手</rb><rt>jing-siu</rt><rb>借手</rb><rt>tsia-siu</rt><rb>离开</rb><rt>li-kʽæ</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>凡百</rb><rt>væn-pah</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>去个</rb><rt>kyʽi-go</rt><rb>路</rb><rt>lu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>事干</rb><rt>z-ken</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>顺流</rb><rt>jing-liu</rt></ruby>。<sup>8</sup> <ruby><rb>各</rb><rt>Koh</rt><rb>律法</rb><rt>lih-fah</rt><rb>书</rb><rt>shü</rt><rb>弗可</rb><rt>feh-kʽo</rt><rb>离开</rb><rt>li-kʽæ</rt><rb>尔个</rb><rt>ng-go</rt><rb>口</rb><rt>kʽeo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>应当</rb><rt>ing-tông</rt><rb>日夜</rb><rt>nyih-yia</rt><rb>思忖</rb><rt>s-tsʽeng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>能够</rb><rt>neng-keo</rt><rb>遵依</rb><rt>tseng-i</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>照</rb><rt>tsiao</rt><rb>凡百</rb><rt>væn-pah</rt><rb>写来</rb><rt>sia-læ</rt><rb>箇本</rb><rt>keh-peng</rt><rb>书里</rb><rt>shü-li</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>箇遭</rb><rt>keh-tsao</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>可以</rb><rt>kʽo-yi</rt><rb>一路</rb><rt>ih-lu</rt><rb>顺境</rb><rt>jing-kying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>事干</rb><rt>z-ken</rt><rb>顺流</rb><rt>jing-liu</rt></ruby><sup>\[1\]</sup>。<sup>9</sup> <ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>难道</rb><rt>næn-dao</rt><rb>呒得</rb><rt>m-neh</rt><rb>吩咐</rb><rt>feng-fu</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>过</rb><rt>ko</rt><rb>吗</rb><rt>ma</rt></ruby>？<ruby><rb>用</rb><rt>Yüong</rt><rb>刚强</rb><rt>kông-gyiang</rt><rb>放</rb><rt>fông</rt><rb>大</rb><rt>do</rt><rb>胆子</rb><rt>tæn-ts</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>惊𢞕</rb><rt>kying-hoh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>胆小</rb><rt>tæn-siao</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>因为</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>弗论</rb><rt>feh-leng</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>何里</rb><rt>ʽah-li</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>耶和华</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>尔个</rb><rt>ng-go</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>必定</rb><rt>pih-ding</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>来东</rb><rt>læ-tong</rt></ruby>。」<sup>10</sup> <ruby><rb>介</rb><rt>Ka</rt><rb>时候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>约书亚</rb><rt>Iah-shü-üô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>吩咐</rb><rt>feng-fu</rt><rb>百姓个</rb><rt>pah-sing-go</rt><rb>官长</rb><rt>kwun-tsiang</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：<sup>11</sup> 「<ruby><rb>尔</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>走遍</rb><rt>tseo-pin</rt><rb>营盘</rb><rt>ying-bun</rt><rb>吩咐</rb><rt>feng-fu</rt><rb>百姓</rb><rt>pah-sing</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：『<ruby><rb>用</rb><rt>Yüong</rt><rb>预备</rb><rt>yü-be</rt><rb>口粮</rb><rt>kʽeo-liang</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>因为</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>再</rb><rt>tsæ</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>三日</rb><rt>sæn-nyih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>过</rb><rt>ko</rt><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>约但</rb><rt>Iah-dæn</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>河</rb><rt>ʽo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>进去</rb><rt>tsing-kyʽi</rt><rb>得着</rb><rt>teh-djoh</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>耶和华</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>赐拨</rb><rt>s-peh</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>产业</rb><rt>tsʽæn-nyih</rt><rb>地</rb><rt>di</rt></ruby>。』」

<sup>12</sup> <ruby><rb>当时</rb><rt>Tông-z</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>约书亚</rb><rt>Iah-shü-üô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>流便</rb><rt>Liu-bin</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>支派</rb><rt>ts-pʽa</rt></ruby>、<u><ruby><rb>迦得</rb><rt>Kyüô-teh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>支派</rb><rt>ts-pʽa</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>玛拿西</rb><rt>Mô-nô-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>半</rb><rt>pun</rt><rb>支派</rb><rt>ts-pʽa</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：<sup>13</sup> 「<ruby><rb>用</rb><rt>Yüong</rt><rb>记得</rb><rt>kyi-teh</rt><rb>耶和华个</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô-go</rt><rb>奴仆</rb><rt>nu-boh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>吩咐</rb><rt>feng-fu</rt><rb>尔拉个</rb><rt>ng-lah-go</rt><rb>说话</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：『<ruby><rb>耶和华</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>尔拉个</rb><rt>ng-lah-go</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>已经</rb><rt>yi-kying</rt><rb>赐拨</rb><rt>s-peh</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>安息</rb><rt>en-sih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>赐</rb><rt>s</rt><rb>箇个</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>地</rb><rt>di</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt></ruby>。』<sup>14</sup> <ruby><rb>尔拉个</rb><rt>ng-lah-go</rt><rb>妻子</rb><rt>tsʽi-ts</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>小人</rb><rt>siao-nying</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>牲口</rb><rt>sang-kʽeo</rt><rb>可以</rb><rt>kʽo-yi</rt><rb>庉</rb><rt>deng</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>赐拨</rb><rt>s-peh</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>约但</rb><rt>Iah-dæn</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>河外个</rb><rt>ʽo-nga-go</rt><rb>地</rb><rt>di</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>但是</rb><rt>dæn-z</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>凡百</rb><rt>væn-pah</rt><rb>能够</rb><rt>neng-keo</rt><rb>打仗个</rb><rt>tang-tsiang-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>整顿</rb><rt>tsing-teng</rt><rb>兵队</rb><rt>ping-de</rt><rb>过去</rb><rt>ko-kyʽi</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>兄弟</rb><rt>hyüong-di</rt><rb>面前</rb><rt>min-zin</rt><rb>走</rb><rt>tseo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>帮助</rb><rt>pông-dzu</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt></ruby>；<sup>15</sup> <ruby><rb>等到</rb><rt>teng-tao</rt><rb>耶和华</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>赐拨</rb><rt>s-peh</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>兄弟</rb><rt>hyüong-di</rt><rb>安息</rb><rt>en-sih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>像</rb><rt>ziang</rt><rb>赐拨</rb><rt>s-peh</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>一样</rb><rt>ih-yiang</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>等到</rb><rt>teng-tao</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>得着</rb><rt>teh-djoh</rt><rb>耶和华</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>尔拉个</rb><rt>ng-lah-go</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>赐拨</rb><rt>s-peh</rt><rb>其拉个</rb><rt>gyi-lah-go</rt><rb>地</rb><rt>di</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>箇遭</rb><rt>keh-tsao</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>可以</rb><rt>kʽo-yi</rt><rb>归去</rb><rt>kyü-kyʽi</rt><rb>享受</rb><rt>hyiang-ziu</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>得着</rb><rt>teh-djoh</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>地</rb><rt>di</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>耶和华个</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô-go</rt><rb>奴仆</rb><rt>nu-boh</rt><rb>赐拨</rb><rt>s-peh</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>约但</rb><rt>Iah-dæn</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>河外</rb><rt>ʽo-nga</rt><rb>向</rb><rt>hyiang</rt><rb>日头</rb><rt>nyih-deo</rt><rb>出去个</rb><rt>cʽih-kyʽi-go</rt><rb>地</rb><rt>di</rt></ruby>。」<sup>16</sup> <ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt><rb>回答</rb><rt>we-teh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>约书亚</rb><rt>Iah-shü-üô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>尔</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>凡百</rb><rt>væn-pah</rt><rb>吩咐</rb><rt>feng-fu</rt><rb>阿拉个</rb><rt>ah-lah-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>依勒</rb><rt>i-leh</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>差</rb><rt>tsʽa</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>何里</rb><rt>ʽah-li</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>。<sup>17</sup> <ruby><rb>正像</rb><rt>Tsing-ziang</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>从前</rb><rt>dzong-zin</rt><rb>凡百</rb><rt>væn-pah</rt><rb>事干</rb><rt>z-ken</rt><rb>听从</rb><rt>tʽing-dzong</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>听从</rb><rt>tʽing-dzong</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>只要</rb><rt>tsih-iao</rt><rb>耶和华</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>尔个</rb><rt>ng-go</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>来东</rb><rt>læ-tong</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>像</rb><rt>ziang</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>来间</rb><rt>læ-kæn</rt><rb>一样</rb><rt>ih-yiang</rt></ruby>。<sup>18</sup> <ruby><rb>弗论</rb><rt>Feh-leng</rt><rb>啥个</rb><rt>soh-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>违背</rb><rt>we-be</rt><rb>尔个</rb><rt>ng-go</rt><rb>命令</rb><rt>ming-ling</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>听从</rb><rt>tʽing-dzong</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>凡百</rb><rt>væn-pah</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>吩咐</rb><rt>feng-fu</rt><rb>其个</rb><rt>gyi-go</rt><rb>说话</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>必定</rb><rt>pih-ding</rt><rb>治</rb><rt>djü</rt><rb>死</rb><rt>si</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>只要</rb><rt>tsih-iao</rt><rb>刚强</rb><rt>kông-gyiang</rt><rb>放</rb><rt>fông</rt><rb>大</rb><rt>do</rt><rb>胆子</rb><rt>tæn-ts</rt><rb>是了</rb><rt>z-de</rt></ruby>。」

### Is. II.

<ruby><rb><u>嫩</u>个</rb><rt>NENG-GO</rt><rb>儿子</rb><rt>ng-ts</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>约书亚</rb><rt>Iah-shü-üô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>从</rb><rt>dzong</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>失亭</rb><rt>Shih-ding</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>差</rb><rt>tsʽa</rt><rb>两个</rb><rt>liang-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>私下</rb><rt>s-ʽô</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>探看</rb><rt>tʽen-kʽen</rt><rb>地</rb><rt>di</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>去</rb><rt>Kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>看</rb><rt>kʽen</rt><rb>箇个</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>地</rb><rt>di</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶利哥</rb><rt>Yiæ-li-ko</rt></ruby></u>。」<ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>走进</rb><rt>tseo-tsing</rt><rb>一个</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>妓女</rb><rt>gyi-nyü</rt><rb>叫勒</rb><rt>kyiao-leh</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>喇合</u>个</rb><rt>Lah-ʽeh-go</rt><rb>屋里</rb><rt>oh-li</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>箇头</rb><rt>keh-deo</rt><rb>庉宿</rb><rt>deng-soh</rt></ruby>。<sup>2</sup> <ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>Yiu</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>通知</rb><rt>tʽong-cü</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶利哥</rb><rt>Yiæ-li-ko</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>王</rb><rt>wông</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>今朝</rb><rt>Kying-tsiao</rt><rb>夜到</rb><rt>yia-tao</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>几个</rb><rt>kyi-go</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>子孙</rb><rt>ts-seng</rt><rb>走进</rb><rt>tseo-tsing</rt><rb>此地</rb><rt>tsʽ-di</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>探看</rb><rt>tʽen-kʽen</rt><rb>箇个</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>地</rb><rt>di</rt></ruby>。」<sup>3</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶利哥</rb><rt>Yiæ-li-ko</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>王</rb><rt>wông</rt><rb>差</rb><rt>tsʽa</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>喇合</rb><rt>Lah-ʽeh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>箇星</rb><rt>Keh-sing</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>走进</rb><rt>tseo-tsing</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>屋里个</rb><rt>oh-li-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>带</rb><rt>ta</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>出来</rb><rt>cʽih-læ</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>因为</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>探看</rb><rt>tʽen-kʽen</rt><rb>箇个</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>全地</rb><rt>djün-di</rt></ruby>。」<sup>4</sup> <ruby><rb>女人</rb><rt>Nyü-nying</rt><rb>将</rb><rt>tsiang</rt><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>两个</rb><rt>liang-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>藏囥</rb><rt>dzông-kʽông</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>Yiu</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>只是</rb><rt>tsih-z</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>晓得</rb><rt>hyiao-teh</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>从</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>何里</rb><rt>ʽah-li</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>。<sup>5</sup> <ruby><rb>天</rb><rt>tʽin</rt><rb>暗落</rb><rt>en-loh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>关</rb><rt>kwæn</rt><rb>城门</rb><rt>dzing-meng</rt><rb>时候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>走出</rb><rt>tseo-cʽih</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>晓得</rb><rt>hyiao-teh</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>何里</rb><rt>ʽah-li</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>快快</rb><rt>kwʽa-kwʽa</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>追</rb><rt>tse</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>一定</rb><rt>ih-ding</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>追着</rb><rt>tse-djoh</rt></ruby>。」<sup>6</sup> <ruby><rb>但是</rb><rt>Dæn-z</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>已经</rb><rt>yi-kying</rt><rb>领</rb><rt>ling</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>走上</rb><rt>tseo-zông</rt><rb>屋顶</rb><rt>oh-teng</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>将</rb><rt>tsiang</rt><rb>屋顶</rb><rt>oh-teng</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>铺间</rb><rt>pʽu-kæn</rt><rb>麻杆</rb><rt>mô-ken</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>藏囥</rb><rt>dzông-kʽông</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt></ruby>。<sup>7</sup> <ruby><rb>箇星</rb><rt>Keh-sing</rt><rb>追赶个</rb><rt>tse-ken-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>依</rb><rt>i</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>约但</rb><rt>Iah-dæn</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>箇埭</rb><rt>keh-da</rt><rb>路</rb><rt>lu</rt><rb>追</rb><rt>tse</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>渡头</rb><rt>du-deo</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>一</rb><rt>ih</rt><rb>走</rb><rt>tseo</rt><rb>出</rb><rt>cʽih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>城门</rb><rt>dzing-meng</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>关了</rb><rt>kwæn-de</rt></ruby>。<sup>8</sup> <ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>Keh</rt><rb>两人</rb><rt>liang-nying</rt><rb>还</rb><rt>wa</rt><rb>未曾</rb><rt>vi-dzeng</rt><rb>困</rb><rt>kwʽeng</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>女人</rb><rt>nyü-nying</rt><rb>走上</rb><rt>tseo-zông</rt><rb>屋顶</rb><rt>oh-teng</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt></ruby>，<sup>9</sup> <ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>晓得</rb><rt>hyiao-teh</rt><rb>耶和华</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>已经</rb><rt>yi-kying</rt><rb>将</rb><rt>tsiang</rt><rb>箇个</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>地</rb><rt>di</rt><rb>赐拨</rb><rt>s-peh</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>为了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>吃𢞕</rb><rt>kyʽih-hoh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>此地</rb><rt>tsʽ-di</rt><rb>一切个</rb><rt>ih-tsʽih-go</rt><rb>百姓</rb><rt>pah-sing</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>为了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>丧胆</rb><rt>sông-tæn</rt></ruby>。<sup>10</sup> <ruby><rb>因为</rb><rt>Ing-we</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>已经</rb><rt>yi-kying</rt><rb>听闻</rb><rt>tʽing-meng</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>出</rb><rt>cʽih</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>埃及</rb><rt>Yiæ-gyih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>时候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>耶和华</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>为了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>红</u>海个</rb><rt>ʽOng-hæ-go</rt><rb>水</rb><rt>shü</rt><rb>干燥</rb><rt>ken-sao</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>还有</rb><rt>wa-yiu</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>向</rb><rt>hyiang</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>约但</rb><rt>Iah-dæn</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>河</rb><rt>ʽo</rt><rb>外</rb><rt>nga</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>亚摩利</rb><rt>Üô-mo-li</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>两个</rb><rt>liang-go</rt><rb>王</rb><rt>wông</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>咋</rb><rt>dza</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>西宏</rb><rt>Si-ʽong</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>噩</rb><rt>Ngoh</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>灭掉个</rb><rt>mih-diao-go</rt></ruby>。<sup>11</sup> <ruby><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>Ah-lah</rt><rb>一</rb><rt>ih</rt><rb>听闻</rb><rt>tʽing-meng</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>事干</rb><rt>z-ken</rt><rb>心</rb><rt>sing</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>衰了</rb><rt>sæ-de</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>为了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt><rb>尔个</rb><rt>ng-go</rt><rb>缘故</rb><rt>yün-kwu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>一个</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>呒得</rb><rt>m-neh</rt><rb>胆量</rb><rt>tæn-liang</rt><rb>了</rb><rt>de</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>因为</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>尔个</rb><rt>ng-go</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>耶和华</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>天上</rb><rt>tʽin-zông</rt><rb>地下个</rb><rt>di-ʽô-go</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt></ruby>。<sup>12</sup> <ruby><rb>所以</rb><rt>Sô-yi</rt><rb>现在</rb><rt>yin-dzæ</rt><rb>求</rb><rt>gyiu</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>指点</rb><rt>ts-tin</rt><rb>耶和华</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>罚</rb><rt>vah</rt><rb>一个</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>咒</rb><rt>tsiu</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>既然</rb><rt>kyi-jün</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>恩惠</rb><rt>eng-we</rt><rb>待</rb><rt>dæ</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>必定</rb><rt>pih-ding</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>恩惠</rb><rt>eng-we</rt><rb>待</rb><rt>dæ</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>阿爹</rb><rt>ah-tia</rt><rb>一家</rb><rt>ih-kô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>赐拨</rb><rt>s-peh</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>一个</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>实为个</rb><rt>jih-we-go</rt><rb>凭据</rb><rt>bing-kyü</rt></ruby>：<sup>13</sup> <ruby><rb>定</rb><rt>ding</rt><rb>救活</rb><rt>kyiu-weh</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>爹娘</rb><rt>tia-nyiang</rt><rb>兄弟</rb><rt>hyüong-di</rt><rb>姊妹</rb><rt>tsi-me</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>凡百</rb><rt>væn-pah</rt><rb>属于</rb><rt>joh-ü</rt><rb>其拉个</rb><rt>gyi-lah-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>救</rb><rt>kyiu</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>性命</rb><rt>sing-ming</rt><rb>省勒</rb><rt>sæn-leh</rt><rb>灭亡</rb><rt>mih-vông</rt></ruby>。」<sup>14</sup> <ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>Keh</rt><rb>两个</rb><rt>liang-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>回答</rb><rt>we-teh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>尔</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>若</rb><rt>ziah</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>泄漏</rb><rt>sih-leo</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>箇起</rb><rt>keh-kyʽi</rt><rb>事干</rb><rt>z-ken</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>性命</rb><rt>sing-ming</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>抵</rb><rt>ti</rt><rb>尔个</rb><rt>ng-go</rt><rb>性命</rb><rt>sing-ming</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>耶和华</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>将</rb><rt>tsiang</rt><rb>箇个</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>地</rb><rt>di</rt><rb>交付</rb><rt>kao-fu</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>时候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>必定</rb><rt>pih-ding</rt><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>恩惠</rb><rt>eng-we</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>诚实</rb><rt>dzing-jih</rt><rb>待</rb><rt>dæ</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt></ruby>。」<sup>15</sup> <ruby><rb>女人</rb><rt>Nyü-nying</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>绳</rb><rt>zing</rt><rb>从</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>槛头</rb><rt>kʽæn-deo</rt><rb>吊</rb><rt>tiao</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>落</rb><rt>loh</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>因为</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>屋</rb><rt>oh</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>城子</rb><rt>dzing-ts</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>庉</rb><rt>deng</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>城墙</rb><rt>dzing-ziang</rt><rb>上</rb><rt>zông</rt></ruby>。<sup>16</sup> <ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>Ng-lah</rt><rb>快</rb><rt>kwʽa</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>山里</rb><rt>sæn-li</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>恐怕</rb><rt>kʽong-pʽô</rt><rb>被</rb><rt>be</rt><rb>追赶</rb><rt>tse-ken</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>碰着</rb><rt>pʽong-djoh</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>箇头</rb><rt>keh-deo</rt><rb>躲避</rb><rt>to-bi</rt><rb>三日</rb><rt>sæn-nyih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等到</rb><rt>teng-tao</rt><rb>追赶</rb><rt>tse-ken</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>归来</rb><rt>kyü-læ</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>后头</rb><rt>ʽeo-deo</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>可以</rb><rt>kʽo-yi</rt><rb>走</rb><rt>tseo</rt><rb>尔拉个</rb><rt>ng-lah-go</rt><rb>路</rb><rt>lu</rt></ruby>。」<sup>17</sup> <ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>Keh</rt><rb>两个</rb><rt>liang-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>尔</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>罚个</rb><rt>vah-go</rt><rb>咒</rb><rt>tsiu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>必定</rb><rt>pih-ding</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>失信</rb><rt>shih-sing</rt></ruby>。<sup>18</sup> <ruby><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>Ah-lah</rt><rb>走进</rb><rt>tseo-tsing</rt><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>地</rb><rt>di</rt><rb>时候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>缚</rb><rt>bo</rt><rb>箇根</rb><rt>keh-keng</rt><rb>红绳</rb><rt>ʽong-zing</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>吊</rb><rt>tiao</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>落个</rb><rt>loh-go</rt><rb>槛头</rb><rt>kʽæn-deo</rt><rb>上</rb><rt>zông</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>聚集</rb><rt>jü-jih</rt><rb>尔个</rb><rt>ng-go</rt><rb>爹娘</rb><rt>tia-nyiang</rt><rb>兄弟</rb><rt>hyüong-di</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>阿爹个</rb><rt>ah-tia-go</rt><rb>全家</rb><rt>djün-kô</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>尔个</rb><rt>ng-go</rt><rb>屋里</rb><rt>oh-li</rt></ruby>。<sup>19</sup> <ruby><rb>弗论</rb><rt>Feh-leng</rt><rb>谁</rb><rt>jü</rt><rb>走出</rb><rt>tseo-cʽih</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>墙门</rb><rt>ziang-meng</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>路街里</rb><rt>lu-ka-li</rt><rb>去个</rb><rt>kyʽi-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>血</rb><rt>hyüih</rt><rb>归到</rb><rt>kwe-tao</rt><rb>自</rb><rt>zi</rt><rb>头上</rb><rt>deo-zông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>呒罪</rb><rt>m-ze</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>弗论</rb><rt>feh-leng</rt><rb>谁</rb><rt>jü</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>屋里</rb><rt>oh-li</rt><rb>来东</rb><rt>læ-tong</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>若</rb><rt>ziah</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>害</rb><rt>ʽæ</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其个</rb><rt>gyi-go</rt><rb>血</rb><rt>hyüih</rt><rb>归到</rb><rt>kwe-tao</rt><rb>阿拉个</rb><rt>ah-lah-go</rt><rb>头上</rb><rt>deo-zông</rt></ruby>。<sup>20</sup> <ruby><rb>尔</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>若</rb><rt>ziah</rt><rb>泄漏</rb><rt>sih-leo</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>箇起</rb><rt>keh-kyʽi</rt><rb>事干</rb><rt>z-ken</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>罚个</rb><rt>vah-go</rt><rb>咒</rb><rt>tsiu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>呒</rb><rt>m</rt><rb>关涉</rb><rt>kwæn-dzih</rt></ruby>。」<sup>21</sup> <ruby><rb>女人</rb><rt>Nyü-nying</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>照</rb><rt>Tsiao</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>说话</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt><rb>是了</rb><rt>z-de</rt></ruby>。」<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>讴</rb><rt>eo</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>缚</rb><rt>bo</rt><rb>箇根</rb><rt>keh-keng</rt><rb>红绳</rb><rt>ʽong-zing</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>槛头</rb><rt>kʽæn-deo</rt><rb>上</rb><rt>zông</rt></ruby>。<sup>22</sup> <ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>走到</rb><rt>tseo-tao</rt><rb>山里</rb><rt>sæn-li</rt><rb>庉</rb><rt>deng</rt><rb>三日</rb><rt>sæn-nyih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等到</rb><rt>teng-tao</rt><rb>追赶</rb><rt>tse-ken</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>走转来</rb><rt>tseo-cün-læ</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>追赶</rb><rt>tse-ken</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>一路</rb><rt>ih-lu</rt><rb>寻</rb><rt>zing</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>终于</rb><rt>cong-ü</rt><rb>寻弗着</rb><rt>zing-feh-djoh</rt></ruby>。<sup>23</sup> <ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>Keh</rt><rb>两个</rb><rt>liang-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>归去</rb><rt>kyü-kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>走落</rb><rt>tseo-loh</rt><rb>山</rb><rt>sæn</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>过</rb><rt>ko</rt><rb>河</rb><rt>ʽo</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>嫩</u>个</rb><rt>Neng-go</rt><rb>儿子</rb><rt>ng-ts</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>约书亚</rb><rt>Iah-shü-üô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>将</rb><rt>tsiang</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>碰见个</rb><rt>pʽong-kyin-go</rt><rb>事干</rb><rt>z-ken</rt><rb>拢总</rb><rt>long-tsong</rt><rb>告诉</rb><rt>kao-su</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>。<sup>24</sup> <ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>约书亚</rb><rt>Iah-shü-üô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>耶和华</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>真正</rb><rt>tsing-tsing</rt><rb>把</rb><rt>pô</rt><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>全</rb><rt>djün</rt><rb>地</rb><rt>di</rt><rb>交代</rb><rt>kao-dæ</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>手里</rb><rt>siu-li</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>因为</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>地方个</rb><rt>di-fông-go</rt><rb>众</rb><rt>cong</rt><rb>百姓</rb><rt>pah-sing</rt><rb>为了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>丧胆</rb><rt>sông-tæn</rt></ruby>。」

---

### Is. III.

<ruby><rb>第二日</rb><rt>DI-NYI-NYIH</rt><rb>早</rb><rt>tsao</rt><rb>天亮</rb><rt>tʽin-liang</rt></ruby>，<u><ruby><rb>约书亚</rb><rt>Iah-shü-üô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>起来</rb><rt>kyʽi-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>众</rb><rt>cong</rt><rb>百姓</rb><rt>pah-sing</rt><rb>从</rb><rt>dzong</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>失亭</rb><rt>Shih-ding</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>动身</rb><rt>dong-sing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>走到</rb><rt>tseo-tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>约但</rb><rt>Iah-dæn</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>河边</rb><rt>ʽo-pin</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>弗曾</rb><rt>feh-zing</rt><rb>过去</rb><rt>ko-kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>箇头</rb><rt>keh-deo</rt><rb>庉宿</rb><rt>deng-soh</rt></ruby>。<sup>2</sup> <ruby><rb>过了</rb><rt>Ko-leh</rt><rb>三日</rb><rt>sæn-nyih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>官长</rb><rt>kwun-tsiang</rt><rb>走遍</rb><rt>tseo-pin</rt><rb>营盘</rb><rt>ying-bun</rt></ruby>；<sup>3</sup> <ruby><rb>吩咐</rb><rt>feng-fu</rt><rb>百姓</rb><rt>pah-sing</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>Ng-lah</rt><rb>看见</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>耶和华个</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô-go</rt><rb>约箱</rb><rt>iah-siang</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>祭司</rb><rt>tsi-s</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>利未</rb><rt>Li-vi</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>抬</rb><rt>dæ</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>时候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇遭</rb><rt>keh-tsao</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>从</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt><rb>动身</rb><rt>dong-sing</rt><rb>跟勒</rb><rt>keng-leh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>。<sup>4</sup> <ruby><rb>不过</rb><rt>Peh-ko</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>约箱</rb><rt>iah-siang</rt><rb>应当</rb><rt>ing-tông</rt><rb>隔开</rb><rt>kah-kʽæ</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>空</rb><rt>kʽong</rt><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>大约</rb><rt>da-iah</rt><rb>差</rb><rt>tsʽô</rt><rb>二千</rb><rt>nyi-tsʽin</rt><rb>阿买</rb><rt>ah-ma</rt></ruby><sup>\[1\]</sup>；<ruby><rb>弗可</rb><rt>feh-kʽo</rt><rb>近拢</rb><rt>gying-long</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>晓得</rb><rt>hyiao-teh</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>应当</rb><rt>ing-tông</rt><rb>去个</rb><rt>kyʽi-go</rt><rb>路</rb><rt>lu</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>因为</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>从前</rb><rt>dzong-zin</rt><rb>弗曾</rb><rt>feh-zing</rt><rb>走</rb><rt>tseo</rt><rb>箇埭</rb><rt>keh-da</rt><rb>路</rb><rt>lu</rt><rb>过</rb><rt>ko</rt></ruby>。」<sup>5</sup> <u><ruby><rb>约书亚</rb><rt>Iah-shü-üô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>百姓</rb><rt>pah-sing</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>用</rb><rt>Yüong</rt><rb>自</rb><rt>zi</rt><rb>洁净</rb><rt>kyih-zing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>因为</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>明朝</rb><rt>ming-tsiao</rt><rb>耶和华</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>中央</rb><rt>cong-nyiang</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>奇事</rb><rt>gyi-z</rt></ruby>。」<sup>6</sup> <u><ruby><rb>约书亚</rb><rt>Iah-shü-üô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>祭司</rb><rt>tsi-s</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>Ng-lah</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>抬</rb><rt>dæ</rt><rb>约箱</rb><rt>iah-siang</rt><rb>先如</rb><rt>sin-jü</rt><rb>百姓</rb><rt>pah-sing</rt><rb>过去</rb><rt>ko-kyʽi</rt></ruby>。」<ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>抬</rb><rt>dæ</rt><rb>约箱</rb><rt>iah-siang</rt><rb>走</rb><rt>tseo</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>百姓</rb><rt>pah-sing</rt><rb>面前</rb><rt>min-zin</rt></ruby>。<sup>7</sup> <ruby><rb>耶和华</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>约书亚</rb><rt>Iah-shü-üô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>从</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>今朝</rb><rt>kying-tsiao</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>众</rb><rt>cong</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>面前</rb><rt>min-zin</rt><rb>尊</rb><rt>tseng</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>大</rb><rt>do</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>晓得</rb><rt>hyiao-teh</rt><rb>正像</rb><rt>tsing-ziang</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>从前</rb><rt>dzong-zin</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>来间</rb><rt>læ-kæn</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>现在</rb><rt>yin-dzæ</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>来东</rb><rt>læ-tong</rt></ruby>。<sup>8</sup> <ruby><rb>尔</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>吩咐</rb><rt>feng-fu</rt><rb>抬</rb><rt>dæ</rt><rb>约箱个</rb><rt>iah-siang-go</rt><rb>祭司</rb><rt>tsi-s</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：『<ruby><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>Ng-lah</rt><rb>到了</rb><rt>tao-leh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>约但</rb><rt>Iah-dæn</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>水</rb><rt>shü</rt><rb>边沿</rb><rt>pin-yin</rt><rb>当该</rb><rt>tông-kæ</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>约但</rb><rt>Iah-dæn</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>河里</rb><rt>ʽo-li</rt><rb>立落</rb><rt>lih-loh</rt></ruby>。』」

<sup>9</sup> <u><ruby><rb>约书亚</rb><rt>Iah-shü-üô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>子孙</rb><rt>ts-seng</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>Ng-lah</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>走拢来</rb><rt>tseo-long-læ</rt><rb>听</rb><rt>tʽing</rt><rb>耶和华</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>神明个</rb><rt>Jing-ming-go</rt><rb>说话</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt></ruby>。」<sup>10</sup> <u><ruby><rb>约书亚</rb><rt>Iah-shü-üô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>箇回</rb><rt>Keh-we</rt><rb>活</rb><rt>weh</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>中央</rb><rt>cong-nyiang</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>必定</rb><rt>pih-ding</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>面前</rb><rt>min-zin</rt><rb>赶出</rb><rt>ken-cʽih</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>迦南</rb><rt>Kyüô-nen</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>、<u><ruby><rb>赫</rb><rt>Heh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>、<u><ruby><rb>希未</rb><rt>Hyi-vi</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>、<u><ruby><rb>比利洗</rb><rt>Pi-li-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>、<u><ruby><rb>革迦撒</rb><rt>Keh-kyüô-seh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>、<u><ruby><rb>亚摩利</rb><rt>Üô-mo-li</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>、<u><ruby><rb>耶布士</rb><rt>Yiæ-pu-z</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>从此</rb><rt>dzong-tsʽ</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>晓得</rb><rt>hyiao-teh</rt></ruby>。<sup>11</sup> <ruby><rb>全地</rb><rt>Djün-di</rt><rb>主个</rb><rt>Cü-go</rt><rb>约箱</rb><rt>iah-siang</rt><rb>走</rb><rt>tseo</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>前头</rb><rt>zin-deo</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>约但</rb><rt>Iah-dæn</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>河里</rb><rt>ʽo-li</rt></ruby>。<sup>12</sup> <ruby><rb>所以</rb><rt>Sô-yi</rt><rb>现在</rb><rt>yin-dzæ</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>该</rb><rt>kæ</rt><rb>从</rb><rt>dzong</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>支派</rb><rt>ts-pʽa</rt><rb>拣选</rb><rt>kæn-shün</rt><rb>十二</rb><rt>jih-nyi</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>每</rb><rt>me</rt><rb>支派</rb><rt>ts-pʽa</rt><rb>一个</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>。<sup>13</sup> <ruby><rb>等到</rb><rt>Teng-tao</rt><rb>抬</rb><rt>dæ</rt><rb>全地个</rb><rt>djün-di-go</rt><rb>主</rb><rt>Cü</rt><rb>耶和华个</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô-go</rt><rb>约箱</rb><rt>iah-siang</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>祭司</rb><rt>tsi-s</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>脚底</rb><rt>kyiah-ti</rt><rb>一</rb><rt>ih</rt><rb>立落</rb><rt>lih-loh</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>约但</rb><rt>Iah-dæn</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>河个</rb><rt>ʽo-go</rt><rb>水里</rb><rt>shü-li</rt></ruby>，<u><ruby><rb>约但</rb><rt>Iah-dæn</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>河</rb><rt>ʽo</rt><rb>水</rb><rt>shü</rt><rb>必定</rb><rt>pih-ding</rt><rb>截断</rb><rt>zih-dön</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>从</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>上</rb><rt>zông</rt><rb>流落个</rb><rt>liu-loh-go</rt><rb>水</rb><rt>shü</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>立起来</rb><rt>lih-kyʽi-læ</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>一堆</rb><rt>ih-te</rt></ruby>。」<sup>14</sup> <ruby><rb>百姓</rb><rt>Pah-sing</rt><rb>从</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>帐篷</rb><rt>tsiang-bong</rt><rb>动身</rb><rt>dong-sing</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>过</rb><rt>ko</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>约但</rb><rt>Iah-dæn</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>河</rb><rt>ʽo</rt><rb>时候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>抬</rb><rt>dæ</rt><rb>约箱个</rb><rt>iah-siang-go</rt><rb>祭司</rb><rt>tsi-s</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>百姓</rb><rt>pah-sing</rt><rb>面前</rb><rt>min-zin</rt><rb>走</rb><rt>tseo</rt></ruby>；<sup>15</sup> <ruby><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>抬</rb><rt>dæ</rt><rb>约箱</rb><rt>iah-siang</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>走到</rb><rt>tseo-tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>约但</rb><rt>Iah-dæn</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>河</rb><rt>ʽo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>抬</rb><rt>dæ</rt><rb>约箱</rb><rt>iah-siang</rt><rb>祭司</rb><rt>tsi-s</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>脚</rb><rt>kyiah</rt><rb>一</rb><rt>ih</rt><rb>蹽落</rb><rt>liao-loh</rt><rb>水里</rb><rt>shü-li</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>，（<ruby><rb>因为</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>收割</rb><rt>siu-keh</rt><rb>时候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>约但</rb><rt>Iah-dæn</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>河水</rb><rt>ʽo-shü</rt><rb>溢上</rb><rt>kah-zông</rt><rb>岸顶</rb><rt>ngen-teng</rt></ruby>，）<sup>16</sup> <ruby><rb>从</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>上</rb><rt>zông</rt><rb>流落来个</rb><rt>liu-loh-læ-go</rt><rb>水</rb><rt>shü</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>立起来</rb><rt>lih-kyʽi-læ</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>一堆</rb><rt>ih-te</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>离</rb><rt>li</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>撒利但</rb><rt>Sah-li-dæn</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>旁边个</rb><rt>bông-pin-go</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>亚当</rb><rt>Üô-tông</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>城</rb><rt>dzing</rt><rb>差</rb><rt>tsʽô</rt><rb>远</rb><rt>yün</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>还有</rb><rt>wa-yiu</rt><rb>向</rb><rt>hyiang</rt><rb>平垟个</rb><rt>bing-yiang-go</rt><rb>海</rb><rt>hæ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>盐</u>海</rb><rt>Yin-hæ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>流落去个</rb><rt>liu-loh-kyʽi-go</rt><rb>水</rb><rt>shü</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>截断了</rb><rt>zih-dön-de</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>百姓</rb><rt>pah-sing</rt><rb>扣扣</rb><rt>kʽeo-kʽeo</rt><rb>对着</rb><rt>te-djoh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶利哥</rb><rt>Yiæ-li-ko</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>走过去</rb><rt>tseo-ko-kyʽi</rt></ruby>。<sup>17</sup> <ruby><rb>抬</rb><rt>Dæ</rt><rb>耶和华</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>约箱</rb><rt>iah-siang</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>祭司</rb><rt>tsi-s</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>约但</rb><rt>Iah-dæn</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>河</rb><rt>ʽo</rt><rb>中央</rb><rt>cong-nyiang</rt><rb>燥地</rb><rt>sao-di</rt><rb>上</rb><rt>zông</rt><rb>立定</rb><rt>lih-ding</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等到</rb><rt>teng-tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>走</rb><rt>tseo</rt><rb>燥地</rb><rt>sao-di</rt><rb>经过</rb><rt>kying-ko</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>约但</rb><rt>Iah-dæn</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>河</rb><rt>ʽo</rt></ruby>。

### Is. IV.

<ruby><rb>众</rb><rt>CONG</rt><rb>百姓</rb><rt>pah-sing</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>已经</rb><rt>yi-kying</rt><rb>过</rb><rt>ko</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>约但</rb><rt>Iah-dæn</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>河</rb><rt>ʽo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>耶和华</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>吩咐</rb><rt>feng-fu</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>约书亚</rb><rt>Iah-shü-üô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：<sup>2</sup> 「<ruby><rb>从</rb><rt>Dzong</rt><rb>百姓</rb><rt>pah-sing</rt><rb>中央</rb><rt>cong-nyiang</rt><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>拣选</rb><rt>kæn-shün</rt><rb>十二</rb><rt>jih-nyi</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>每</rb><rt>me</rt><rb>支派</rb><rt>ts-pʽa</rt><rb>一个</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>，<sup>3</sup> <ruby><rb>吩咐</rb><rt>feng-fu</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：『<ruby><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>Ng-lah</rt><rb>从</rb><rt>dzong</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>约但</rb><rt>Iah-dæn</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>河</rb><rt>ʽo</rt><rb>中央</rb><rt>cong-nyiang</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>祭司</rb><rt>tsi-s</rt><rb>脚</rb><rt>kyiah</rt><rb>立定</rb><rt>lih-ding</rt><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>驮</rb><rt>do</rt><rb>十二</rb><rt>jih-nyi</rt><rb>块</rb><rt>kwʽe</rt><rb>石头</rb><rt>zah-deo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>揹过去</rb><rt>pe-ko-kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>放</rb><rt>fông</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>今朝</rb><rt>kying-tsiao</rt><rb>夜到</rb><rt>yia-tao</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>庉宿个</rb><rt>deng-soh-go</rt><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt></ruby>。』」<sup>4</sup> <u><ruby><rb>约书亚</rb><rt>Iah-shü-üô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>把</rb><rt>pô</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>备办</rb><rt>be-bæn</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>子孙</rb><rt>ts-seng</rt><rb>十二</rb><rt>jih-nyi</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>每</rb><rt>me</rt><rb>支派</rb><rt>ts-pʽa</rt><rb>一个</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>召</rb><rt>dziao</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>：<sup>5</sup> <u><ruby><rb>约书亚</rb><rt>Iah-shü-üô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>Ng-lah</rt><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>走过</rb><rt>tseo-ko</rt><rb>耶和华</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>约箱</rb><rt>iah-siang</rt><rb>前头</rb><rt>zin-deo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>约但</rb><rt>Iah-dæn</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>河</rb><rt>ʽo</rt><rb>中央</rb><rt>cong-nyiang</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>河</rb><rt>ʽo</rt><rb>中央</rb><rt>cong-nyiang</rt><rb>各人</rb><rt>koh-nying</rt><rb>驮</rb><rt>do</rt><rb>一块</rb><rt>ih-kwʽe</rt><rb>石头</rb><rt>zah-deo</rt><rb>肩胛头</rb><rt>kyin-kah-deo</rt><rb>揹勒去</rb><rt>pe-leh-kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>照</rb><rt>tsiao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>支派个</rb><rt>ts-pʽa-go</rt><rb>数目</rb><rt>su-moh</rt></ruby>：<sup>6</sup> <ruby><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>可以</rb><rt>kʽo-yi</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>中央</rb><rt>cong-nyiang</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>记号</rb><rt>kyi-ʽao</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>将来</rb><rt>tsiang-læ</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>子孙</rb><rt>ts-seng</rt><rb>问</rb><rt>meng</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：『<ruby><rb>箇星</rb><rt>Keh-sing</rt><rb>石头</rb><rt>zah-deo</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>啥个</rb><rt>soh-go</rt><rb>意思</rb><rt>i-s</rt></ruby>？』<sup>7</sup> <ruby><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>讲</rb><rt>kông</rt></ruby>：『<ruby><rb>前头</rb><rt>Zin-deo</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>约但</rb><rt>Iah-dæn</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>河</rb><rt>ʽo</rt><rb>水</rb><rt>shü</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>耶和华</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>约箱</rb><rt>iah-siang</rt><rb>面前</rb><rt>min-zin</rt><rb>截断</rb><rt>zih-dön</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>约箱</rb><rt>iah-siang</rt><rb>过</rb><rt>ko</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>约但</rb><rt>Iah-dæn</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>河</rb><rt>ʽo</rt><rb>时候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt></ruby>，<u><ruby><rb>约但</rb><rt>Iah-dæn</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>河</rb><rt>ʽo</rt><rb>水</rb><rt>shü</rt><rb>截断</rb><rt>zih-dön</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>石头</rb><rt>zah-deo</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>子孙</rb><rt>ts-seng</rt><rb>永远</rb><rt>üong-yün</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>记号</rb><rt>kyi-ʽao</rt></ruby>。』」<sup>8</sup> <u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>子孙</rb><rt>ts-seng</rt><rb>照</rb><rt>tsiao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>约书亚</rb><rt>Iah-shü-üô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>吩咐</rb><rt>feng-fu</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>从</rb><rt>dzong</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>约但</rb><rt>Iah-dæn</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>河</rb><rt>ʽo</rt><rb>中央</rb><rt>cong-nyiang</rt><rb>驮</rb><rt>do</rt><rb>十二</rb><rt>jih-nyi</rt><rb>块</rb><rt>kwʽe</rt><rb>石头</rb><rt>zah-deo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>照</rb><rt>tsiao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>支派个</rb><rt>ts-pʽa-go</rt><rb>数目</rb><rt>su-moh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>依</rb><rt>i</rt><rb>耶和华</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>约书亚</rb><rt>Iah-shü-üô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>揹</rb><rt>pe</rt><rb>石头</rb><rt>zah-deo</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>庉宿个</rb><rt>deng-soh-go</rt><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>放</rb><rt>fông</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>箇头</rb><rt>keh-deo</rt></ruby>。<sup>9</sup> <u><ruby><rb>约书亚</rb><rt>Iah-shü-üô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>立</rb><rt>lih</rt><rb>十二</rb><rt>jih-nyi</rt><rb>块</rb><rt>kwʽe</rt><rb>石头</rb><rt>zah-deo</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>约但</rb><rt>Iah-dæn</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>河里</rb><rt>ʽo-li</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>抬</rb><rt>dæ</rt><rb>约箱个</rb><rt>iah-siang-go</rt><rb>祭司</rb><rt>tsi-s</rt><rb>脚</rb><rt>kyiah</rt><rb>立个</rb><rt>lih-go</rt><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>如今</rb><rt>jü-kying</rt><rb>箇头</rb><rt>keh-deo</rt><rb>还</rb><rt>wa</rt><rb>来间</rb><rt>læ-kæn</rt></ruby>。<sup>10</sup> <ruby><rb>因为</rb><rt>Ing-we</rt><rb>抬</rb><rt>dæ</rt><rb>约箱个</rb><rt>iah-siang-go</rt><rb>祭司</rb><rt>tsi-s</rt><rb>立</rb><rt>lih</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>约但</rb><rt>Iah-dæn</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>河里</rb><rt>ʽo-li</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等到</rb><rt>teng-tao</rt><rb>耶和华</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>吩咐</rb><rt>feng-fu</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>约书亚</rb><rt>Iah-shü-üô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>百姓</rb><rt>pah-sing</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>说话</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>完毕</rb><rt>wun-pih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>照</rb><rt>tsiao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>凡百</rb><rt>væn-pah</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>吩咐</rb><rt>feng-fu</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>约书亚</u>个</rb><rt>Iah-shü-üô-go</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>百姓</rb><rt>pah-sing</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>快快</rb><rt>kwʽa-kwʽa</rt><rb>走过去</rb><rt>tseo-ko-kyʽi</rt></ruby>。<sup>11</sup> <sup>15</sup> <ruby><rb>耶和华</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>吩咐</rb><rt>feng-fu</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>约书亚</rb><rt>Iah-shü-üô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：<sup>16</sup> ### Is. V.

<u><ruby><rb>约但</rb><rt>IAH-DÆN</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>河</rb><rt>ʽo</rt><rb>西边</rb><rt>si-pin</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>亚摩利</rb><rt>Üô-mo-li</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>拢总</rb><rt>long-tsong</rt><rb>王</rb><rt>wông</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>海边沿个</rb><rt>hæ-pin-yin-go</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>迦南</rb><rt>Kyüô-nen</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>拢总</rb><rt>long-tsong</rt><rb>王</rb><rt>wông</rt><rb>听闻</rb><rt>tʽing-meng</rt><rb>耶和华</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>子孙</rb><rt>ts-seng</rt><rb>面前</rb><rt>min-zin</rt><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>约但</rb><rt>Iah-dæn</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>河</rb><rt>ʽo</rt><rb>水</rb><rt>shü</rt><rb>干燥</rb><rt>ken-sao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等到</rb><rt>teng-tao</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>走过</rb><rt>tseo-ko</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>为了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>子孙</rb><rt>ts-seng</rt><rb>心</rb><rt>sing</rt><rb>衰了</rb><rt>sæ-de</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>胆量</rb><rt>tæn-liang</rt><rb>呒得</rb><rt>m-neh</rt><rb>了</rb><rt>de</rt></ruby>。

<sup>2</sup> <ruby><rb>介</rb><rt>Ka</rt><rb>时候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt><rb>耶和华</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>约书亚</rb><rt>Iah-shü-üô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>尔</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>火石</rb><rt>ho-zah</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>刀</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>再</rb><rt>tsæ</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>第二</rb><rt>di-nyi</rt><rb>遭</rb><rt>tsao</rt><rb>行</rb><rt>ʽang</rt><rb>周割礼</rb><rt>tsiu-keh-li</rt></ruby>。」<sup>3</sup> <u><ruby><rb>约书亚</rb><rt>Iah-shü-üô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>火石</rb><rt>ho-zah</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>刀</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>割</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>皮个</rb><rt>bi-go</rt><rb>山里</rb><rt>sæn-li</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>子孙</rb><rt>ts-seng</rt><rb>行</rb><rt>ʽang</rt><rb>周割礼</rb><rt>tsiu-keh-li</rt></ruby>。<sup>4</sup> <u><ruby><rb>约书亚</rb><rt>Iah-shü-üô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>行</rb><rt>ʽang</rt><rb>周割礼个</rb><rt>tsiu-keh-li-go</rt><rb>缘故</rb><rt>yün-kwu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>因为</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>出</rb><rt>cʽih</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>埃及</rb><rt>Yiæ-gyih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>众</rb><rt>cong</rt><rb>百姓</rb><rt>pah-sing</rt><rb>一切</rb><rt>ih-tsʽih</rt><rb>能够</rb><rt>neng-keo</rt><rb>出</rb><rt>cʽih</rt><rb>兵</rb><rt>ping</rt><rb>打仗个</rb><rt>tang-tsiang-go</rt><rb>男丁</rb><rt>nen-ting</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>出</rb><rt>cʽih</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>埃及</rb><rt>Yiæ-gyih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>之后</rb><rt>ts-ʽeo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>旷野</rb><rt>kwʽông-iæ</rt><rb>路上</rb><rt>lu-zông</rt><rb>死完</rb><rt>si-wun</rt></ruby>：<sup>5</sup> <ruby><rb>因为</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>凡百</rb><rt>væn-pah</rt><rb>出来个</rb><rt>cʽih-læ-go</rt><rb>百姓</rb><rt>pah-sing</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>受过</rb><rt>ziu-ko</rt><rb>周割礼</rb><rt>tsiu-keh-li</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>但是</rb><rt>dæn-z</rt><rb>凡百</rb><rt>væn-pah</rt><rb>出</rb><rt>cʽih</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>埃及</rb><rt>Yiæ-gyih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>之后</rb><rt>ts-ʽeo</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>旷野</rb><rt>kwʽông-iæ</rt><rb>路上</rb><rt>lu-zông</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>生个</rb><rt>sang-go</rt><rb>百姓</rb><rt>pah-sing</rt><rb>呒得</rb><rt>m-neh</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>行</rb><rt>ʽang</rt><rb>周割礼</rb><rt>tsiu-keh-li</rt><rb>过</rb><rt>ko</rt></ruby>。<sup>6</sup> <ruby><rb>因为</rb><rt>Ing-we</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>子孙</rb><rt>ts-seng</rt><rb>走</rb><rt>tseo</rt><rb>旷野</rb><rt>kwʽông-iæ</rt><rb>四十</rb><rt>s-jih</rt><rb>年</rb><rt>nyin</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等到</rb><rt>teng-tao</rt><rb>众</rb><rt>cong</rt><rb>百姓</rb><rt>pah-sing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>出<u>埃及</u>个</rb><rt>cʽih-Yiæ-gyih-go</rt><rb>出兵</rb><rt>cʽih-ping</rt><rb>打仗</rb><rt>tang-tsiang</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>灭掉</rb><rt>mih-diao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>为了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>听从</rb><rt>tʽing-dzong</rt><rb>耶和华个</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô-go</rt><rb>说话</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>耶和华</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>向</rb><rt>hyiang</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>罚咒</rb><rt>vah-tsiu</rt><rb>必定</rb><rt>pih-ding</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>看见</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt><rb>耶和华</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>向</rb><rt>hyiang</rt><rb>列祖</rb><rt>lih-tsu</rt><rb>罚咒</rb><rt>vah-tsiu</rt><rb>应许</rb><rt>ing-hyü</rt><rb>赐拨</rb><rt>s-peh</rt><rb>阿拉个</rb><rt>ah-lah-go</rt><rb>地</rb><rt>di</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>流</rb><rt>liu</rt><rb>奶</rb><rt>na</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>蜜糖个</rb><rt>mih-dông-go</rt><rb>地</rb><rt>di</rt></ruby>。<sup>7</sup> <ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt><rb>子孙</rb><rt>ts-seng</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>主</rb><rt>Cü</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>接代个</rb><rt>tsih-dæ-go</rt></ruby>，<u><ruby><rb>约书亚</rb><rt>Iah-shü-üô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>行</rb><rt>ʽang</rt><rb>周割礼</rb><rt>tsiu-keh-li</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>弗曾</rb><rt>feh-zing</rt><rb>受过</rb><rt>ziu-ko</rt><rb>周割礼</rb><rt>tsiu-keh-li</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>为了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt><rb>路上</rb><rt>lu-zông</rt><rb>呒得</rb><rt>m-neh</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>行</rb><rt>ʽang</rt><rb>周割礼</rb><rt>tsiu-keh-li</rt><rb>过</rb><rt>ko</rt></ruby>。<sup>8</sup> <ruby><rb>既经</rb><rt>Kyi-kying</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>众</rb><rt>cong</rt><rb>百姓</rb><rt>pah-sing</rt><rb>行</rb><rt>ʽang</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>周割礼</rb><rt>tsiu-keh-li</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>营寨里</rb><rt>ying-za-li</rt><rb>各人</rb><rt>koh-nying</rt><rb>庉</rb><rt>deng</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>自</rb><rt>zi</rt><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt><rb>等到</rb><rt>teng-tao</rt><rb>痊愈</rb><rt>djün-yü</rt></ruby>。<sup>9</sup> <ruby><rb>耶和华</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>约书亚</rb><rt>Iah-shü-üô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>今朝</rb><rt>kying-tsiao</rt><rb>从</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>尔拉个</rb><rt>ng-lah-go</rt><rb>身上</rb><rt>sing-zông</rt><rb>把</rb><rt>pô</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>埃及</u>个</rb><rt>Yiæ-gyih-go</rt><rb>谤毁</rb><rt>pông-hwe</rt><rb>转过</rb><rt>cün-ko</rt><rb>离开</rb><rt>li-kʽæ</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>所以</rb><rt>Sô-yi</rt><rb>一直</rb><rt>ih-dzih</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>如今</rb><rt>jü-kying</rt><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt><rb>叫勒</rb><rt>kyiao-leh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>吉甲</rb><rt>Kyih-kyiah</rt></ruby></u><sup>\[1\]</sup>。」

<sup>\[2\] *Ziu-z* cün-ko ka-shih. 就是「转过」解说。（原注）</sup>

<sup>10</sup> <u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>子孙</rb><rt>ts-seng</rt><rb>搭营</rb><rt>tah-ying</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>吉甲</rb><rt>Kyih-kyiah</rt></ruby></u>；<ruby><rb>本月</rb><rt>peng-yüih</rt><rb>十四</rb><rt>jih-s</rt><rb>箇日</rb><rt>keh-nyih</rt><rb>夜快</rb><rt>yia-kwʽa</rt><rb>收</rb><rt>siu</rt><rb>逾越节</rb><rt>Yü-yüih-tsih</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>耶利哥</u>个</rb><rt>Yiæ-li-ko-go</rt><rb>平垟</rb><rt>bing-yiang</rt><rb>里</rb><rt>li</rt></ruby>。<sup>11</sup> <ruby><rb>逾越节</rb><rt>Yü-yüih-tsih</rt><rb>第二</rb><rt>di-nyi</rt><rb>日</rb><rt>nyih</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>吃</rb><rt>kyʽüoh</rt><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>地方个</rb><rt>di-fông-go</rt><rb>五谷</rb><rt>ng-koh</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>当日</rb><rt>tông-nyih</rt><rb>吃</rb><rt>kyʽüoh</rt><rb>无酵</rb><rt>vu-kao</rt><rb>饼</rb><rt>ping</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>烘过了</rb><rt>hong-ko-liao</rt><rb>谷</rb><rt>koh</rt></ruby>。<sup>12</sup> <ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt><rb>吃过</rb><rt>kyʽüoh-ko</rt><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>地方个</rb><rt>di-fông-go</rt><rb>五谷</rb><rt>ng-koh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>第二</rb><rt>di-nyi</rt><rb>日</rb><rt>nyih</rt><rb>吗嗱</rb><rt>mô-nô</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>止了</rb><rt>ts-de</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>以后</rb><rt>yi-ʽeo</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>子孙</rb><rt>ts-seng</rt><rb>呒得</rb><rt>m-neh</rt><rb>再</rb><rt>tsæ</rt><rb>看见</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt><rb>吗嗱</rb><rt>mô-nô</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>一年</rb><rt>ih-nyin</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>吃</rb><rt>kyʽüoh</rt><rb>迦<u>南</rb><rt>Kyüô-nen</rt><rb>地</u>个</rb><rt>di-go</rt><rb>出产</rb><rt>cʽih-tsʽæn</rt></ruby>。

<sup>13</sup> <u><ruby><rb>约书亚</rb><rt>Iah-shü-üô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>近</rb><rt>gying</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶利哥</rb><rt>Yiæ-li-ko</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>时候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt><rb>抬起</rb><rt>dæ-kyʽi</rt><rb>眼睛</rb><rt>ngæn-tsing</rt><rb>看看</rb><rt>kʽen-kʽen</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>一个</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>手里</rb><rt>siu-li</rt><rb>驮</rb><rt>do</rt><rb>拔出了</rb><rt>bah-cʽih-liao</rt><rb>刀</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>立</rb><rt>lih</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>对面</rb><rt>te-min</rt></ruby>；<u><ruby><rb>约书亚</rb><rt>Iah-shü-üô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>走拢</rb><rt>tseo-long</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>问</rb><rt>meng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>尔</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>向</rb><rt>hyiang</rt><rb>阿拉个</rb><rt>ah-lah-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>还</rb><rt>wa</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>向</rb><rt>hyiang</rt><rb>仇敌个</rb><rt>dziu-dih-go</rt></ruby>？」<sup>14</sup> <ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>弗是介</rb><rt>Feh-z-ka</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>耶和华</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>军器个</rb><rt>kyüing-kyʽi-go</rt><rb>大</rb><rt>da</rt><rb>将军</rb><rt>tsiang-kyüing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>现在</rb><rt>yin-dzæ</rt><rb>来了</rb><rt>læ-de</rt></ruby>。」<u><ruby><rb>约书亚</rb><rt>Iah-shü-üô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>面孔</rb><rt>min-kʽong</rt><rb>扑倒</rb><rt>pʽoh-tao</rt><rb>地里</rb><rt>di-li</rt><rb>拜拜</rb><rt>pa-pa</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>主</rb><rt>Cü</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>咋</rb><rt>dza</rt><rb>吩咐</rb><rt>feng-fu</rt><rb>奴仆</rb><rt>nu-boh</rt></ruby>？」<sup>15</sup> <ruby><rb>耶和华</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>军营个</rb><rt>kyüing-ying-go</rt><rb>大</rb><rt>da</rt><rb>将军</rb><rt>tsiang-kyüing</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>约书亚</rb><rt>Iah-shü-üô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>尔</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>脱掉</rb><rt>tʽeh-diao</rt><rb>鞋</rb><rt>ʽa</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>因为</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>立的个</rb><rt>lih-tih-go</rt><rb>地</rb><rt>di</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>圣善</rb><rt>sing-jün</rt></ruby>。」<u><ruby><rb>约书亚</rb><rt>Iah-shü-üô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt></ruby>。

---

### Is. VI.

<ruby><rb>为了</rb><rt>WE-LEH</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>子孙</rb><rt>ts-seng</rt><rb>缘故</rb><rt>yün-kwu</rt></ruby>，<u><ruby><rb>耶利哥</rb><rt>Yiæ-li-ko</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>城门</rb><rt>dzing-meng</rt><rb>紧闭</rb><rt>kying-pi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>呒人</rb><rt>m-nying</rt><rb>走出</rb><rt>tseo-cʽih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>呒人</rb><rt>m-nying</rt><rb>走进</rb><rt>tseo-tsing</rt></ruby>。<sup>2</sup> <ruby><rb>耶和华</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>约书亚</rb><rt>Iah-shü-üô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>尔</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>看</rb><rt>kʽen</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>已经</rb><rt>yi-kying</rt><rb>把</rb><rt>pô</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶利哥</rb><rt>Yiæ-li-ko</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>王</rb><rt>wông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>好佬</rb><rt>hao-lao</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>交代</rb><rt>kao-dæ</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>手里</rb><rt>siu-li</rt></ruby>。<sup>3</sup> <ruby><rb>尔</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>围绕</rb><rt>we-ziao</rt><rb>城子</rb><rt>dzing-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>一切</rb><rt>ih-tsʽih</rt><rb>兵将</rb><rt>ping-tsiang</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>绕</rb><rt>nyiao</rt><rb>城子</rb><rt>dzing-ts</rt><rb>走</rb><rt>tseo</rt><rb>一转</rb><rt>ih-cün</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>该</rb><rt>kæ</rt><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>六日</rb><rt>loh-nyih</rt></ruby>。<sup>4</sup> <ruby><rb>但是</rb><rt>Dæn-z</rt><rb>拢总</rb><rt>long-tsong</rt><rb>金银</rb><rt>kying-nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>铜铁</rb><rt>dong-tʽih</rt><rb>做个</rb><rt>tso-go</rt><rb>器皿</rb><rt>kyʽi-ming</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>尊起</rb><rt>tseng-kyʽi</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>耶和华</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>驮进</rb><rt>do-tsing</rt><rb>耶和华个</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô-go</rt><rb>库里</rb><rt>kwʽu-li</rt></ruby>。」<sup>20</sup> <ruby><rb>祭司</rb><rt>Tsi-s</rt><rb>吹</rb><rt>cʽü</rt><rb>号筒</rb><rt>ʽao-dong</rt><rb>时候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt><rb>百姓</rb><rt>pah-sing</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>讴起来</rb><rt>eo-kyʽi-læ</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>百姓</rb><rt>pah-sing</rt><rb>一</rb><rt>ih</rt><rb>听闻</rb><rt>tʽing-meng</rt><rb>号筒</rb><rt>ʽao-dong</rt><rb>声响</rb><rt>sing-hyiang</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>大大</rb><rt>do-do</rt><rb>声响</rb><rt>sing-hyiang</rt><rb>讴起来</rb><rt>eo-kyʽi-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>城墙</rb><rt>dzing-ziang</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>跌倒</rb><rt>tih-tao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>百姓</rb><rt>pah-sing</rt><rb>走进</rb><rt>tseo-tsing</rt><rb>城里</rb><rt>dzing-li</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>各人</rb><rt>koh-nying</rt><rb>一直</rb><rt>ih-dzih</rt><rb>向</rb><rt>hyiang</rt><rb>前</rb><rt>zin</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>走</rb><rt>tseo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>得着</rb><rt>teh-djoh</rt><rb>城子</rb><rt>dzing-ts</rt></ruby>。<sup>21</sup> <ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt><rb>杀完</rb><rt>sah-wun</rt><rb>凡百</rb><rt>væn-pah</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>城里个</rb><rt>dzing-li-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>男</rb><rt>nen</rt><rb>女</rb><rt>nyü</rt><rb>老少</rb><rt>lao-siao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>牛</rb><rt>ngeo</rt><rb>羊</rb><rt>yiang</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>驴子</rb><rt>li-ts</rt></ruby>。<sup>22</sup> <u><ruby><rb>约书亚</rb><rt>Iah-shü-üô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>吩咐</rb><rt>feng-fu</rt><rb>探看</rb><rt>tʽen-kʽen</rt><rb>地</rb><rt>di</rt><rb>两个</rb><rt>liang-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>用</rb><rt>Yüong</rt><rb>走进</rb><rt>tseo-tsing</rt><rb>妓女</rb><rt>gyi-nyü</rt><rb>屋里</rb><rt>oh-li</rt><rb>把</rb><rt>pô</rt><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>女人</rb><rt>nyü-nying</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>一切</rb><rt>ih-tsʽih</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>有个</rb><rt>yiu-go</rt><rb>带出</rb><rt>ta-cʽih</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>照</rb><rt>tsiao</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>向</rb><rt>hyiang</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>罚过个</rb><rt>vah-ko-go</rt><rb>咒</rb><rt>tsiu</rt></ruby>。」<sup>23</sup> <ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>Keh</rt><rb>两</rb><rt>liang</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>探子个</rb><rt>tʽen-ts-go</rt><rb>少年</rb><rt>siao-nyin</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>走进</rb><rt>tseo-tsing</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>带出</rb><rt>ta-cʽih</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>喇合</rb><rt>Lah-ʽeh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>爹娘</rb><rt>tia-nyiang</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>兄弟</rb><rt>hyüong-di</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>一切</rb><rt>ih-tsʽih</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>有个</rb><rt>yiu-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>并且</rb><rt>ping-tsʽia</rt><rb>带出</rb><rt>ta-cʽih</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>亲眷</rb><rt>tsʽing-kyün</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>安顿</rb><rt>en-teng</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>营寨</rb><rt>ying-za</rt><rb>外边</rb><rt>nga-pin</rt></ruby>。<sup>24</sup> <ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>火</rb><rt>ho</rt><rb>烧</rb><rt>siao</rt><rb>城子</rb><rt>dzing-ts</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>凡百</rb><rt>væn-pah</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>里头个</rb><rt>li-deo-go</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>只有</rb><rt>Tsih-yiu</rt><rb>金银</rb><rt>kying-nying</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>铜铁</rb><rt>dong-tʽih</rt><rb>做个</rb><rt>tso-go</rt><rb>器皿</rb><rt>kyʽi-ming</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>驮进</rb><rt>do-tsing</rt><rb>耶和华</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>殿里个</rb><rt>din-li-go</rt><rb>库里</rb><rt>kwʽu-li</rt></ruby>。<sup>25</sup> <u><ruby><rb>约书亚</rb><rt>Iah-shü-üô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>救活</rb><rt>kyiu-weh</rt><rb>妓女</rb><rt>gyi-nyü</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>喇合</rb><rt>Lah-ʽeh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>阿爹个</rb><rt>ah-tia-go</rt><rb>一家</rb><rt>ih-kô</rt><rb>连</rb><rt>lin</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>凡百</rb><rt>væn-pah</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>有个</rb><rt>yiu-go</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>如今</rb><rt>jü-kying</rt><rb>住</rb><rt>djü</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>中央</rb><rt>cong-nyiang</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>因为</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>藏囥</rb><rt>dzông-kʽông</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>约书亚</rb><rt>Iah-shü-üô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>打发</rb><rt>tang-fah</rt><rb>探看</rb><rt>tʽen-kʽen</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>耶利哥</u>个</rb><rt>Yiæ-li-ko-go</rt><rb>差使</rb><rt>tsʽa-s</rt></ruby>。<sup>26</sup> <u><ruby><rb>约书亚</rb><rt>Iah-shü-üô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>向</rb><rt>hyiang</rt><rb>百姓</rb><rt>pah-sing</rt><rb>罚咒</rb><rt>vah-tsiu</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>Yiu</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>起来</rb><rt>kyʽi-læ</rt><rb>再</rb><rt>tsæ</rt><rb>造</rb><rt>zao</rt><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶利哥</rb><rt>Yiæ-li-ko</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>城</rb><rt>dzing</rt><rb>必定</rb><rt>pih-ding</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>耶和华</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>面前</rb><rt>min-zin</rt><rb>被</rb><rt>be</rt><rb>咒诅</rb><rt>tsiu-cü</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>放</rb><rt>fông</rt><rb>地基</rb><rt>di-kyi</rt><rb>时候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt><rb>必定</rb><rt>pih-ding</rt><rb>丧</rb><rt>sông</rt><rb>大</rb><rt>do</rt><rb>儿子</rb><rt>ng-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>上</rb><rt>zông</rt><rb>门</rb><rt>meng</rt><rb>时候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt><rb>必定</rb><rt>pih-ding</rt><rb>丧</rb><rt>sông</rt><rb>小</rb><rt>siao</rt><rb>儿子</rb><rt>ng-ts</rt></ruby>。」<sup>27</sup> <ruby><rb>是介</rb><rt>Z-ka</rt><rb>耶和华</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>约书亚</rb><rt>Iah-shü-üô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>来间</rb><rt>læ-kæn</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>其个</rb><rt>gyi-go</rt><rb>名声</rb><rt>ming-sing</rt><rb>遍地</rb><rt>pin-di</rt><rb>传开</rb><rt>djün-kʽæ</rt></ruby>。
