### Is. VII.

<u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>YI-SEH-LIH</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>子孙</rb><rt>ts-seng</rt><rb>为了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt><rb>该</rb><rt>kæ</rt><rb>毁灭个</rb><rt>hwe-mih-go</rt><rb>东西</rb><rt>tong-si</rt><rb>犯罪</rb><rt>væn-ze</rt></ruby>；<u><ruby><rb>犹大</rb><rt>Yiu-da</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>支派个</rb><rt>ts-pʽa-go</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>西拉</rb><rt>Si-lah</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>曾孙</rb><rt>tseng-seng</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb><u>撒底</u>个</rb><rt>Sah-ti-go</rt><rb>孙子</rb><rt>seng-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb><u>迦米</u>个</rb><rt>Kyüô-mi-go</rt><rb>儿子</rb><rt>ng-ts</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>亚干</rb><rt>Üô-ken</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>驮</rb><rt>do</rt><rb>该</rb><rt>kæ</rt><rb>毁灭个</rb><rt>hwe-mih-go</rt><rb>东西</rb><rt>tong-si</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>耶和华</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>𢛟惶</rb><rt>ô-wông</rt><rb>攻打</rb><rt>kong-tang</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>子孙</rb><rt>ts-seng</rt></ruby>。

<sup>2</sup> <u><ruby><rb>约书亚</rb><rt>Iah-shü-üô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>从</rb><rt>dzong</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶利哥</rb><rt>Yiæ-li-ko</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>差</rb><rt>tsʽa</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>百亚文</rb><rt>Pah-üô-veng</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>相近</rb><rt>siang-gying</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>伯特利</rb><rt>Pah-dah-li</rt></ruby></u><sup>\[1\]</sup><ruby><rb>东边个</rb><rt>tong-pin-go</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>埃</rb><rt>Yiæ</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>城</rb><rt>dzing</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>吩咐</rb><rt>feng-fu</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>Ng-lah</rt><rb>上去</rb><rt>zông-kyʽi</rt><rb>探看</rb><rt>tʽen-kʽen</rt><rb>箇个</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>地</rb><rt>di</rt></ruby>。」<ruby><rb>箇星</rb><rt>Keh-sing</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>上去</rb><rt>zông-kyʽi</rt><rb>探看</rb><rt>tʽen-kʽen</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>埃</rb><rt>Yiæ</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>城</rb><rt>dzing</rt></ruby>。<sup>3</sup> <ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt><rb>归到</rb><rt>kyü-tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>约书亚</rb><rt>Iah-shü-üô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>弗</rb><rt>Feh</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>众</rb><rt>cong</rt><rb>百姓</rb><rt>pah-sing</rt><rb>上去</rb><rt>zông-kyʽi</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>约来</rb><rt>iah-læ</rt><rb>二</rb><rt>nyi</rt><rb>三</rb><rt>sæn</rt><rb>千</rb><rt>tsʽin</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>上去</rb><rt>zông-kyʽi</rt><rb>攻打</rb><rt>kong-tang</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>埃</rb><rt>Yiæ</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>城</rb><rt>dzing</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>众</rb><rt>cong</rt><rb>百姓</rb><rt>pah-sing</rt><rb>劳劳碌碌</rb><rt>lao-lao-loh-loh</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>箇头</rb><rt>keh-deo</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>因为</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>缺个</rb><rt>kyʽüih-go</rt></ruby>。」<sup>4</sup> <ruby><rb>百姓</rb><rt>Pah-sing</rt><rb>大约</rb><rt>da-iah</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>三千</rb><rt>sæn-tsʽin</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>上去</rb><rt>zông-kyʽi</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>埃</rb><rt>Yiæ</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>城</rb><rt>dzing</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>面前</rb><rt>min-zin</rt><rb>逃走</rb><rt>dao-tseo</rt></ruby>。<sup>5</sup> <u><ruby><rb>埃</rb><rt>Yiæ</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>城</rb><rt>dzing</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>打煞</rb><rt>tang-sah</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>三十六个</rb><rt>sæn-jih-loh-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>从</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>城门</rb><rt>dzing-meng</rt><rb>前</rb><rt>zin</rt><rb>赶</rb><rt>ken</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>一直</rb><rt>ih-dzih</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>示巴琳</rb><rt>Z-pô-ling</rt></ruby></u>；<ruby><rb>落去</rb><rt>loh-kyʽi</rt><rb>时候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt><rb>打</rb><rt>tang</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>百姓</rb><rt>pah-sing</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>心</rb><rt>sing</rt><rb>衰了</rb><rt>sæ-de</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>像</rb><rt>ziang</rt><rb>水</rb><rt>shü</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>懦弱</rb><rt>no-ziah</rt><rb>了</rb><rt>de</rt></ruby>。<sup>6</sup> <u><ruby><rb>约书亚</rb><rt>Iah-shü-üô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>长老</rb><rt>tsiang-lao</rt><rb>扯开</rb><rt>cʽô-kʽæ</rt><rb>衣服</rb><rt>i-voh</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>耶和华</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>约箱</rb><rt>iah-siang</rt><rb>前</rb><rt>zin</rt><rb>面孔</rb><rt>min-kʽong</rt><rb>扑倒</rb><rt>pʽoh-tao</rt><rb>地里</rb><rt>di-li</rt><rb>直到</rb><rt>dzih-tao</rt><rb>夜快</rb><rt>yia-kwʽa</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>并且</rb><rt>ping-tsʽia</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>头上</rb><rt>deo-zông</rt><rb>扎灰</rb><rt>tsah-hwe</rt></ruby>。<sup>7</sup> <u><ruby><rb>约书亚</rb><rt>Iah-shü-üô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>嗐</rb><rt>ʽÆ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>耶和华</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>咋会</rb><rt>dza-we</rt><rb>领</rb><rt>ling</rt><rb>箇个</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>百姓</rb><rt>pah-sing</rt><rb>过</rb><rt>ko</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>约但</rb><rt>Iah-dæn</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>河</rb><rt>ʽo</rt><rb>把</rb><rt>pô</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>交代</rb><rt>kao-dæ</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>亚摩利</rb><rt>Üô-mo-li</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>手里</rb><rt>siu-li</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>灭亡</rb><rt>mih-vông</rt><rb>呢</rb><rt>ni</rt></ruby>？<ruby><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>Ah-lah</rt><rb>何弗</rb><rt>ʽo-feh</rt><rb>庉</rb><rt>deng</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>约但</rb><rt>Iah-dæn</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>河</rb><rt>ʽo</rt><rb>箇边</rb><rt>keh-pin</rt><rb>自</rb><rt>zi</rt><rb>知足</rb><rt>cü-tsoh</rt></ruby>。<sup>8</sup> <ruby><rb>主</rb><rt>Cü</rt><rb>嗄</rb><rt>ʽô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>该</rb><rt>kæ</rt><rb>咋</rb><rt>dza</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt><rb>法</rb><rt>fah</rt></ruby>？<ruby><rb>因为</rb><rt>Ing-we</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>仇敌</rb><rt>dziu-dih</rt><rb>面前</rb><rt>min-zin</rt><rb>打败</rb><rt>tang-ba</rt><rb>了</rb><rt>de</rt></ruby>。<sup>9</sup> <ruby><rb>因为</rb><rt>Ing-we</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>迦南</rb><rt>Kyüô-nen</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>一切</rb><rt>ih-tsʽih</rt><rb>住</rb><rt>djü</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>地个</rb><rt>di-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>听闻</rb><rt>tʽing-meng</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>必定</rb><rt>pih-ding</rt><rb>围困</rb><rt>we-kwʽeng</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>定</rb><rt>ding</rt><rb>地上</rb><rt>di-zông</rt><rb>除掉</rb><rt>djü-diao</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>名字</rb><rt>ming-z</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>尔</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>为了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt><rb>尔个</rb><rt>Ng-go</rt><rb>大</rb><rt>do</rt><rb>名头</rb><rt>ming-deo</rt><rb>咋</rb><rt>dza</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>呢</rb><rt>ni</rt></ruby>？」<sup>10</sup> <ruby><rb>耶和华</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>约书亚</rb><rt>Iah-shü-üô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>尔</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>立起来</rb><rt>lih-kyʽi-læ</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>咋会</rb><rt>dza-we</rt><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>面孔</rb><rt>min-kʽong</rt><rb>扑倒</rb><rt>pʽoh-tao</rt><rb>地</rb><rt>di</rt><rb>呢</rb><rt>ni</rt></ruby>？<sup>11</sup> <u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>已经</rb><rt>yi-kying</rt><rb>犯罪</rb><rt>væn-ze</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>违背</rb><rt>we-be</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>吩咐</rb><rt>feng-fu</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>约</rb><rt>iah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>驮</rb><rt>do</rt><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>该</rb><rt>kæ</rt><rb>毁灭</rb><rt>hwe-mih</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>东西</rb><rt>tong-si</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>偷</rb><rt>tʽeo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>隐瞒</rb><rt>ing-mun</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>甚至于</rb><rt>zing-ts-ü</rt><rb>囥</rb><rt>kʽông</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>自</rb><rt>zi</rt><rb>东西</rb><rt>tong-si</rt><rb>中央</rb><rt>cong-nyiang</rt></ruby>。<sup>12</sup> <ruby><rb>所以</rb><rt>Sô-yi</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>以色列</u>个</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih-go</rt><rb>子孙</rb><rt>ts-seng</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>仇敌</rb><rt>dziu-dih</rt><rb>面前</rb><rt>min-zin</rt><rb>立弗住</rb><rt>lih-feh-djü</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>仇敌</rb><rt>dziu-dih</rt><rb>面前</rb><rt>min-zin</rt><rb>转背</rb><rt>cün-pe</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>因为</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>做了</rb><rt>tso-leh</rt><rb>该</rb><rt>kæ</rt><rb>毁灭个</rb><rt>hwe-mih-go</rt><rb>东西</rb><rt>tong-si</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>若</rb><rt>ziah</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>从</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>中央</rb><rt>cong-nyiang</rt><rb>除掉</rb><rt>djü-diao</rt><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>该</rb><rt>kæ</rt><rb>毁灭个</rb><rt>hwe-mih-go</rt><rb>东西</rb><rt>tong-si</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>以后</rb><rt>yi-ʽeo</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>来东</rb><rt>læ-tong</rt></ruby>。<sup>13</sup> <ruby><rb>起来</rb><rt>Kyʽi-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>百姓</rb><rt>pah-sing</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>圣洁</rb><rt>sing-kyih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：『<ruby><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>Ng-lah</rt><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>自</rb><rt>zi</rt><rb>圣洁</rb><rt>sing-kyih</rt><rb>备办</rb><rt>be-bæn</rt><rb>明朝</rb><rt>ming-tsiao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>因为</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>耶和华</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>，<u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>嗄</rb><rt>ʽô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>中央</rb><rt>cong-nyiang</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>一样</rb><rt>ih-yiang</rt><rb>该</rb><rt>kæ</rt><rb>毁灭个</rb><rt>hwe-mih-go</rt><rb>东西</rb><rt>tong-si</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>仇敌</rb><rt>dziu-dih</rt><rb>面前</rb><rt>min-zin</rt><rb>立弗住</rb><rt>lih-feh-djü</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等到</rb><rt>teng-tao</rt><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>该</rb><rt>kæ</rt><rb>毁灭</rb><rt>hwe-mih</rt><rb>东西</rb><rt>tong-si</rt><rb>从</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>中央</rb><rt>cong-nyiang</rt><rb>除掉</rb><rt>djü-diao</rt></ruby>。』<sup>14</sup> <ruby><rb>所以</rb><rt>Sô-yi</rt><rb>明朝</rb><rt>ming-tsiao</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>照</rb><rt>tsiao</rt><rb>尔拉个</rb><rt>ng-lah-go</rt><rb>支派</rb><rt>ts-pʽa</rt><rb>走上来</rb><rt>tseo-zông-læ</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>耶和华</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>抽着个</rb><rt>tsʽiu-djoh-go</rt><rb>支派</rb><rt>ts-pʽa</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>照</rb><rt>tsiao</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>宗族</rb><rt>tsong-dzoh</rt><rb>走上来</rb><rt>tseo-zông-læ</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>耶和华</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>抽着</rb><rt>tsʽiu-djoh</rt><rb>一族</rb><rt>ih-dzoh</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>照</rb><rt>tsiao</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>房份</rb><rt>vông-veng</rt><rb>走上来</rb><rt>tseo-zông-læ</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>耶和华</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>抽着个</rb><rt>tsʽiu-djoh-go</rt><rb>房份</rb><rt>vông-veng</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>个打个</rb><rt>ko-tang-ko</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>走来</rb><rt>tseo-læ</rt></ruby>。<sup>15</sup> <ruby><rb>被</rb><rt>Be</rt><rb>抽着</rb><rt>tsʽiu-djoh</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>该</rb><rt>kæ</rt><rb>毁灭</rb><rt>hwe-mih</rt><rb>东西个</rb><rt>tong-si-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>火</rb><rt>ho</rt><rb>𤈦</rb><rt>me</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>凡百</rb><rt>væn-pah</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>因为</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>违背</rb><rt>we-be</rt><rb>耶和华个</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô-go</rt><rb>约</rb><rt>iah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>中央</rb><rt>cong-nyiang</rt><rb>乱</rb><rt>lön</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>事干</rb><rt>z-ken</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>\[1\] 此处原书错印为 Pah-dah-li，后文均为 Pah-deh-li</sup>

<sup>16</sup> <u><ruby><rb>约书亚</rb><rt>Iah-shü-üô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>早</rb><rt>tsao</rt><rb>天亮</rb><rt>tʽin-liang</rt><rb>起来</rb><rt>kyʽi-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>把</rb><rt>pô</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>照</rb><rt>tsiao</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>支派</rb><rt>ts-pʽa</rt><rb>带上来</rb><rt>ta-zông-læ</rt></ruby>，<u><ruby><rb>犹大</rb><rt>Yiu-da</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>支派</rb><rt>ts-pʽa</rt><rb>被</rb><rt>be</rt><rb>抽着</rb><rt>tsʽiu-djoh</rt></ruby>。<sup>17</sup> <ruby><rb>带</rb><rt>Ta</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>犹大</rb><rt>Yiu-da</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>宗族</rb><rt>tsong-dzoh</rt><rb>上来</rb><rt>zông-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>抽着</rb><rt>tsʽiu-djoh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>西拉</rb><rt>Si-lah</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>族</rb><rt>dzoh</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>带</rb><rt>ta</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>西拉</rb><rt>Si-lah</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>房份</rb><rt>vông-veng</rt><rb>上来</rb><rt>zông-læ</rt><rb>抽着</rb><rt>tsʽiu-djoh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>撒底</rb><rt>Sah-ti</rt></ruby></u>：<sup>18</sup> <ruby><rb>带</rb><rt>ta</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>一家</rb><rt>ih-kô</rt><rb>个打个</rb><rt>ko-tang-ko</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>上来</rb><rt>zông-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>抽着</rb><rt>tsʽiu-djoh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>犹大</rb><rt>Yiu-da</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>支派</rb><rt>ts-pʽa</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>西拉</rb><rt>Si-lah</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>曾孙</rb><rt>tseng-seng</rt></ruby>，<u><ruby><rb>撒底</rb><rt>Sah-ti</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>孙子</rb><rt>seng-ts</rt></ruby>，<u><ruby><rb>迦米</rb><rt>Kyüô-mi</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>儿子</rb><rt>ng-ts</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>亚干</rb><rt>Üô-ken</rt></ruby></u>。<sup>19</sup> <u><ruby><rb>约书亚</rb><rt>Iah-shü-üô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>亚干</rb><rt>Üô-ken</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>我个</rb><rt>Ngô-go</rt><rb>儿子</rb><rt>ng-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>求</rb><rt>gyiu</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>归</rb><rt>kwe</rt><rb>荣华</rb><rt>yüong-wô</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>耶和华</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>以色列</u>个</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih-go</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>面前</rb><rt>min-zin</rt><rb>认罪</rb><rt>jing-ze</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>做过个</rb><rt>tso-ko-go</rt><rb>现在</rb><rt>yin-dzæ</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt><rb>向</rb><rt>hyiang</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>道</rb><rt>dao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>瞒</rb><rt>mun</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt></ruby>。」<sup>20</sup> <u><ruby><rb>亚干</rb><rt>Üô-ken</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>回答</rb><rt>we-teh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>约书亚</rb><rt>Iah-shü-üô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>真正</rb><rt>tsing-tsing</rt><rb>得罪</rb><rt>teh-ze</rt><rb>耶和华</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>以色列</u>个</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih-go</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>事干</rb><rt>z-ken</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>介个</rb><rt>ka-go</rt><rb>介个</rb><rt>ka-go</rt></ruby>。<sup>21</sup> <ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>看见</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>掳来个</rb><rt>lo-læ-go</rt><rb>东西</rb><rt>tong-si</rt><rb>中央</rb><rt>cong-nyiang</rt><rb>一件</rb><rt>ih-gyin</rt><rb>从</rb><rt>dzong</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>示拿</rb><rt>Z-nô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>出来个</rb><rt>cʽih-læ-go</rt><rb>华丽</rb><rt>wô-li</rt><rb>衣裳</rb><rt>i-zông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>二百</rb><rt>nyi-pah</rt><rb>㖷喀</rb><rt>z-kʽeh</rt></ruby><sup>\[1\]</sup><ruby><rb>银子</rb><rt>nying-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>一锭</rb><rt>ih-ding</rt><rb>金子</rb><rt>kying-ts</rt><rb>五十</rb><rt>ng-jih</rt><rb>㖷喀</rb><rt>z-kʽeh</rt><rb>重</rb><rt>djong</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>生</rb><rt>sang</rt><rb>贪心</rb><rt>tʽen-sing</rt><rb>驮</rb><rt>do</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>东西</rb><rt>tong-si</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>现在</rb><rt>yin-dzæ</rt><rb>囥</rb><rt>kʽông</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>帐篷</rb><rt>tsiang-bong</rt><rb>里向</rb><rt>li-hyiang</rt><rb>地垟里</rb><rt>di-yiang-li</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>银子</rb><rt>nying-ts</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>底下</rb><rt>ti-ʽô</rt></ruby>。」<sup>22</sup> <u><ruby><rb>约书亚</rb><rt>Iah-shü-üô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>差</rb><rt>tsʽa</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>奔</rb><rt>peng</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>帐篷</rb><rt>tsiang-bong</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>东西</rb><rt>tong-si</rt><rb>果然</rb><rt>ko-jün</rt><rb>囥</rb><rt>kʽông</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>帐篷</rb><rt>tsiang-bong</rt><rb>里</rb><rt>li</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>银子</rb><rt>nying-ts</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>底下</rb><rt>ti-ʽô</rt></ruby>。<sup>23</sup> <ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt><rb>从</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>帐篷</rb><rt>tsiang-bong</rt><rb>中央</rb><rt>cong-nyiang</rt><rb>驮出</rb><rt>do-cʽih</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>带</rb><rt>ta</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>约书亚</rb><rt>Iah-shü-üô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>众</rb><rt>cong</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>子孙个</rb><rt>ts-seng-go</rt><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>放</rb><rt>fông</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>耶和华</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>面前</rb><rt>min-zin</rt></ruby>。<sup>24</sup> <u><ruby><rb>约书亚</rb><rt>Iah-shü-üô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>众</rb><rt>cong</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>把</rb><rt>pô</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>西拉</rb><rt>Si-lah</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>儿子</rb><rt>ng-ts</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>亚干</rb><rt>Üô-ken</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>银子</rb><rt>nying-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>衣裳</rb><rt>i-zông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>箇锭</rb><rt>keh-ding</rt><rb>金子</rb><rt>kying-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>还有</rb><rt>wa-yiu</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>儿子</rb><rt>ng-ts</rt><rb>囡</rb><rt>nön</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其个</rb><rt>gyi-go</rt><rb>牛</rb><rt>ngeo</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>驴</rb><rt>li</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>羊</rb><rt>yiang</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>帐篷</rb><rt>tsiang-bong</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>连</rb><rt>lin</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>凡百</rb><rt>væn-pah</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>带上</rb><rt>ta-zông</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>亚割</rb><rt>Üô-keh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>山岙</rb><rt>sæn-ao</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>。<sup>25</sup> <u><ruby><rb>约书亚</rb><rt>Iah-shü-üô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>尔</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>咋会</rb><rt>dza-we</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>害</rb><rt>ʽæ</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt></ruby>？<ruby><rb>今朝</rb><rt>Kying-tsiao</rt><rb>耶和华</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>害</rb><rt>ʽæ</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt></ruby>。」<ruby><rb>众</rb><rt>Cong</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>石头</rb><rt>zah-deo</rt><rb>揞</rb><rt>kʽang</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>煞</rb><rt>sah</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>火</rb><rt>ho</rt><rb>𤈦掉</rb><rt>me-diao</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>并且</rb><rt>ping-tsʽia</rt><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>石头</rb><rt>zah-deo</rt><rb>揞煞</rb><rt>kʽang-sah</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt></ruby>。<sup>26</sup> <ruby><rb>还有</rb><rt>Wa-yiu</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>上</rb><rt>zông</rt><rb>头</rb><rt>deo</rt><rb>堆积</rb><rt>te-tsih</rt><rb>石头</rb><rt>zah-deo</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>大大</rb><rt>do-do</rt><rb>石堆</rb><rt>zah-te</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>如今</rb><rt>jü-kying</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>耶和华</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>息</rb><rt>sih</rt><rb>其个</rb><rt>Gyi-go</rt><rb>大</rb><rt>do</rt><rb>𢛟惶</rb><rt>ô-wông</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>为了</rb><rt>We-leh</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇个</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt><rb>一直</rb><rt>ih-dzih</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>如今</rb><rt>jü-kying</rt><rb>叫勒</rb><rt>kyiao-leh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>亚割</rb><rt>Üô-keh</rt></ruby></u><sup>\[2\]</sup><ruby><rb>山岙</rb><rt>sæn-ao</rt></ruby>。

<sup>\[1\] Kʽen C. 30. 13. 看 C. 30. 13.（原注）</sup>

<sup>\[2\] *Ziu-z* ʽæ ka-shih. 就是「害」解说。（原注）</sup>

---

### Is. VIII.

<ruby><rb>耶和华</rb><rt>YIÆ-ʽO-WÔ</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>约书亚</rb><rt>Iah-shü-üô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>尔</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>弗要</rb><rt>feh-iao</rt><rb>怕惧</rb><rt>pʽô-gyü</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>弗要</rb><rt>feh-iao</rt><rb>胆小</rb><rt>tæn-siao</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>起来</rb><rt>kyʽi-læ</rt><rb>带</rb><rt>ta</rt><rb>拢总</rb><rt>long-tsong</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>打仗个</rb><rt>tang-tsiang-go</rt><rb>百姓</rb><rt>pah-sing</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>上</rb><rt>zông</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>埃</rb><rt>Yiæ</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>城</rb><rt>dzing</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>已经</rb><rt>yi-kying</rt><rb>把</rb><rt>pô</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>埃</u>个</rb><rt>Yiæ-go</rt><rb>王</rb><rt>wông</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>百姓</rb><rt>pah-sing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>城子</rb><rt>dzing-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>地</rb><rt>di</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>交代</rb><rt>kao-dæ</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>手里</rb><rt>siu-li</rt></ruby>。<sup>2</sup> <ruby><rb>尔</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>向</rb><rt>hyiang</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>埃</rb><rt>Yiæ</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>王</rb><rt>wông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>像</rb><rt>ziang</rt><rb>前头</rb><rt>zin-deo</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶利哥</rb><rt>Yiæ-li-ko</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>王</rb><rt>wông</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>做法</rb><rt>tso-fah</rt></ruby>：<ruby><rb>但是</rb><rt>dæn-z</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>掳掠</rb><rt>lo-liah</rt><rb>东西</rb><rt>tong-si</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>牲口</rb><rt>sang-kʽeo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>自</rb><rt>zi</rt><rb>驮归</rb><rt>do-kyü</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>埋伏</rb><rt>ma-voh</rt><rb>兵</rb><rt>ping</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>城子</rb><rt>dzing-ts</rt><rb>后背</rb><rt>ʽeo-pe</rt></ruby>。」<sup>3</sup> <u><ruby><rb>约书亚</rb><rt>Iah-shü-üô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>拢总</rb><rt>long-tsong</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>打仗个</rb><rt>tang-tsiang-go</rt><rb>百姓</rb><rt>pah-sing</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>起来</rb><rt>kyʽi-læ</rt><rb>上</rb><rt>zông</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>埃</rb><rt>Yiæ</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>城</rb><rt>dzing</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>；<u><ruby><rb>约书亚</rb><rt>Iah-shü-üô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>挑选</rb><rt>tʽiao-shün</rt><rb>三万</rb><rt>sæn-væn</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>好佬</rb><rt>hao-lao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>夜里</rb><rt>yia-li</rt><rb>差</rb><rt>tsʽa</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>；<sup>4</sup> <ruby><rb>吩咐</rb><rt>feng-fu</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>Ng-lah</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>城子</rb><rt>dzing-ts</rt><rb>后背</rb><rt>ʽeo-pe</rt><rb>对着</rb><rt>te-djoh</rt><rb>城子</rb><rt>dzing-ts</rt><rb>埋伏</rb><rt>ma-voh</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>弗可</rb><rt>feh-kʽo</rt><rb>离</rb><rt>li</rt><rb>城</rb><rt>dzing</rt><rb>过</rb><rt>ko</rt><rb>远</rb><rt>yün</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>该</rb><rt>kæ</rt><rb>自</rb><rt>zi</rt><rb>预备</rb><rt>yü-be</rt></ruby>；<sup>5</sup> <ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>来东个</rb><rt>læ-tong-go</rt><rb>百姓</rb><rt>pah-sing</rt><rb>拢总</rb><rt>long-tsong</rt><rb>近拢</rb><rt>gying-long</rt><rb>城子</rb><rt>dzing-ts</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>像</rb><rt>ziang</rt><rb>前头</rb><rt>zin-deo</rt><rb>一样</rb><rt>ih-yiang</rt><rb>出</rb><rt>cʽih</rt><rb>城</rb><rt>dzing</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>抵敌</rb><rt>ti-dih</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>面前</rb><rt>min-zin</rt><rb>逃走</rb><rt>dao-tseo</rt></ruby>；<sup>6</sup> <ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>必定</rb><rt>pih-ding</rt><rb>出来</rb><rt>cʽih-læ</rt><rb>追赶</rb><rt>tse-ken</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等到</rb><rt>teng-tao</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>哄</rb><rt>hong</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>远开</rb><rt>yün-kʽæ</rt><rb>城子</rb><rt>dzing-ts</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>因为</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>必定</rb><rt>pih-ding</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：『<ruby><rb>箇星</rb><rt>Keh-sing</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>像</rb><rt>ziang</rt><rb>从前</rb><rt>dzong-zin</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>面前</rb><rt>min-zin</rt><rb>逃走</rb><rt>dao-tseo</rt></ruby>；』<sup>7</sup> <ruby><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>面前</rb><rt>min-zin</rt><rb>逃走</rb><rt>dao-tseo</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>从</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>埋伏</rb><rt>ma-voh</rt><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt><rb>起来</rb><rt>kyʽi-læ</rt><rb>夺</rb><rt>deh</rt><rb>箇个</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>城子</rb><rt>dzing-ts</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>因为</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>耶和华</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>尔拉个</rb><rt>ng-lah-go</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>必定</rb><rt>pih-ding</rt><rb>交代</rb><rt>kao-dæ</rt><rb>尔个</rb><rt>ng-go</rt><rb>手里</rb><rt>siu-li</rt></ruby>。<sup>8</sup> <ruby><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>Ng-lah</rt><rb>城子</rb><rt>dzing-ts</rt><rb>夺来</rb><rt>deh-læ</rt><rb>之后</rb><rt>ts-ʽeo</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>城里</rb><rt>dzing-li</rt><rb>放</rb><rt>fông</rt><rb>火</rb><rt>ho</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>必要</rb><rt>pih-iao</rt><rb>照</rb><rt>tsiao</rt><rb>耶和华</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>命令</rb><rt>ming-ling</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>吩咐</rb><rt>feng-fu</rt><rb>尔拉个</rb><rt>ng-lah-go</rt></ruby>。」<sup>9</sup> <u><ruby><rb>约书亚</rb><rt>Iah-shü-üô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>差</rb><rt>tsʽa</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>之后</rb><rt>ts-ʽeo</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>埋伏</rb><rt>ma-voh</rt><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>庉</rb><rt>deng</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>伯特利</rb><rt>Pah-deh-li</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>埃</rb><rt>Yiæ</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>城</rb><rt>dzing</rt><rb>当中</rb><rt>tông-cong</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>埃</rb><rt>Yiæ</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>城</rb><rt>dzing</rt><rb>西边</rb><rt>si-pin</rt></ruby>；<u><ruby><rb>约书亚</rb><rt>Iah-shü-üô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>一夜</rb><rt>ih-yia</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>百姓</rb><rt>pah-sing</rt><rb>中央</rb><rt>cong-nyiang</rt><rb>庉宿</rb><rt>deng-soh</rt></ruby>。

<sup>10</sup> <u><ruby><rb>约书亚</rb><rt>Iah-shü-üô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>早</rb><rt>tsao</rt><rb>天亮</rb><rt>tʽin-liang</rt><rb>起来</rb><rt>kyʽi-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>聚拢</rb><rt>jü-long</rt><rb>百姓</rb><rt>pah-sing</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>长老</rb><rt>tsiang-lao</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>百姓</rb><rt>pah-sing</rt><rb>面前</rb><rt>min-zin</rt><rb>上</rb><rt>zông</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>埃</rb><rt>Yiæ</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>城</rb><rt>dzing</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>。<sup>11</sup> <ruby><rb>众</rb><rt>Cong</rt><rb>百姓</rb><rt>pah-sing</rt><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>打仗</rb><rt>tang-tsiang</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>上去</rb><rt>zông-kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>近拢</rb><rt>gying-long</rt><rb>城子</rb><rt>dzing-ts</rt><rb>面前</rb><rt>min-zin</rt><rb>搭营</rb><rt>tah-ying</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>埃</rb><rt>Yiæ</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>城</rb><rt>dzing</rt><rb>北边</rb><rt>poh-pin</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>埃</rb><rt>Yiæ</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>城</rb><rt>dzing</rt><rb>中央</rb><rt>cong-nyiang</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>一个</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>山岙</rb><rt>sæn-ao</rt></ruby>。<sup>12</sup> <ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>大约</rb><rt>da-iah</rt><rb>点</rb><rt>tin</rt><rb>五千</rb><rt>ng-tsʽin</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>埋伏</rb><rt>ma-voh</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>伯特利</rb><rt>Pah-deh-li</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>埃</rb><rt>Yiæ</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>城</rb><rt>dzing</rt><rb>当中</rb><rt>tông-cong</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>城子</rb><rt>dzing-ts</rt><rb>西边</rb><rt>si-pin</rt></ruby>。<sup>13</sup> <ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>派定</rb><rt>pʽa-ding</rt><rb>百姓</rb><rt>pah-sing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>全</rb><rt>djün</rt><rb>营</rb><rt>ying</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>城子</rb><rt>dzing-ts</rt><rb>北边</rb><rt>poh-pin</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>还有</rb><rt>wa-yiu</rt><rb>埋伏</rb><rt>ma-voh</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>城子</rb><rt>dzing-ts</rt><rb>西边</rb><rt>si-pin</rt></ruby>。<u><ruby><rb>约书亚</rb><rt>Iah-shü-üô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>一夜</rb><rt>ih-yia</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>山岙</rb><rt>sæn-ao</rt><rb>中央</rb><rt>cong-nyiang</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>。<sup>14</sup> <u><ruby><rb>约书亚</rb><rt>Iah-shü-üô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>众</rb><rt>cong</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>面前</rb><rt>min-zin</rt><rb>假</rb><rt>kô</rt><rb>装</rb><rt>tsông</rt><rb>打败了</rb><rt>tang-ba-de</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>依勒</rb><rt>i-leh</rt><rb>旷野</rb><rt>kwʽông-iæ</rt><rb>箇埭</rb><rt>keh-da</rt><rb>路</rb><rt>lu</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>逃走</rb><rt>dao-tseo</rt></ruby>。<sup>16</sup> <ruby><rb>耶和华</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>约书亚</rb><rt>Iah-shü-üô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>用</rb><rt>Yüong</rt><rb>向</rb><rt>hyiang</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>埃</rb><rt>Yiæ</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>城</rb><rt>dzing</rt><rb>提起</rb><rt>di-kyʽi</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>手里</rb><rt>siu-li</rt><rb>驮的个</rb><rt>do-tih-go</rt><rb>枪</rb><rt>tsʽiang</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>因为</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>必定</rb><rt>pih-ding</rt><rb>把</rb><rt>pô</rt><rb>城子</rb><rt>dzing-ts</rt><rb>交代</rb><rt>kao-dæ</rt><rb>尔个</rb><rt>ng-go</rt><rb>手里</rb><rt>siu-li</rt></ruby>；」<u><ruby><rb>约书亚</rb><rt>Iah-shü-üô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>向</rb><rt>hyiang</rt><rb>城子</rb><rt>dzing-ts</rt><rb>提起</rb><rt>di-kyʽi</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>手里</rb><rt>siu-li</rt><rb>驮的个</rb><rt>do-tih-go</rt><rb>枪</rb><rt>tsʽiang</rt></ruby>。<sup>19</sup> <u><ruby><rb>约书亚</rb><rt>Iah-shü-üô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>一</rb><rt>ih</rt><rb>提起</rb><rt>di-kyʽi</rt><rb>手</rb><rt>siu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>埋伏间个</rb><rt>ma-voh-kæn-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>快快</rb><rt>kwʽa-kwʽa</rt><rb>从</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>其拉个</rb><rt>gyi-lah-go</rt><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt><rb>出来</rb><rt>cʽih-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>奔进</rb><rt>peng-tsing</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>夺</rb><rt>deh</rt><rb>城子</rb><rt>dzing-ts</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>快快</rb><rt>kwʽa-kwʽa</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>城里</rb><rt>dzing-li</rt><rb>放</rb><rt>fông</rt><rb>火</rb><rt>ho</rt></ruby>。<sup>20</sup> <u><ruby><rb>埃</rb><rt>Yiæ</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>城</rb><rt>dzing</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>向</rb><rt>hyiang</rt><rb>后</rb><rt>ʽeo</rt><rb>一看</rb><rt>ih-kʽen</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>看见</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt><rb>城里</rb><rt>dzing-li</rt><rb>烟头</rb><rt>in-deo</rt><rb>冲</rb><rt>cʽong</rt><rb>天</rb><rt>tʽin</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>或者</rb><rt>ʽôh-tsia</rt><rb>荡边</rb><rt>dông-pin</rt><rb>箇边</rb><rt>keh-pin</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>呒力</rb><rt>m-lih</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>逃</rb><rt>dao</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>向</rb><rt>hyiang</rt><rb>旷野</rb><rt>kwʽông-iæ</rt><rb>逃个</rb><rt>dao-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>回转来</rb><rt>we-cün-læ</rt><rb>攻打</rb><rt>kong-tang</rt><rb>追</rb><rt>tse</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>。<sup>21</sup> <u><ruby><rb>约书亚</rb><rt>Iah-shü-üô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>众</rb><rt>cong</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>看见</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt><rb>伏兵</rb><rt>voh-ping</rt><rb>已经</rb><rt>yi-kying</rt><rb>夺着</rb><rt>deh-djoh</rt><rb>城子</rb><rt>dzing-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>城里个</rb><rt>dzing-li-go</rt><rb>烟头</rb><rt>in-deo</rt><rb>来的</rb><rt>læ-tih</rt><rb>冲上</rb><rt>cʽong-zông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>回转</rb><rt>we-cün</rt><rb>打煞</rb><rt>tang-sah</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>埃</rb><rt>Yiæ</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>城个</rb><rt>dzing-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>。<sup>22</sup> <ruby><rb>伏兵</rb><rt>Voh-ping</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>从</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>城里</rb><rt>dzing-li</rt><rb>出来</rb><rt>cʽih-læ</rt><rb>攻打</rb><rt>kong-tang</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>致使得</rb><rt>cü-s-teh</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>围困</rb><rt>we-kwʽeng</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>当中</rb><rt>tông-cong</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>荡边</rb><rt>dông-pin</rt><rb>箇边</rb><rt>keh-pin</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>打煞</rb><rt>tang-sah</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>呒得</rb><rt>m-neh</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>一个</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>留落</rb><rt>liu-loh</rt><rb>逃走</rb><rt>dao-tseo</rt></ruby>。<sup>23</sup> <ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt><rb>活擒</rb><rt>weh-gying</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>埃</u>个</rb><rt>Yiæ-go</rt><rb>王</rb><rt>wông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>解</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>约书亚</rb><rt>Iah-shü-üô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt></ruby>。<sup>24</sup> <u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>杀完</rb><rt>sah-wun</rt><rb>庉</rb><rt>deng</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>埃</u>个</rb><rt>Yiæ-go</rt><rb>众人</rb><rt>cong-nying</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>田里</rb><rt>din-li</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>追赶</rb><rt>tse-ken</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>旷野</rb><rt>kwʽông-iæ</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>被</rb><rt>be</rt><rb>刀</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>杀落</rb><rt>sah-loh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>一直</rb><rt>ih-dzih</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>杀完</rb><rt>sah-wun</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇遭</rb><rt>keh-tsao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>众人</rb><rt>cong-nying</rt><rb>回</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>埃</rb><rt>Yiæ</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>城</rb><rt>dzing</rt><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>刀</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>攻打</rb><rt>kong-tang</rt></ruby>。<sup>25</sup> <ruby><rb>箇日</rb><rt>Keh-nyih</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>打煞个</rb><rt>tang-sah-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>男女</rb><rt>nen-nyü</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>一万</rb><rt>ih-væn</rt><rb>二千</rb><rt>nyi-tsʽin</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>埃</rb><rt>Yiæ</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>城个</rb><rt>dzing-go</rt><rb>众人</rb><rt>cong-nying</rt></ruby>。<sup>26</sup> <ruby><rb>因为</rb><rt>Ing-we</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>约书亚</rb><rt>Iah-shü-üô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>放落</rb><rt>fông-loh</rt><rb>手</rb><rt>siu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>提起</rb><rt>di-kyʽi</rt><rb>枪个</rb><rt>tsʽiang-go</rt><rb>手</rb><rt>siu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>直到</rb><rt>dzih-tao</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>庉</rb><rt>deng</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>埃</rb><rt>Yiæ</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>城个</rb><rt>dzing-go</rt><rb>众人</rb><rt>cong-nying</rt><rb>除灭</rb><rt>djü-mih</rt><rb>完</rb><rt>wun</rt></ruby>。<sup>27</sup> <ruby><rb>只有</rb><rt>Tsih-yiu</rt><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>城子个</rb><rt>dzing-ts-go</rt><rb>牲口</rb><rt>sang-kʽeo</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>财物</rb><rt>dzæ-veh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>自</rb><rt>zi</rt><rb>驮去</rb><rt>do-kyʽi</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>照</rb><rt>tsiao</rt><rb>耶和华</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>吩咐</rb><rt>feng-fu</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>约书亚</u>个</rb><rt>Iah-shü-üô-go</rt><rb>命令</rb><rt>ming-ling</rt></ruby>。<sup>28</sup> <u><ruby><rb>约书亚</rb><rt>Iah-shü-üô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>烧掉</rb><rt>siao-diao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>埃</rb><rt>Yiæ</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>城</rb><rt>dzing</rt><rb>永远</rb><rt>üong-yün</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>高山堆</rb><rt>kao-sæn-te</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>荒地</rb><rt>hwông-di</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>如今</rb><rt>jü-kying</rt></ruby>。<sup>29</sup> <ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>把</rb><rt>pô</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>埃</u>个</rb><rt>Yiæ-go</rt><rb>王</rb><rt>wông</rt><rb>挂</rb><rt>kwô</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>树上</rb><rt>jü-zông</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>夜快</rb><rt>yia-kwʽa</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>日头</rb><rt>nyih-deo</rt><rb>落山</rb><rt>loh-sæn</rt><rb>时候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt></ruby>，<u><ruby><rb>约书亚</rb><rt>Iah-shü-üô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>吩咐</rb><rt>feng-fu</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>从</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>树里</rb><rt>jü-li</rt><rb>驮落</rb><rt>do-loh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>尸首</rb><rt>s-siu</rt><rb>揞</rb><rt>kʽang</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>城</rb><rt>dzing</rt><rb>门口</rb><rt>meng-kʽeo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>上头</rb><rt>zông-deo</rt><rb>堆</rb><rt>te</rt><rb>一个</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>大</rb><rt>do</rt><rb>石堆</rb><rt>zah-te</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>如今</rb><rt>jü-kying</rt><rb>还</rb><rt>wa</rt><rb>来间</rb><rt>læ-kæn</rt></ruby>。

<sup>30</sup> <ruby><rb>箇遭</rb><rt>Keh-tsao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>约书亚</rb><rt>Iah-shü-üô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以八</rb><rt>Yi-pah</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>山</rb><rt>sæn</rt><rb>为了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt><rb>耶和华</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>以色列</u>个</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih-go</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>筑</rb><rt>coh</rt><rb>一座</rb><rt>ih-zo</rt><rb>坛</rb><rt>dæn</rt></ruby>，<sup>31</sup> <ruby><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>呒有</rb><rt>m-yiu</rt><rb>经过</rb><rt>kying-ko</rt><rb>铁器个</rb><rt>tʽih-kyʽi-go</rt><rb>整块</rb><rt>tsing-kwʽe</rt><rb>石头</rb><rt>zah-deo</rt><rb>筑个</rb><rt>coh-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>照</rb><rt>tsiao</rt><rb>耶和华</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>奴仆</rb><rt>nu-boh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>吩咐</rb><rt>feng-fu</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>子孙</rb><rt>ts-seng</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>像</rb><rt>ziang</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>律法</rb><rt>lih-fah</rt><rb>书里</rb><rt>shü-li</rt><rb>写的</rb><rt>sia-tih</rt><rb>介个</rb><rt>ka-go</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>箇座</rb><rt>keh-zo</rt><rb>坛上</rb><rt>dæn-zông</rt><rb>献</rb><rt>hyin</rt><rb>燔祭</rb><rt>væn-tsi</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>耶和华</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>献</rb><rt>hyin</rt><rb>谢恩祭</rb><rt>zia-eng-tsi</rt></ruby>。<sup>32</sup> <u><ruby><rb>约书亚</rb><rt>Iah-shü-üô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>箇头</rb><rt>keh-deo</rt><rb>把</rb><rt>pô</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>子孙</rb><rt>ts-seng</rt><rb>面前</rb><rt>min-zin</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>写个</rb><rt>sia-go</rt><rb>律法</rb><rt>lih-fah</rt><rb>书</rb><rt>shü</rt><rb>抄</rb><rt>tsʽao</rt><rb>写</rb><rt>sia</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>石头</rb><rt>zah-deo</rt><rb>上</rb><rt>zông</rt></ruby>。<sup>33</sup> <ruby><rb>众</rb><rt>Cong</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>弗论</rb><rt>feh-leng</rt><rb>本地</rb><rt>peng-di</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>外来</rb><rt>nga-læ</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>长老</rb><rt>tsiang-lao</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>官府</rb><rt>kwun-fu</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>士师</rb><rt>z-s</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>立</rb><rt>lih</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>约箱</rb><rt>iah-siang</rt><rb>两边</rb><rt>liang-pin</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>抬</rb><rt>dæ</rt><rb>耶和华个</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô-go</rt><rb>约箱</rb><rt>iah-siang</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>利未</rb><rt>Li-vi</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>祭司</rb><rt>tsi-s</rt><rb>面前</rb><rt>min-zin</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>一半</rb><rt>ih-pun</rt><rb>对着</rb><rt>te-djoh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>其利心</rb><rt>Gyi-li-sing</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>山</rb><rt>sæn</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>一半</rb><rt>ih-pun</rt><rb>对着</rb><rt>te-djoh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以八</rb><rt>Yi-pah</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>山</rb><rt>sæn</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>照</rb><rt>tsiao</rt><rb>耶和华</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>奴仆</rb><rt>nu-boh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>前头</rb><rt>zin-deo</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>吩咐个</rb><rt>feng-fu-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>先</rb><rt>sin</rt><rb>祝福</rb><rt>coh-foh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>百姓</rb><rt>pah-sing</rt></ruby>。<sup>34</sup> <ruby><rb>后头</rb><rt>ʽEo-deo</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>读</rb><rt>doh</rt><rb>律法里</rb><rt>lih-fah-li</rt><rb>一切</rb><rt>ih-tsʽih</rt><rb>说话</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>应许</rb><rt>ing-hyü</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>福气</rb><rt>foh-kyʽi</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>祸祟</rb><rt>ʽo-se</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>照</rb><rt>tsiao</rt><rb>凡百</rb><rt>væn-pah</rt><rb>律法</rb><rt>lih-fah</rt><rb>书里</rb><rt>shü-li</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>写的个</rb><rt>sia-tih-go</rt></ruby>。<sup>35</sup> <u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>凡百</rb><rt>væn-pah</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>吩咐个</rb><rt>feng-fu-go</rt></ruby>，<u><ruby><rb>约书亚</rb><rt>Iah-shü-üô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>众</rb><rt>cong</rt><rb>公会</rb><rt>kong-we</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>女人</rb><rt>nyü-nying</rt><rb>小人</rb><rt>siao-nying</rt><rb>来往个</rb><rt>læ-w̆ông-go</rt><rb>外来</rb><rt>nga-læ</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>面前</rb><rt>min-zin</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>呒有</rb><rt>m-yiu</rt><rb>一句</rb><rt>ih-kyü</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>读</rb><rt>doh</rt></ruby>。

---

### Is. IX.

<u><ruby><rb>约但</rb><rt>IAH-DÆN</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>河</rb><rt>ʽo</rt><rb>箇边</rb><rt>keh-pin</rt><rb>庉</rb><rt>deng</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>山</rb><rt>sæn</rt><rb>地</rb><rt>di</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>平</rb><rt>bing</rt><rb>地</rb><rt>di</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>黎巴嫩</rb><rt>Li-pô-neng</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>面前</rb><rt>min-zin</rt><rb>大</rb><rt>Do</rt><rb>海</rb><rt>hæ</rt><rb>边沿个</rb><rt>pin-yin-go</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>王</rb><rt>wông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>赫</rb><rt>Heh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>、<u><ruby><rb>亚摩利</rb><rt>Üô-mo-li</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>、<u><ruby><rb>迦南</rb><rt>Kyüô-nen</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>、<u><ruby><rb>比利洗</rb><rt>Pi-li-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>、<u><ruby><rb>希未</rb><rt>Hyi-vi</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>、<u><ruby><rb>耶布士</rb><rt>Yiæ-pu-z</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人个</rb><rt>nying-go</rt><rb>王</rb><rt>wông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>听闻</rb><rt>tʽing-meng</rt><rb>箇个</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>事干</rb><rt>z-ken</rt></ruby>。<sup>2</sup> <ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>同心</rb><rt>dong-sing</rt><rb>合意</rb><rt>ʽeh-i</rt><rb>聚拢</rb><rt>jü-long</rt><rb>攻打</rb><rt>kong-tang</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>约书亚</rb><rt>Iah-shü-üô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>。

<sup>3</sup> <ruby><rb>但是</rb><rt>Dæn-z</rt><rb>庉</rb><rt>deng</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>基遍</u>个</rb><rt>Kyi-pin-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>听闻</rb><rt>tʽing-meng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>约书亚</rb><rt>Iah-shü-üô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>向</rb><rt>hyiang</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶利哥</rb><rt>Yiæ-li-ko</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>埃</rb><rt>Yiæ</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>城</rb><rt>dzing</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>做过个</rb><rt>tso-ko-go</rt><rb>事干</rb><rt>z-ken</rt></ruby>，<sup>4</sup> <ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>设</rb><rt>shih</rt><rb>奸计</rb><rt>kæn-kyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>假</rb><rt>kô</rt><rb>扮</rb><rt>pæn</rt><rb>差使</rb><rt>tsʽa-s</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>驮勒</rb><rt>do-leh</rt><rb>破个</rb><rt>pʽo-go</rt><rb>袋皮</rb><rt>dæ-bi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>补过了</rb><rt>pu-ko-liao</rt><rb>𣥼</rb><rt>tsi</rt><rb>酒</rb><rt>tsiu</rt><rb>旧</rb><rt>gyiu</rt><rb>皮袋</rb><rt>bi-dæ</rt><rb>放</rb><rt>fông</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>驴子</rb><rt>li-ts</rt><rb>上</rb><rt>zông</rt></ruby>；<sup>5</sup> <ruby><rb>还有</rb><rt>wa-yiu</rt><rb>脚</rb><rt>kyiah</rt><rb>穿</rb><rt>cʽün</rt><rb>布料个</rb><rt>pu-liao-go</rt><rb>旧</rb><rt>gyiu</rt><rb>鞋</rb><rt>ʽa</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>身上</rb><rt>sing-zông</rt><rb>穿</rb><rt>cʽün</rt><rb>旧</rb><rt>gyiu</rt><rb>衣裳</rb><rt>i-zông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>吃食</rb><rt>kyʽüoh-zih</rt><rb>干燥</rb><rt>ken-sao</rt><rb>发</rb><rt>fah</rt><rb>斑毛个</rb><rt>pæn-mao-go</rt></ruby>。<sup>6</sup> <ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt><rb>走到</rb><rt>tseo-tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>吉甲</rb><rt>Kyih-kyiah</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>营寨</rb><rt>ying-za</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>约书亚</rb><rt>Iah-shü-üô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>向</rb><rt>hyiang</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>Ah-lah</rt><rb>从</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>远</rb><rt>yün</rt><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>难间</rb><rt>næn-kæn</rt><rb>求</rb><rt>gyiu</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>立</rb><rt>lih</rt><rb>一个</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>约</rb><rt>iah</rt></ruby>。」<sup>7</sup> <u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>希未</rb><rt>Hyi-vi</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>恐怕</rb><rt>Kʽong-pʽô</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>庉</rb><rt>deng</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>中央</rb><rt>cong-nyiang</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>咋</rb><rt>dza</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>立约</rb><rt>lih-iah</rt><rb>呢</rb><rt>ni</rt></ruby>？」<sup>8</sup> <ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>约书亚</rb><rt>Iah-shü-üô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>尔个</rb><rt>ng-go</rt><rb>奴仆</rb><rt>nu-boh</rt></ruby>。」<u><ruby><rb>约书亚</rb><rt>Iah-shü-üô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>Ng-lah</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>啥个</rb><rt>soh-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>？<ruby><rb>从</rb><rt>Dzong</rt><rb>何里</rb><rt>ʽah-li</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>？」<sup>9</sup> <ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>尔个</rb><rt>Ng-go</rt><rb>奴仆</rb><rt>nu-boh</rt><rb>为了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt><rb>耶和华</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>尔个</rb><rt>ng-go</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>名头</rb><rt>ming-deo</rt><rb>从</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>顶</rb><rt>ting</rt><rb>远</rb><rt>yün</rt><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>因为</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>听闻</rb><rt>tʽing-meng</rt><rb>其个</rb><rt>Gyi-go</rt><rb>名声</rb><rt>ming-sing</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>埃及</rb><rt>Yiæ-gyih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>凡百</rb><rt>væn-pah</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>做个</rb><rt>tso-go</rt></ruby>，<sup>10</sup> <ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>向</rb><rt>hyiang</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>约但</rb><rt>Iah-dæn</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>河外</rb><rt>ʽo-nga</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>亚摩利</rb><rt>Üô-mo-li</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>两</rb><rt>liang</rt><rb>王</rb><rt>wông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>希实本</rb><rt>Hyi-jih-peng</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>王</rb><rt>wông</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>西宏</rb><rt>Si-ʽong</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>亚大录</u>个</rb><rt>Üô-da-loh-go</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>巴珊</rb><rt>Pô-sæn</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>王</rb><rt>wông</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>噩</rb><rt>ngoh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>做个</rb><rt>tso-go</rt><rb>事干</rb><rt>z-ken</rt></ruby>。<sup>11</sup> <ruby><rb>阿拉个</rb><rt>Ah-lah-go</rt><rb>长老</rb><rt>tsiang-lao</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>庉</rb><rt>deng</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>地方个</rb><rt>di-fông-go</rt><rb>众人</rb><rt>cong-nying</rt><rb>向</rb><rt>hyiang</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：『<ruby><rb>手里</rb><rt>Siu-li</rt><rb>驮</rb><rt>do</rt><rb>路上</rb><rt>lu-zông</rt><rb>用个</rb><rt>yüong-go</rt><rb>口粮</rb><rt>kʽeo-liang</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>迎接</rb><rt>nying-tsih</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：<ruby><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>Ah-lah</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>尔个</rb><rt>ng-go</rt><rb>奴仆</rb><rt>nu-boh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>现在</rb><rt>yin-dzæ</rt><rb>求</rb><rt>gyiu</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>立</rb><rt>lih</rt><rb>一个</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>约</rb><rt>iah</rt></ruby>。』<sup>12</sup> <ruby><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>Ah-lah</rt><rb>动身</rb><rt>dong-sing</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>箇日</rb><rt>keh-nyih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>从</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>屋里</rb><rt>oh-li</rt><rb>驮</rb><rt>do</rt><rb>箇个</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>饼</rb><rt>ping</rt><rb>当</rb><rt>tông</rt><rb>口粮</rb><rt>kʽeo-liang</rt><rb>还</rb><rt>wa</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>热个</rb><rt>nyih-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>现在</rb><rt>yin-dzæ</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>干燥</rb><rt>ken-sao</rt><rb>发</rb><rt>fah</rt><rb>斑毛</rb><rt>pæn-mao</rt><rb>了</rb><rt>de</rt></ruby>。<sup>13</sup> <ruby><rb>箇星</rb><rt>Keh-sing</rt><rb>𣥼</rb><rt>tsi</rt><rb>酒个</rb><rt>tsiu-go</rt><rb>皮袋</rb><rt>bi-dæ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>𣥼</rb><rt>tsi</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>时候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt><rb>还</rb><rt>wa</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>新个</rb><rt>sing-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>难间</rb><rt>næn-kæn</rt><rb>已经</rb><rt>yi-kying</rt><rb>破了</rb><rt>pʽo-de</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>衣裳</rb><rt>i-zông</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>鞋</rb><rt>ʽa</rt><rb>因为</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>路途</rb><rt>lu-du</rt><rb>遥远</rb><rt>yiao-yün</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>破了</rb><rt>pʽo-de</rt></ruby>。」<sup>14</sup> <u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>驮</rb><rt>do</rt><rb>其拉个</rb><rt>gyi-lah-go</rt><rb>口粮</rb><rt>kʽeo-liang</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>请问</rb><rt>tsʽing-meng</rt><rb>耶和华</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt></ruby>。<sup>15</sup> <u><ruby><rb>约书亚</rb><rt>Iah-shü-üô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>讲和</rb><rt>kông-ʽo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>立约</rb><rt>lih-iah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>应许</rb><rt>ing-hyü</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>存活</rb><rt>dzeng-weh</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>公会个</rb><rt>kong-we-go</rt><rb>牧伯</rb><rt>moh-pah</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>罚咒</rb><rt>vah-tsiu</rt></ruby>。<sup>16</sup> <ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>Teng</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>立约</rb><rt>lih-iah</rt><rb>之后</rb><rt>ts-ʽeo</rt><rb>过了</rb><rt>ko-leh</rt><rb>三日</rb><rt>sæn-nyih</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>听闻</rb><rt>tʽing-meng</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>邻居</rb><rt>ling-kyü</rt><rb>庉</rb><rt>deng</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>中央个</rb><rt>cong-nyiang-go</rt></ruby>。<sup>17</sup> <u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>子孙</rb><rt>ts-seng</rt><rb>动身</rb><rt>dong-sing</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>第三</rb><rt>di-sæn</rt><rb>日</rb><rt>nyih</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>城子</rb><rt>dzing-ts</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>其拉个</rb><rt>Gyi-lah-go</rt><rb>城子</rb><rt>dzing-ts</rt><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>基遍</rb><rt>Kyi-pin</rt></ruby></u>、<u><ruby><rb>基非拉</rb><rt>Kyi-fi-lah</rt></ruby></u>、<u><ruby><rb>比录</rb><rt>Pi-loh</rt></ruby></u>、<u><ruby><rb>基列耶琳</rb><rt>Kyi-lih-yiæ-ling</rt></ruby></u>。<sup>18</sup> <u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>子孙</rb><rt>ts-seng</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>攻打</rb><rt>kong-tang</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>因为</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>公会个</rb><rt>kong-we-go</rt><rb>牧伯</rb><rt>moh-pah</rt><rb>已经</rb><rt>yi-kying</rt><rb>指点</rb><rt>ts-tin</rt><rb>耶和华</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>以色列</u>个</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih-go</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>罚咒</rb><rt>vah-tsiu</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>公会</rb><rt>Kong-we</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>媢怨</rb><rt>mao-ün</rt><rb>牧伯</rb><rt>moh-pah</rt></ruby>。<sup>19</sup> <ruby><rb>但是</rb><rt>Dæn-z</rt><rb>众</rb><rt>cong</rt><rb>牧伯</rb><rt>moh-pah</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>众</rb><rt>cong</rt><rb>公会</rb><rt>kong-we</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>Ah-lah</rt><rb>已经</rb><rt>yi-kying</rt><rb>指点</rb><rt>ts-tin</rt><rb>耶和华</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>罚过</rb><rt>vah-ko</rt><rb>咒</rb><rt>tsiu</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>所以</rb><rt>sô-yi</rt><rb>现在</rb><rt>yin-dzæ</rt><rb>弗可</rb><rt>feh-kʽo</rt><rb>犯着</rb><rt>væn-djoh</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt></ruby>。<sup>20</sup> <ruby><rb>现在</rb><rt>Yin-dzæ</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>被</rb><rt>be</rt><rb>咒诅</rb><rt>tsiu-cü</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>必定</rb><rt>pih-ding</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>断</rb><rt>dön</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>奴仆</rb><rt>nu-boh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>神明个</rb><rt>Jing-ming-go</rt><rb>殿里</rb><rt>din-li</rt><rb>劈柴</rb><rt>pʽih-za</rt><rb>打水个</rb><rt>tang-shü-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>。」<sup>24</sup> <ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt><rb>回答</rb><rt>we-teh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>约书亚</rb><rt>Iah-shü-üô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>因为</rb><rt>Ing-we</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>靠实</rb><rt>kʽao-jih</rt><rb>通知</rb><rt>tʽong-cü</rt><rb>奴仆</rb><rt>nu-boh</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：『<ruby><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>Ng-lah</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>耶和华</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>吩咐</rb><rt>feng-fu</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>奴仆</rb><rt>nu-boh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>赐拨</rb><rt>s-peh</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>箇个</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>地</rb><rt>di</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>还有</rb><rt>wa-yiu</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>面前</rb><rt>min-zin</rt><rb>除灭</rb><rt>djü-mih</rt><rb>庉</rb><rt>deng</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>地上个</rb><rt>di-zông-go</rt><rb>众</rb><rt>cong</rt><rb>百姓</rb><rt>pah-sing</rt></ruby>；』<ruby><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>为了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>缘故</rb><rt>yün-kwu</rt><rb>大样</rb><rt>do-nyiang</rt><rb>怕</rb><rt>pʽô</rt><rb>丧命</rb><rt>sông-ming</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>所以</rb><rt>sô-yi</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>箇起</rb><rt>keh-kyʽi</rt><rb>事干</rb><rt>z-ken</rt></ruby>。<sup>25</sup> <ruby><rb>现在</rb><rt>Yin-dzæ</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>尔个</rb><rt>ng-go</rt><rb>手里</rb><rt>siu-li</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>照</rb><rt>tsiao</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>看</rb><rt>kʽen</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>好个</rb><rt>hao-go</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>公平个</rb><rt>kong-bing-go</rt><rb>向</rb><rt>hyiang</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt></ruby>。」<sup>26</sup> <u><ruby><rb>约书亚</rb><rt>Iah-shü-üô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>箇日</rb><rt>keh-nyih</rt><rb>派</rb><rt>pʽa</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>劈柴</rb><rt>pʽih-za</rt><rb>打</rb><rt>tang</rt><rb>水个</rb><rt>shü-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>公会</rb><rt>kong-we</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>耶和华个</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô-go</rt><rb>坛里</rb><rt>dæn-li</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>如今</rb><rt>jü-kying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>主</rb><rt>Cü</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>拣选</rb><rt>kæn-shün</rt><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt></ruby>。
