### Is. X.

<u><ruby><rb>耶路撒冷</rb><rt>YIÆ-LU-SAH-LENG</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>王</rb><rt>wông</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>亚多尼洗特</rb><rt>Üô-to-nyi-si-deh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>听闻</rb><rt>tʽing-meng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>约书亚</rb><rt>Iah-shü-üô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>已经</rb><rt>yi-kying</rt><rb>夺</rb><rt>deh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>埃</rb><rt>Yiæ</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>城</rb><rt>dzing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>把</rb><rt>pô</rt><rb>城子</rb><rt>dzing-ts</rt><rb>毁灭</rb><rt>hwe-mih</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>像</rb><rt>ziang</rt><rb>从前</rb><rt>dzong-zin</rt><rb>向</rb><rt>hyiang</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶利哥</rb><rt>Yiæ-li-ko</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其个</rb><rt>gyi-go</rt><rb>王</rb><rt>wông</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>做个</rb><rt>tso-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>向</rb><rt>hyiang</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>埃</rb><rt>Yiæ</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>城</rb><rt>dzing</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其拉个</rb><rt>gyi-lah-go</rt><rb>王</rb><rt>wông</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>做过</rb><rt>tso-ko</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>还有</rb><rt>wa-yiu</rt><rb>听闻</rb><rt>tʽing-meng</rt><rb>庉</rb><rt>deng</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>基遍</u>个</rb><rt>Kyi-pin-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>立</rb><rt>lih</rt><rb>和约</rb><rt>ʽo-iah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>庉</rb><rt>deng</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>中央</rb><rt>cong-nyiang</rt></ruby>，<sup>2</sup> <ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>大样</rb><rt>do-nyiang</rt><rb>怕惧</rb><rt>pʽô-gyü</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>因为</rb><rt>ing-we</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>基遍</rb><rt>Kyi-pin</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>一个</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>大</rb><rt>do</rt><rb>城子</rb><rt>dzing-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>像</rb><rt>ziang</rt><rb>京城</rb><rt>kying-dzing</rt><rb>一样</rb><rt>ih-yiang</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>比</rb><rt>pi</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>埃</rb><rt>Yiæ</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>城</rb><rt>dzing</rt><rb>大</rb><rt>do</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其个</rb><rt>gyi-go</rt><rb>百姓</rb><rt>pah-sing</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>好佬</rb><rt>hao-lao</rt></ruby>。<sup>3</sup> <ruby><rb>所以</rb><rt>Sô-yi</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶路撒冷</rb><rt>Yiæ-lu-sah-leng</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>王</rb><rt>wông</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>亚多尼洗特</rb><rt>Üô-to-nyi-si-deh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>差</rb><rt>tsʽa</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>希伯仑</rb><rt>Hyi-pah-leng</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>王</rb><rt>wông</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>何咸</rb><rt>ʽO-yin</rt></ruby></u>、<u><ruby><rb>耶末</rb><rt>Yiæ-meh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>王</rb><rt>wông</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>庇兰</rb><rt>Pi-læn</rt></ruby></u>、<u><ruby><rb>拉吉</rb><rt>Lah-kyih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>王</rb><rt>wông</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶非亚</rb><rt>Yiæ-fi-üô</rt></ruby></u>、<u><ruby><rb>厄伦</rb><rt>Ah-leng</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>王</rb><rt>wông</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>底璧</rb><rt>Ti-pih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：<sup>4</sup> 「<ruby><rb>求</rb><rt>Gyiu</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>帮助</rb><rt>pông-dzu</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>抄灭</rb><rt>tsʽao-mih</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>基遍</rb><rt>Kyi-pin</rt></ruby></u>；<ruby><rb>因为</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>约书亚</rb><rt>Iah-shü-üô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>子孙</rb><rt>ts-seng</rt><rb>立</rb><rt>lih</rt><rb>和约</rb><rt>ʽo-iah</rt></ruby>。」<sup>5</sup> <ruby><rb>所以</rb><rt>Sô-yi</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>亚摩利</rb><rt>Üô-mo-li</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>五个</rb><rt>ng-go</rt><rb>王</rb><rt>wông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶路撒冷</rb><rt>Yiæ-lu-sah-leng</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>王</rb><rt>wông</rt></ruby>、<u><ruby><rb>希伯仑</rb><rt>Hyi-pah-leng</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>王</rb><rt>wông</rt></ruby>、<u><ruby><rb>耶末</rb><rt>Yiæ-meh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>王</rb><rt>wông</rt></ruby>、<u><ruby><rb>拉吉</rb><rt>Lah-kyih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>王</rb><rt>wông</rt></ruby>、<u><ruby><rb>厄伦</rb><rt>Ah-leng</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>王</rb><rt>wông</rt><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>聚集</rb><rt>jü-jih</rt><rb>上去</rb><rt>zông-kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>众</rb><rt>cong</rt><rb>兵将</rb><rt>ping-tsiang</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>基遍</rb><rt>Kyi-pin</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>面前</rb><rt>min-zin</rt><rb>搭营</rb><rt>tah-ying</rt><rb>攻打</rb><rt>kong-tang</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>。<sup>6</sup> <ruby><rb><u>基遍</u>个</rb><rt>Kyi-pin-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>差</rb><rt>tsʽa</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>约书亚</rb><rt>Iah-shü-üô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>吉甲</rb><rt>Kyih-kyiah</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>营寨</rb><rt>ying-za</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>弗要</rb><rt>Feh-iao</rt><rb>束勒</rb><rt>soh-leh</rt><rb>手</rb><rt>siu</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>管</rb><rt>kwun</rt><rb>尔个</rb><rt>ng-go</rt><rb>奴仆</rb><rt>nu-boh</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>快快</rb><rt>kwʽa-kwʽa</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>救</rb><rt>kyiu</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>帮助</rb><rt>pông-dzu</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>因为</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>庉</rb><rt>deng</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>山里</rb><rt>sæn-li</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>亚摩利</rb><rt>Üô-mo-li</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>王</rb><rt>wông</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>聚集</rb><rt>jü-jih</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>攻打</rb><rt>kong-tang</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt></ruby>。」<sup>7</sup> <ruby><rb>所以</rb><rt>Sô-yi</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>约书亚</rb><rt>Iah-shü-üô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>自</rb><rt>zi</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>拢总</rb><rt>long-tsong</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>打仗个</rb><rt>tang-tsiang-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>还有</rb><rt>wa-yiu</rt><rb>拢总</rb><rt>long-tsong</rt><rb>好佬</rb><rt>hao-lao</rt><rb>从</rb><rt>dzong</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>吉甲</rb><rt>Kyih-kyiah</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>上去</rb><rt>zông-kyʽi</rt></ruby>。<sup>8</sup> <ruby><rb>耶和华</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>约书亚</rb><rt>Iah-shü-üô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>弗要</rb><rt>Feh-iao</rt><rb>怕</rb><rt>pʽô</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>因为</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>已经</rb><rt>yi-kying</rt><rb>把</rb><rt>pô</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>交代</rb><rt>kao-dæ</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>手里</rb><rt>siu-li</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>呒得</rb><rt>m-neh</rt><rb>一个</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>面前</rb><rt>min-zin</rt><rb>立勒住</rb><rt>lih-leh-djü</rt></ruby>。」<sup>9</sup> <ruby><rb>所以</rb><rt>Sô-yi</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>约书亚</rb><rt>Iah-shü-üô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>突然</rb><rt>deh-jün</rt><rb>攻打</rb><rt>kong-tang</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>因为</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>从</rb><rt>dzong</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>吉甲</rb><rt>Kyih-kyiah</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>上来</rb><rt>zông-læ</rt><rb>全夜</rb><rt>djün-yia</rt><rb>来的</rb><rt>læ-tih</rt><rb>走</rb><rt>tseo</rt></ruby>。<sup>10</sup> <ruby><rb>耶和华</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>打败</rb><rt>tang-ba</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>面前</rb><rt>min-zin</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>基遍</rb><rt>Kyi-pin</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>大样</rb><rt>do-nyiang</rt><rb>杀掉</rb><rt>sah-diao</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>追</rb><rt>tse</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>上</rb><rt>zông</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>伯和仑</rb><rt>Pah-ʽo-leng</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>路</rb><rt>lu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>一直</rb><rt>ih-dzih</rt><rb>杀</rb><rt>sah</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>亚西加</rb><rt>Üô-si-kyüô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>玛基大</rb><rt>Mô-kyi-da</rt></ruby></u>。<sup>11</sup> <ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>面前</rb><rt>min-zin</rt><rb>逃走</rb><rt>dao-tseo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>落</rb><rt>loh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>伯和仑</rb><rt>Pah-ʽo-leng</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>时候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt><rb>耶和华</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>从</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>天</rb><rt>tʽin</rt><rb>降落</rb><rt>kông-loh</rt><rb>大大</rb><rt>do-do</rt><rb>雹子</rb><rt>boh-ts</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>身上</rb><rt>sing-zông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>一直</rb><rt>ih-dzih</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>亚西加</rb><rt>Üô-si-kyüô</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>死了</rb><rt>si-de</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>被</rb><rt>be</rt><rb>雹子</rb><rt>boh-ts</rt><rb>打煞个</rb><rt>tang-sah-go</rt><rb>比</rb><rt>pi</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>刀</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>杀个</rb><rt>sah-go</rt><rb>更加</rb><rt>keng-kô</rt><rb>多</rb><rt>to</rt></ruby>。

<sup>12</sup> <ruby><rb>当</rb><rt>Tông</rt><rb>耶和华</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>将</rb><rt>tsiang</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>亚摩利</rb><rt>Üô-mo-li</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>交付</rb><rt>kao-fu</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>子孙</rb><rt>ts-seng</rt><rb>箇日</rb><rt>keh-nyih</rt></ruby>，<u><ruby><rb>约书亚</rb><rt>Iah-shü-üô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>向</rb><rt>hyiang</rt><rb>耶和华</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>讲</rb><rt>kông</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>眼前</rb><rt>ngæn-zin</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>日头</rb><rt>Nyih-deo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>停住</rb><rt>ding-djü</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>基遍</rb><rt>Kyi-pin</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>上头</rb><rt>zông-deo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>月亮</rb><rt>yüih-liang</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>亚耶仑</u>个</rb><rt>Üô-yiæ-leng-go</rt><rb>山岙</rb><rt>sæn-ao</rt></ruby>。」<sup>13</sup> <ruby><rb>箇遭</rb><rt>Keh-tsao</rt><rb>日头</rb><rt>nyih-deo</rt><rb>停住</rb><rt>ding-djü</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>月亮</rb><rt>yüih-liang</rt><rb>庉落</rb><rt>deng-loh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>一直</rb><rt>ih-dzih</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>百姓</rb><rt>pah-sing</rt><rb>向</rb><rt>hyiang</rt><rb>仇敌</rb><rt>dziu-dih</rt><rb>报仇</rb><rt>pao-dziu</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>箇个</rb><rt>Keh-go</rt><rb>事干</rb><rt>z-ken</rt><rb>弗是</rb><rt>feh-z</rt><rb>写</rb><rt>sia</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>耶述</u>个</rb><rt>Yiæ-jih-go</rt><rb>书里</rb><rt>shü-li</rt><rb>吗</rb><rt>ma</rt></ruby>？<ruby><rb>日头</rb><rt>Nyih-deo</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>天</rb><rt>tʽin</rt><rb>当中</rb><rt>tông-cong</rt><rb>停住</rb><rt>ding-djü</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>快快</rb><rt>kwʽa-kwʽa</rt><rb>落山</rb><rt>loh-sæn</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>大约</rb><rt>da-iah</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>一</rb><rt>ih</rt><rb>长</rb><rt>dziang</rt><rb>日</rb><rt>nyih</rt></ruby>。<sup>14</sup> <ruby><rb>来</rb><rt>Læ</rt><rb>箇日</rb><rt>keh-nyih</rt><rb>之前</rb><rt>ts-zin</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇日</rb><rt>keh-nyih</rt><rb>之后</rb><rt>ts-ʽeo</rt><rb>呒有</rb><rt>m-yiu</rt><rb>像</rb><rt>ziang</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>耶和华</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>听</rb><rt>tʽing</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>说话</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>因为</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>耶和华</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>为了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>打仗</rb><rt>tang-tsiang</rt></ruby>。

<sup>15</sup> <u><ruby><rb>约书亚</rb><rt>Iah-shü-üô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>众人</rb><rt>cong-nying</rt><rb>归到</rb><rt>kyü-tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>吉甲</rb><rt>Kyih-kyiah</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>营寨</rb><rt>ying-za</rt></ruby>。

<sup>16</sup> <ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>Keh</rt><rb>五个</rb><rt>ng-go</rt><rb>王</rb><rt>wông</rt><rb>逃走</rb><rt>dao-tseo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>玛基大</rb><rt>Mô-kyi-da</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>幽</rb><rt>iu</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>山洞</rb><rt>sæn-dong</rt><rb>里</rb><rt>li</rt></ruby>。<sup>17</sup> <ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>Yiu</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>通知</rb><rt>tʽong-cü</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>约书亚</rb><rt>Iah-shü-üô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>五个</rb><rt>Ng-go</rt><rb>王</rb><rt>wông</rt><rb>寻着了</rb><rt>zing-djoh-de</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>幽</rb><rt>iu</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>玛基大</u>个</rb><rt>Mô-kyi-da-go</rt><rb>山洞里</rb><rt>sæn-dong-li</rt></ruby>。」<sup>18</sup> <u><ruby><rb>约书亚</rb><rt>Iah-shü-üô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>翻</rb><rt>Fæn</rt><rb>大</rb><rt>do</rt><rb>石头</rb><rt>zah-deo</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>岩洞</rb><rt>ngæn-dong</rt><rb>口里</rb><rt>kʽeo-li</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>派</rb><rt>pʽa</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>箇头</rb><rt>keh-deo</rt><rb>管守</rb><rt>kwun-siu</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt></ruby>；<sup>19</sup> <ruby><rb>但是</rb><rt>dæn-z</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>自</rb><rt>zi</rt><rb>弗可</rb><rt>feh-kʽo</rt><rb>耽搁</rb><rt>tæn-koh</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>追赶</rb><rt>tse-ken</rt><rb>尔个</rb><rt>ng-go</rt><rb>仇敌</rb><rt>dziu-dih</rt><rb>打</rb><rt>tang</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>后队</rb><rt>ʽeo-de</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>弗要</rb><rt>feh-iao</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>走进</rb><rt>tseo-tsing</rt><rb>自</rb><rt>zi</rt><rb>城子</rb><rt>dzing-ts</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>因为</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>耶和华</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>尔拉个</rb><rt>ng-lah-go</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>已经</rb><rt>yi-kying</rt><rb>把</rb><rt>pô</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>交代</rb><rt>kao-dæ</rt><rb>尔个</rb><rt>ng-go</rt><rb>手里</rb><rt>siu-li</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>20</sup> <u><ruby><rb>约书亚</rb><rt>Iah-shü-üô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>子孙</rb><rt>ts-seng</rt><rb>大样</rb><rt>do-nyiang</rt><rb>打败</rb><rt>tang-ba</rt><rb>杀掉</rb><rt>sah-diao</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>一直</rb><rt>ih-dzih</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>灭完</rb><rt>mih-wun</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>剩落个</rb><rt>dzing-loh-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>已经</rb><rt>yi-kying</rt><rb>走进</rb><rt>tseo-tsing</rt><rb>坚固</rb><rt>kyin-kwu</rt><rb>城子</rb><rt>dzing-ts</rt><rb>之后</rb><rt>ts-ʽeo</rt></ruby>。<sup>21</sup> <ruby><rb>箇遭</rb><rt>keh-tsao</rt><rb>众</rb><rt>cong</rt><rb>百姓</rb><rt>pah-sing</rt><rb>安安耽耽</rb><rt>en-en-tæn-tæn</rt><rb>归到</rb><rt>kyü-tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>玛基大</rb><rt>Mô-kyi-da</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>营寨</rb><rt>ying-za</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>约书亚</rb><rt>Iah-shü-üô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>呒有</rb><rt>m-yiu</rt><rb>一个</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>敢</rb><rt>ken</rt><rb>向着</rb><rt>hyiang-djoh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>子孙</rb><rt>ts-seng</rt><rb>摇</rb><rt>yiao</rt><rb>舌头个</rb><rt>zih-deo-go</rt></ruby>。<sup>22</sup> <ruby><rb>箇遭</rb><rt>Keh-tsao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>约书亚</rb><rt>Iah-shü-üô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>好</rb><rt>Hao</rt><rb>开</rb><rt>kʽæ</rt><rb>山岩洞</rb><rt>sæn-ngæn-dong</rt><rb>口</rb><rt>kʽeo</rt><rb>带</rb><rt>ta</rt><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>五个</rb><rt>ng-go</rt><rb>王</rb><rt>wông</rt><rb>从</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>山岩洞</rb><rt>sæn-ngæn-dong</rt><rb>出来</rb><rt>cʽih-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt></ruby>。」<sup>23</sup> <ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>带</rb><rt>ta</rt><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>五个</rb><rt>ng-go</rt><rb>王</rb><rt>wông</rt><rb>出来</rb><rt>cʽih-læ</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶路撒冷</rb><rt>Yiæ-lu-sah-leng</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>王</rb><rt>wông</rt></ruby>、<u><ruby><rb>希伯仑</rb><rt>Hyi-pah-leng</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>王</rb><rt>wông</rt></ruby>、<u><ruby><rb>耶末</rb><rt>Yiæ-meh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>王</rb><rt>wông</rt></ruby>、<u><ruby><rb>拉吉</rb><rt>Lah-kyih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>王</rb><rt>wông</rt></ruby>、<u><ruby><rb>厄伦</rb><rt>Ah-leng</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>王</rb><rt>wông</rt></ruby>。<sup>24</sup> <ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt><rb>带</rb><rt>ta</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>王</rb><rt>wông</rt><rb>出来</rb><rt>cʽih-læ</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>约书亚</rb><rt>Iah-shü-üô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt><rb>时候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt></ruby>，<u><ruby><rb>约书亚</rb><rt>Iah-shü-üô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>召拢</rb><rt>dziao-long</rt><rb>众</rb><rt>cong</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>同队</rb><rt>dong-de</rt><rb>上阵</rb><rt>zông-dzing</rt><rb>打仗</rb><rt>tang-tsiang</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>带领</rb><rt>ta-ling</rt><rb>官</rb><rt>kwun</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>走拢来</rb><rt>Tseo-long-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>尔个</rb><rt>ng-go</rt><rb>脚</rb><rt>kyiah</rt><rb>踏</rb><rt>dah</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>王个</rb><rt>wông-go</rt><rb>头颈上</rb><rt>deo-kying-zông</rt></ruby>。」<ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>走拢</rb><rt>tseo-long</rt><rb>脚</rb><rt>kyiah</rt><rb>踏</rb><rt>dah</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>头颈</rb><rt>deo-kying</rt><rb>上</rb><rt>zông</rt></ruby>。<sup>25</sup> <u><ruby><rb>约书亚</rb><rt>Iah-shü-üô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>弗要</rb><rt>Feh-iao</rt><rb>怕</rb><rt>pʽô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>弗要</rb><rt>feh-iao</rt><rb>胆小</rb><rt>tæn-siao</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>刚强</rb><rt>kông-gyiang</rt><rb>放</rb><rt>fông</rt><rb>大</rb><rt>do</rt><rb>胆子</rb><rt>tæn-ts</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>因为</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>耶和华</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>必定</rb><rt>pih-ding</rt><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>向</rb><rt>hyiang</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>凡百</rb><rt>væn-pah</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>攻打个</rb><rt>kong-tang-go</rt><rb>仇敌</rb><rt>dziu-dih</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt></ruby>。」<sup>26</sup> <ruby><rb>后头</rb><rt>ʽEo-deo</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>约书亚</rb><rt>Iah-shü-üô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>打煞</rb><rt>tang-sah</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>将</rb><rt>tsiang</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>挂</rb><rt>kwô</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>五</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>株</rb><rt>cü</rt><rb>树上</rb><rt>jü-zông</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>挂</rb><rt>kwô</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>树上</rb><rt>jü-zông</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>夜快</rb><rt>yia-kwʽa</rt></ruby>。<sup>27</sup> <ruby><rb>到</rb><rt>Tao</rt><rb>日头</rb><rt>nyih-deo</rt><rb>落山</rb><rt>loh-sæn</rt><rb>时候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt></ruby>，<u><ruby><rb>约书亚</rb><rt>Iah-shü-üô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>吩咐</rb><rt>feng-fu</rt><rb>从</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>树上</rb><rt>jü-zông</rt><rb>驮</rb><rt>do</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>落来</rb><rt>loh-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>揞</rb><rt>kʽang</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>幽个</rb><rt>iu-go</rt><rb>洞眼</rb><rt>dong-ngæn</rt><rb>里</rb><rt>li</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>安</rb><rt>en</rt><rb>大大</rb><rt>do-do</rt><rb>石头</rb><rt>zah-deo</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>洞眼</rb><rt>dong-ngæn</rt><rb>口</rb><rt>kʽeo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>如今</rb><rt>jü-kying</rt><rb>还</rb><rt>wa</rt><rb>来间</rb><rt>læ-kæn</rt></ruby>。

<sup>28</sup> <u><ruby><rb>约书亚</rb><rt>Iah-shü-üô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>当日</rb><rt>tông-nyih</rt><rb>夺</rb><rt>deh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>玛基大</rb><rt>Mô-kyi-da</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>刀口</rb><rt>tao-kʽeo</rt><rb>打</rb><rt>tang</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>杀掉</rb><rt>sah-diao</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>王</rb><rt>wông</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>把</rb><rt>pô</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>一切个</rb><rt>ih-tsʽih-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>除灭</rb><rt>djü-mih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>留落</rb><rt>liu-loh</rt><rb>一个</rb><rt>ih-go</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>像</rb><rt>ziang</rt><rb>前头</rb><rt>zin-deo</rt><rb>待</rb><rt>dæ</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶利哥</rb><rt>Yiæ-li-ko</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>王</rb><rt>wông</rt><rb>待</rb><rt>dæ</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>玛基大</rb><rt>Mô-kyi-da</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>王</rb><rt>wông</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt></ruby>。

<sup>29</sup> <u><ruby><rb>约书亚</rb><rt>Iah-shü-üô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>众人</rb><rt>cong-nying</rt><rb>从</rb><rt>dzong</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>玛基大</rb><rt>Mô-kyi-da</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>走到</rb><rt>tseo-tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>立拿</rb><rt>Lih-nô</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>攻打</rb><rt>kong-tang</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>立拿</rb><rt>Lih-nô</rt></ruby></u>；<sup>30</sup> <ruby><rb>耶和华</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>把</rb><rt>pô</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>立拿</rb><rt>Lih-nô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其个</rb><rt>gyi-go</rt><rb>王</rb><rt>wông</rt><rb>交代</rb><rt>kao-dæ</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>手里</rb><rt>siu-li</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>夺</rb><rt>deh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>刀口</rb><rt>tao-kʽeo</rt><rb>杀</rb><rt>sah</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>里向</rb><rt>li-hyiang</rt><rb>一切个</rb><rt>ih-tsʽih-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>留落</rb><rt>liu-loh</rt><rb>一个</rb><rt>ih-go</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>照</rb><rt>tsiao</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>向</rb><rt>hyiang</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶利哥</rb><rt>Yiæ-li-ko</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>王</rb><rt>wông</rt><rb>凡百</rb><rt>væn-pah</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>做个</rb><rt>tso-go</rt><rb>向</rb><rt>hyiang</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>立拿</rb><rt>Lih-nô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>王</rb><rt>wông</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt></ruby>。

<sup>31</sup> <u><ruby><rb>约书亚</rb><rt>Iah-shü-üô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>众人</rb><rt>cong-nying</rt><rb>从</rb><rt>dzong</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>立拿</rb><rt>Lih-nô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>走到</rb><rt>tseo-tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>拉吉</rb><rt>Lah-kyih</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>对</rb><rt>te</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>拉吉</rb><rt>Lah-kyih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>搭营</rb><rt>tah-ying</rt><rb>攻打</rb><rt>kong-tang</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>；<sup>32</sup> <ruby><rb>耶和华</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>把</rb><rt>pô</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>拉吉</rb><rt>Lah-kyih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>交代</rb><rt>kao-dæ</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>以色列</u>个</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih-go</rt><rb>手</rb><rt>siu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>第二</rb><rt>di-nyi</rt><rb>日</rb><rt>nyih</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>夺着</rb><rt>deh-djoh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>刀口</rb><rt>tao-kʽeo</rt><rb>杀</rb><rt>sah</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>里向</rb><rt>li-hyiang</rt><rb>一切个</rb><rt>ih-tsʽih-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>照</rb><rt>tsiao</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>向</rb><rt>hyiang</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>立拿</rb><rt>Lih-nô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>凡百</rb><rt>væn-pah</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>一样</rb><rt>ih-yiang</rt></ruby>。

<sup>33</sup> <ruby><rb>箇遭</rb><rt>Keh-tsao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>基色</rb><rt>Kyi-seh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>王</rb><rt>wông</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>何兰</rb><rt>ʽO-læn</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>上来</rb><rt>zông-læ</rt><rb>帮助</rb><rt>pông-dzu</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>拉吉</rb><rt>Lah-kyih</rt></ruby></u>；<u><ruby><rb>约书亚</rb><rt>Iah-shü-üô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>打</rb><rt>tang</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其个</rb><rt>gyi-go</rt><rb>百姓</rb><rt>pah-sing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>剩落</rb><rt>dzing-loh</rt><rb>一个</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>。

<sup>34</sup> <u><ruby><rb>约书亚</rb><rt>Iah-shü-üô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>众人</rb><rt>cong-nying</rt><rb>从</rb><rt>dzong</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>拉吉</rb><rt>Lah-kyih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>走到</rb><rt>tseo-tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>厄伦</rb><rt>Ah-leng</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>对着</rb><rt>te-djoh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>厄伦</rb><rt>Ah-leng</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>搭营</rb><rt>tah-ying</rt><rb>攻打</rb><rt>kong-tang</rt></ruby>；<sup>35</sup> <ruby><rb>当日</rb><rt>tông-nyih</rt><rb>夺着</rb><rt>deh-djoh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>刀口</rb><rt>tao-kʽeo</rt><rb>杀</rb><rt>sah</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>除灭</rb><rt>djü-mih</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>里向</rb><rt>li-hyiang</rt><rb>一切</rb><rt>ih-tsʽih</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>照</rb><rt>tsiao</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>向</rb><rt>hyiang</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>拉吉</rb><rt>Lah-kyih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>一样</rb><rt>ih-yiang</rt></ruby>。

<sup>36</sup> <u><ruby><rb>约书亚</rb><rt>Iah-shü-üô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>众人</rb><rt>cong-nying</rt><rb>从</rb><rt>dzong</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>厄伦</rb><rt>Ah-leng</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>上去</rb><rt>zông-kyʽi</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>希伯仑</rb><rt>Hyi-pah-leng</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>攻打</rb><rt>kong-tang</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>；<sup>37</sup> <ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>夺着</rb><rt>deh-djoh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>刀口</rb><rt>tao-kʽeo</rt><rb>打</rb><rt>tang</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>王</rb><rt>wông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>众</rb><rt>cong</rt><rb>臣子</rb><rt>dzing-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>连</rb><rt>lin</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>一切个</rb><rt>ih-tsʽih-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>留落</rb><rt>liu-loh</rt><rb>一个</rb><rt>ih-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>照</rb><rt>tsiao</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>向</rb><rt>hyiang</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>厄伦</rb><rt>Ah-leng</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>一概</rb><rt>ih-kæ</rt><rb>除灭</rb><rt>djü-mih</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>里向</rb><rt>li-hyiang</rt><rb>凡百</rb><rt>væn-pah</rt><rb>庉的个</rb><rt>deng-tih-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>。

<sup>38</sup> <u><ruby><rb>约书亚</rb><rt>Iah-shü-üô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>众人</rb><rt>cong-nying</rt><rb>走转</rb><rt>tseo-cün</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>底璧</rb><rt>Ti-pih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>攻打</rb><rt>kong-tang</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>：<sup>39</sup> <ruby><rb>夺着</rb><rt>deh-djoh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其个</rb><rt>gyi-go</rt><rb>王</rb><rt>wông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>连</rb><rt>lin</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>众</rb><rt>cong</rt><rb>臣子</rb><rt>dzing-ts</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>刀口</rb><rt>tao-kʽeo</rt><rb>打</rb><rt>tang</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>有个</rb><rt>yiu-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>一概</rb><rt>ih-kæ</rt><rb>除灭</rb><rt>djü-mih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>留落</rb><rt>liu-loh</rt><rb>一个</rb><rt>ih-go</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>像</rb><rt>ziang</rt><rb>从前</rb><rt>dzong-zin</rt><rb>待</rb><rt>dæ</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>希伯仑</rb><rt>Hyi-pah-leng</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>待</rb><rt>dæ</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>底璧</rb><rt>Ti-pih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其个</rb><rt>gyi-go</rt><rb>王</rb><rt>wông</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>像</rb><rt>ziang</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>待</rb><rt>dæ</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>立拿</rb><rt>Lih-nô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其个</rb><rt>gyi-go</rt><rb>王</rb><rt>wông</rt><rb>一样</rb><rt>ih-yiang</rt></ruby>。

<sup>40</sup> <ruby><rb>是介</rb><rt>Z-ka</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>约书亚</rb><rt>Iah-shü-üô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>攻打</rb><rt>kong-tang</rt><rb>全地</rb><rt>djün-di</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>山地</rb><rt>sæn-di</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>南边地</rb><rt>nen-pin-di</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>平地</rb><rt>bing-di</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>山岙</rb><rt>sæn-ao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>拢总</rb><rt>long-tsong</rt><rb>王</rb><rt>wông</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>留落</rb><rt>liu-loh</rt><rb>一个</rb><rt>ih-go</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>拢总</rb><rt>long-tsong</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>生气个</rb><rt>seng-kyʽi-go</rt><rb>一概</rb><rt>ih-kæ</rt><rb>除灭</rb><rt>djü-mih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>照</rb><rt>tsiao</rt><rb>耶和华</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>吩咐个</rb><rt>feng-fu-go</rt></ruby>。<sup>41</sup> <u><ruby><rb>约书亚</rb><rt>Iah-shü-üô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>打</rb><rt>tang</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>从</rb><rt>dzong</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>迦铁巴尼亚</rb><rt>Kyüô-tʽih-pô-nyi-üô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>一直</rb><rt>ih-dzih</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>迦萨</rb><rt>Kyüô-sah</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>坷山</rb><rt>Kʽo-sæn</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>全地</rb><rt>djün-di</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>直到</rb><rt>dzih-tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>基遍</rb><rt>Kyi-pin</rt></ruby></u>。<sup>42</sup> <ruby><rb>箇星</rb><rt>Keh-sing</rt><rb>王</rb><rt>wông</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其拉个</rb><rt>gyi-lah-go</rt><rb>地</rb><rt>di</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>约书亚</rb><rt>Iah-shü-üô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>时候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt><rb>夺着</rb><rt>deh-djoh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>因为</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>耶和华</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>以色列</u>个</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih-go</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>为了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>打仗</rb><rt>tang-tsiang</rt></ruby>。<sup>43</sup> <u><ruby><rb>约书亚</rb><rt>Iah-shü-üô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>众人</rb><rt>cong-nying</rt><rb>归到</rb><rt>kyü-tao</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>吉甲</u>个</rb><rt>Kyih-kyiah-go</rt><rb>营寨</rb><rt>ying-za</rt></ruby>。

---

### Is. XI.

<u><ruby><rb>夏朔</rb><rt>YÜÔ-SOH</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>王</rb><rt>wông</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶宾</rb><rt>Yiæ-ping</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>一</rb><rt>ih</rt><rb>听闻</rb><rt>tʽing-meng</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>差</rb><rt>tsʽa</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>见</rb><rt>kyin</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>玛顿</rb><rt>Mô-teng</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>王</rb><rt>wông</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>约八</rb><rt>Iah-pah</rt></ruby></u>、<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>伸仑</rb><rt>Sing-leng</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>王</rb><rt>wông</rt></ruby>、<u><ruby><rb>押煞</rb><rt>Iah-sah</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>王</rb><rt>wông</rt></ruby>，<sup>2</sup> <ruby><rb>还有</rb><rt>wa-yiu</rt><rb>庉</rb><rt>deng</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>北边</rb><rt>poh-pin</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>山地</rb><rt>sæn-di</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>基尼烈</rb><rt>Kyi-nyi-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>南边个</rb><rt>nen-pin-go</rt><rb>平垟</rb><rt>bing-yiang</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>山岙</rb><rt>sæn-ao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>西边个</rb><rt>si-pin-go</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>多珥</rb><rt>To-r</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>高地</rb><rt>kao-di</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>王</rb><rt>wông</rt></ruby>，<sup>3</sup> <ruby><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>东边</rb><rt>tong-pin</rt><rb>西边个</rb><rt>si-pin-go</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>迦南</rb><rt>Kyüô-nen</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>、<u><ruby><rb>亚摩利</rb><rt>Üô-mo-li</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>、<u><ruby><rb>赫</rb><rt>Heh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>、<u><ruby><rb>比利洗</rb><rt>Pi-li-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>庉</rb><rt>deng</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>山里个</rb><rt>sæn-li-go</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶布士</rb><rt>Yiæ-pu-z</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>黑门</rb><rt>Heh-meng</rt></ruby></u><sup>\[1\]</sup><ruby><rb>山脚下</rb><rt>sæn-kyiah-ʽô</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>蜜是巴</rb><rt>Mih-z-pô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>地</rb><rt>di</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>希未</rb><rt>Hyi-vi</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>。<sup>4</sup> <ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>走出去</rb><rt>tseo-cʽih-kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>众</rb><rt>cong</rt><rb>军长</rb><rt>kyüing-tsiang</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>像</rb><rt>ziang</rt><rb>海边个</rb><rt>hæ-pin-go</rt><rb>沙泥</rb><rt>sô-nyi</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>多</rb><rt>to</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>马</rb><rt>mô</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>车子</rb><rt>tsʽô-ts</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>顶</rb><rt>ting</rt><rb>多</rb><rt>to</rt></ruby>。<sup>5</sup> <ruby><rb>箇星</rb><rt>Keh-sing</rt><rb>王</rb><rt>wông</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>聚集</rb><rt>jü-jih</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>米伦</rb><rt>Mi-leng</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>水</rb><rt>shü</rt><rb>边沿</rb><rt>pin-yin</rt><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>搭营</rb><rt>tah-ying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>打仗</rb><rt>tang-tsiang</rt></ruby>。<sup>6</sup> <ruby><rb>耶和华</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>约书亚</rb><rt>Iah-shü-üô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>弗要</rb><rt>Feh-iao</rt><rb>为了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>怕</rb><rt>pʽô</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>明朝</rb><rt>ming-tsiao</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>时候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>把</rb><rt>pô</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>拢总</rb><rt>long-tsong</rt><rb>交付</rb><rt>kao-fu</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>杀掉</rb><rt>sah-diao</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>割断</rb><rt>keh-dön</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>马个</rb><rt>mô-go</rt><rb>脚筋</rb><rt>kyiah-kying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>火</rb><rt>ho</rt><rb>𤈦掉</rb><rt>me-diao</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>车子</rb><rt>tsʽô-ts</rt></ruby>。」<sup>7</sup> <u><ruby><rb>约书亚</rb><rt>Iah-shü-üô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>拢总</rb><rt>long-tsong</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>打仗个</rb><rt>tang-tsiang-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>突然</rb><rt>deh-jün</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>攻打</rb><rt>kong-tang</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>米伦</rb><rt>Mi-leng</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>水</rb><rt>shü</rt><rb>边沿</rb><rt>pin-yin</rt></ruby>。<sup>8</sup> <ruby><rb>耶和华</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>把</rb><rt>pô</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>交付</rb><rt>kao-fu</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>手里</rb><rt>siu-li</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>打</rb><rt>tang</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>追</rb><rt>tse</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>大</rb><rt>do</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>西顿</rb><rt>Si-teng</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>蜜利弗玛音</rb><rt>Mih-li-feh-mô-ing</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>还有</rb><rt>wa-yiu</rt><rb>向</rb><rt>hyiang</rt><rb>东边</rb><rt>tong-pin</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>蜜是巴</rb><rt>Mih-z-pô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>岙</rb><rt>ao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>杀掉</rb><rt>sah-diao</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>留落</rb><rt>liu-loh</rt><rb>一个</rb><rt>ih-go</rt></ruby>。<sup>9</sup> <u><ruby><rb>约书亚</rb><rt>Iah-shü-üô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>待</rb><rt>dæ</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>照</rb><rt>tsiao</rt><rb>耶和华</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>吩咐</rb><rt>feng-fu</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>割断</rb><rt>keh-dön</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>马个</rb><rt>mô-go</rt><rb>脚筋</rb><rt>kyiah-kying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>火</rb><rt>ho</rt><rb>𤈦掉</rb><rt>me-diao</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>车子</rb><rt>tsʽô-ts</rt></ruby>。

<sup>\[1\] 此处原书错印为 Hah-meng，后文均为 Heh-meng</sup>

<sup>10</sup> <ruby><rb>介</rb><rt>Ka</rt><rb>时候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>约书亚</rb><rt>Iah-shü-üô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>倒</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>走转</rb><rt>tseo-cün</rt><rb>夺着</rb><rt>deh-djoh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>夏朔</rb><rt>Yüô-soh</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>刀</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>杀掉</rb><rt>sah-diao</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>王</rb><rt>wông</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>因为</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>从前</rb><rt>dzong-zin</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>夏朔</rb><rt>Yüô-soh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>国份个</rb><rt>koh-veng-go</rt><rb>头</rb><rt>deo</rt></ruby>。<sup>11</sup> <ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>刀口</rb><rt>tao-kʽeo</rt><rb>杀</rb><rt>sah</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>里向</rb><rt>li-hyiang</rt><rb>一切个</rb><rt>ih-tsʽih-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>一概</rb><rt>ih-kæ</rt><rb>除灭</rb><rt>djü-mih</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>留落</rb><rt>liu-loh</rt><rb>一个</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>生气个</rb><rt>seng-kyʽi-go</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>火</rb><rt>ho</rt><rb>烧掉</rb><rt>siao-diao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>夏朔</rb><rt>Yüô-soh</rt></ruby></u>。<sup>12</sup> <ruby><rb>箇星</rb><rt>Keh-sing</rt><rb>王</rb><rt>wông</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>众</rb><rt>cong</rt><rb>臣子</rb><rt>dzing-ts</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>约书亚</rb><rt>Iah-shü-üô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>得着</rb><rt>teh-djoh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>刀口</rb><rt>tao-kʽeo</rt><rb>杀掉</rb><rt>sah-diao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>一概</rb><rt>ih-kæ</rt><rb>除灭</rb><rt>djü-mih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>照</rb><rt>tsiao</rt><rb>耶和华</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>奴仆</rb><rt>nu-boh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>吩咐个</rb><rt>feng-fu-go</rt></ruby>。<sup>13</sup> <ruby><rb>但是</rb><rt>Dæn-z</rt><rb>建造</rb><rt>kyin-zao</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>山上</rb><rt>sæn-zông</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>城子</rb><rt>dzing-ts</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>呒得</rb><rt>m-neh</rt><rb>烧掉</rb><rt>siao-diao</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>只有</rb><rt>tsih-yiu</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>夏朔</rb><rt>Yüô-soh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>一座</rb><rt>ih-zo</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>约书亚</rb><rt>Iah-shü-üô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>烧掉个</rb><rt>siao-diao-go</rt></ruby>。<sup>14</sup> <ruby><rb>箇星</rb><rt>Keh-sing</rt><rb>城子个</rb><rt>dzing-ts-go</rt><rb>财物</rb><rt>dzæ-veh</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>牲口</rb><rt>sang-kʽeo</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>子孙</rb><rt>ts-seng</rt><rb>自</rb><rt>zi</rt><rb>得着</rb><rt>teh-djoh</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>但是</rb><rt>dæn-z</rt><rb>拢总</rb><rt>long-tsong</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>刀口</rb><rt>tao-kʽeo</rt><rb>杀掉</rb><rt>sah-diao</rt><rb>直到</rb><rt>dzih-tao</rt><rb>灭完</rb><rt>mih-wun</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>留落</rb><rt>liu-loh</rt><rb>一个</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>生气个</rb><rt>seng-kyʽi-go</rt></ruby>。<sup>15</sup> <ruby><rb>正像</rb><rt>Tsing-ziang</rt><rb>耶和华</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>吩咐</rb><rt>feng-fu</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>奴仆</rb><rt>nu-boh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>吩咐</rb><rt>feng-fu</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>约书亚</rb><rt>Iah-shü-üô</rt></ruby></u>；<u><ruby><rb>约书亚</rb><rt>Iah-shü-üô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>依勒</rb><rt>i-leh</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt></ruby>：<ruby><rb>耶和华</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>吩咐</rb><rt>feng-fu</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>摩西</u>个</rb><rt>Mo-si-go</rt><rb>呒有</rb><rt>m-yiu</rt><rb>一样</rb><rt>ih-yiang</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>约书亚</rb><rt>Iah-shü-üô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>遵依</rb><rt>tseng-i</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt></ruby>。

<sup>16</sup> <ruby><rb>是介</rb><rt>Z-ka</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>约书亚</rb><rt>Iah-shü-üô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>得着</rb><rt>teh-djoh</rt><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>全地</rb><rt>djün-di</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>山地</rb><rt>sæn-di</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>南边</rb><rt>nen-pin</rt><rb>地</rb><rt>di</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>坷山</rb><rt>Kʽo-sæn</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>全地</rb><rt>djün-di</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>山岙</rb><rt>sæn-ao</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>平垟</rb><rt>bing-yiang</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>连</rb><rt>lin</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>山地</rb><rt>sæn-di</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>山岙</rb><rt>sæn-ao</rt></ruby>，<sup>17</sup> <ruby><rb>从</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>靠近</rb><rt>kʽao-gying</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>西珥</u>个</rb><rt>Si-r-go</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>哈拉</rb><rt>Ha-lah</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>直到</rb><rt>dzih-tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>黑门</rb><rt>Heh-meng</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>山下</rb><rt>sæn-ʽô</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>黎巴嫩</rb><rt>Li-pô-neng</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>岙里个</rb><rt>ao-li-go</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>巴力迦得</rb><rt>Pô-lih-kyüô-teh</rt></ruby></u>；<ruby><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>掳来</rb><rt>lo-læ</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>王</rb><rt>wông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>攻打</rb><rt>kong-tang</rt><rb>杀掉</rb><rt>sah-diao</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt></ruby>。<sup>18</sup> <u><ruby><rb>约书亚</rb><rt>Iah-shü-üô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>王</rb><rt>wông</rt><rb>多</rb><rt>to</rt><rb>时</rb><rt>z</rt></ruby><sup>\[1\]</sup><ruby><rb>打仗</rb><rt>tang-tsiang</rt></ruby>。<sup>19</sup> <ruby><rb>除了</rb><rt>Djü-leh</rt><rb>庉</rb><rt>deng</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>基遍</u>个</rb><rt>Kyi-pin-go</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>希未</rb><rt>Hyi-vi</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>之外</rb><rt>ts-nga</rt><rb>呒有</rb><rt>m-yiu</rt><rb>一个</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>城子</rb><rt>dzing-ts</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>子孙</rb><rt>ts-seng</rt><rb>和好</rb><rt>ʽo-hao</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>打仗</rb><rt>tang-tsiang</rt><rb>夺来个</rb><rt>deh-læ-go</rt></ruby>。<sup>20</sup> <ruby><rb>因为</rb><rt>Ing-we</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>出于</rb><rt>cʽih-ü</rt><rb>耶和华个</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô-go</rt><rb>旨意</rb><rt>ts-i</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>心</rb><rt>sing</rt><rb>硬</rb><rt>ngang</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>打仗</rb><rt>tang-tsiang</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>除灭</rb><rt>djü-mih</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>求</rb><rt>gyiu</rt><rb>恩惠</rb><rt>eng-we</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>被</rb><rt>be</rt><rb>除灭</rb><rt>djü-mih</rt><rb>像</rb><rt>ziang</rt><rb>耶和华</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>吩咐</rb><rt>feng-fu</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt></ruby>。

<sup>21</sup> <ruby><rb>来</rb><rt>Læ</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>子孙个</rb><rt>ts-seng-go</rt><rb>地</rb><rt>di</rt><rb>呒有</rb><rt>m-yiu</rt><rb>留落</rb><rt>liu-loh</rt><rb>一个</rb><rt>ih-go</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>亚衲</rb><rt>Üô-neh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>族</rb><rt>dzoh</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>只有</rb><rt>tsih-yiu</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>迦萨</rb><rt>Kyüô-sah</rt></ruby></u>、<u><ruby><rb>迦特</rb><rt>Kyüô-deh</rt></ruby></u> <u><ruby><rb>亚实突</rb><rt>Üô-jih-deh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>还有</rb><rt>wa-yiu</rt><rb>留落间</rb><rt>liu-loh-kæn</rt></ruby>。<sup>23</sup> <ruby><rb>是介</rb><rt>Z-ka</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>约书亚</rb><rt>Iah-shü-üô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>夺着</rb><rt>deh-djoh</rt><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>全地</rb><rt>djün-di</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>照</rb><rt>tsiao</rt><rb>耶和华</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>凡百</rb><rt>væn-pah</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt></ruby>，<u><ruby><rb>约书亚</rb><rt>Iah-shü-üô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>照</rb><rt>tsiao</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>支派</rb><rt>ts-pʽa</rt><rb>族份</rb><rt>dzoh-veng</rt><rb>把</rb><rt>pô</rt><rb>地</rb><rt>di</rt><rb>分</rb><rt>feng</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>产业</rb><rt>tsʽæn-nyih</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>后头</rb><rt>ʽEo-deo</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>太平</rb><rt>tʽa-bing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>呒得</rb><rt>m-neh</rt><rb>打仗</rb><rt>tang-tsiang</rt><rb>了</rb><rt>de</rt></ruby>。

### Is. XII.

<u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>YI-SEH-LIH</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>子孙</rb><rt>ts-seng</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>约但</rb><rt>Iah-dæn</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>东边</rb><rt>tong-pin</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>攻打个</rb><rt>kong-tang-go</rt><rb>王</rb><rt>wông</rt><rb>得着</rb><rt>teh-djoh</rt><rb>其拉个</rb><rt>gyi-lah-go</rt><rb>地</rb><rt>di</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>产业</rb><rt>tsʽæn-nyih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>从</rb><rt>dzong</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>亚嫩</rb><rt>Üô-neng</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>岙</rb><rt>ao</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>黑门</rb><rt>Heh-meng</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>山</rb><rt>sæn</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>东边个</rb><rt>tong-pin-go</rt><rb>平垟</rb><rt>bing-yiang</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>下文</rb><rt>ʽô-veng</rt></ruby>。<sup>2</sup> <ruby><rb>庉</rb><rt>Deng</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>希实本</rb><rt>Hyi-jih-peng</rt></ruby></u> <u><ruby><rb>亚摩利</rb><rt>Üô-mo-li</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>王</rb><rt>wông</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>西宏</rb><rt>Si-ʽong</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>管治个</rb><rt>kwun-djü-go</rt><rb>地</rb><rt>di</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>从</rb><rt>dzong</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>亚嫩</rb><rt>Üô-neng</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>岙</rb><rt>ao</rt><rb>边沿个</rb><rt>pin-yin-go</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>亚罗珥</rb><rt>Üô-lo-r</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>岙</rb><rt>ao</rt><rb>中央</rb><rt>cong-nyiang</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>基列</rb><rt>Kyi-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>一半</rb><rt>ih-pun</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>直到</rb><rt>dzih-tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>亚扪</rb><rt>Üô-meng</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人个</rb><rt>nying-go</rt><rb>地界</rb><rt>di-ka</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>雅泊</rb><rt>Yüô-boh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>河</rb><rt>ʽo</rt></ruby>；<sup>3</sup> <ruby><rb>还有</rb><rt>wa-yiu</rt><rb>向</rb><rt>hyiang</rt><rb>东个</rb><rt>tong-go</rt><rb>平垟</rb><rt>bing-yiang</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>直到</rb><rt>dzih-tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>基尼烈</rb><rt>Kyi-nyi-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>海</rb><rt>hæ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>还有</rb><rt>wa-yiu</rt><rb>向</rb><rt>hyiang</rt><rb>东</rb><rt>tong</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>平垟个</rb><rt>bing-yiang-go</rt><rb>海</rb><rt>hæ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>盐</rb><rt>Yin</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>海</rb><rt>hæ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>对着</rb><rt>te-djoh</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>伯耶西末</u>个</rb><rt>Pah-yiæ-si-meh-go</rt><rb>路</rb><rt>lu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>还有</rb><rt>wa-yiu</rt><rb>向</rb><rt>hyiang</rt><rb>南边</rb><rt>nen-pin</rt><rb>直到</rb><rt>dzih-tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>比士迦</rb><rt>Pi-z-kyüô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>山脚下</rb><rt>sæn-kyiah-ʽô</rt></ruby>；<sup>4</sup> <ruby><rb>还有</rb><rt>wa-yiu</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>巴珊</rb><rt>Pô-sæn</rt></ruby></u> <ruby><rb><u>噩</u>个</rb><rt>Ngoh-go</rt><rb>地界</rb><rt>di-ka</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>利乏音</rb><rt>Li-vah-ing</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>剩落东个</rb><rt>dzing-loh-tong-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>庉</rb><rt>deng</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>亚大录</rb><rt>Üô-da-loh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以得来</rb><rt>Yi-teh-læ</rt></ruby></u>。<sup>5</sup> <ruby><rb>所</rb><rt>Sô</rt><rb>管治个</rb><rt>kwun-djü-go</rt><rb>地</rb><rt>di</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>黑门</rb><rt>Heh-meng</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>山</rb><rt>sæn</rt></ruby>、<u><ruby><rb>撒迦</rb><rt>Sah-kyüô</rt></ruby></u> <u><ruby><rb>巴珊</rb><rt>Pô-sæn</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>全地</rb><rt>djün-di</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>基述</rb><rt>Kyi-jih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>、<u><ruby><rb>玛迦</rb><rt>Mô-kyüô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>地界</rb><rt>di-ka</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>基列</rb><rt>Kyi-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>一半</rb><rt>ih-pun</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>希实本</rb><rt>Hyi-jih-peng</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>王</rb><rt>wông</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>西宏</u>个</rb><rt>Si-ʽong-go</rt><rb>地界</rb><rt>di-ka</rt></ruby>。<sup>6</sup> <ruby><rb>耶和华个</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô-go</rt><rb>奴仆</rb><rt>nu-boh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>子孙</rb><rt>ts-seng</rt><rb>攻打</rb><rt>kong-tang</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>耶和华个</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô-go</rt><rb>奴仆</rb><rt>nu-boh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>把</rb><rt>pô</rt><rb>箇个</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>地</rb><rt>di</rt><rb>赐拨</rb><rt>s-peh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>流便</rb><rt>Liu-bin</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>、<u><ruby><rb>迦得</rb><rt>Kyüô-teh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>玛拿西</rb><rt>Mô-nô-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>半</rb><rt>pun</rt><rb>支派</rb><rt>ts-pʽa</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>产业</rb><rt>tsʽæn-nyih</rt></ruby>。

<sup>7</sup> <u><ruby><rb>约书亚</rb><rt>Iah-shü-üô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>子孙</rb><rt>ts-seng</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>约但</rb><rt>Iah-dæn</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>西边</rb><rt>si-pin</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>攻打个</rb><rt>kong-tang-go</rt><rb>王</rb><rt>wông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>从</rb><rt>dzong</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>黎巴嫩</rb><rt>Li-pô-neng</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>岙个</rb><rt>ao-go</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>巴力迦得</rb><rt>Pô-lih-kyüô-teh</rt></ruby></u><sup>\[1\]</sup>，<ruby><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>靠近</rb><rt>kʽao-gying</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>西珥</u>个</rb><rt>Si-r-go</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>哈录</rb><rt>Ha-loh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>山</rb><rt>sæn</rt></ruby>；<u><ruby><rb>约书亚</rb><rt>Iah-shü-üô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>把</rb><rt>pô</rt><rb>箇个</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>地</rb><rt>di</rt><rb>照</rb><rt>tsiao</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>族份</rb><rt>dzoh-veng</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>支派</rb><rt>ts-pʽa</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>产业个</rb><rt>tsʽæn-nyih-go</rt></ruby>；<sup>8</sup> <ruby><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>山地</rb><rt>sæn-di</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>平地</rb><rt>bing-di</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>平垟</rb><rt>bing-yiang</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>山脚下个</rb><rt>sæn-kyiah-ʽô-go</rt><rb>平地</rb><rt>bing-di</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>旷野</rb><rt>kwʽông-iæ</rt><rb>地</rb><rt>di</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>南边个</rb><rt>nen-pin-go</rt><rb>地</rb><rt>di</rt></ruby>；<u><ruby><rb>赫</rb><rt>Heh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>、<u><ruby><rb>亚摩利</rb><rt>Üô-mo-li</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>、<u><ruby><rb>迦南</rb><rt>Kyüô-nen</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>、<u><ruby><rb>比利洗</rb><rt>Pi-li-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>、<u><ruby><rb>希未</rb><rt>Hyi-vi</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>、<u><ruby><rb>耶布士</rb><rt>Yiæ-pu-z</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>。<sup>9</sup> ### Is. XIII.

<u><ruby><rb>约书亚</rb><rt>IAH-SHÜ-ÜÔ</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>老了</rb><rt>lao-de</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>年纪</rb><rt>nyin-kyi</rt><rb>大了</rb><rt>do-de</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>耶和华</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>尔</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>老了</rb><rt>lao-de</rt><rb>年纪</rb><rt>nyin-kyi</rt><rb>大了</rb><rt>do-de</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>还有</rb><rt>wa-yiu</rt><rb>许多</rb><rt>hyü-to</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>得着</rb><rt>teh-djoh</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>产业个</rb><rt>tsʽæn-nyih-go</rt><rb>地</rb><rt>di</rt></ruby>。<sup>2</sup>
