<ruby><rb>平垟个</rb><rt>bing-yiang-go</rt><rb>各</rb><rt>koh</rt><rb>城子</rb><rt>dzing-ts</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>庉</rb><rt>deng</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>希实本</rb><rt>Hyi-jih-peng</rt></ruby></u> <u><ruby><rb>亚摩利</rb><rt>Üô-mo-li</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>王</rb><rt>wông</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>西宏</u>个</rb><rt>Si-ʽong-go</rt><rb>全国</rb><rt>djün-koh</rt></ruby>；<u><ruby><rb>西宏</rb><rt>Si-ʽong</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>杀</rb><rt>sah</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>米甸</rb><rt>Mi-din</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>五个</rb><rt>ng-go</rt><rb>牧伯</rb><rt>moh-pah</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>时候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt><rb>杀个</rb><rt>sah-go</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以未</rb><rt>Yi-vi</rt></ruby></u>、<u><ruby><rb>利金</rb><rt>Li-kying</rt></ruby></u>、<u><ruby><rb>苏珥</rb><rt>Su-r</rt></ruby></u>、<u><ruby><rb>户珥</rb><rt>Wu-r</rt></ruby></u>、<u><ruby><rb>利巴</rb><rt>Li-pô</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>西宏</u>个</rb><rt>Si-ʽong-go</rt><rb>诸侯</rb><rt>cü-ʽeo</rt><rb>庉</rb><rt>deng</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>箇个</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>地</rb><rt>di</rt></ruby>。<sup>22</sup> <ruby><rb>用</rb><rt>Yüong</rt><rb>邪法个</rb><rt>zia-fah-go</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>比珥</rb><rt>Pi-r</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>儿子</rb><rt>ng-ts</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>巴兰</rb><rt>Pô-læn</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>子孙</rb><rt>ts-seng</rt><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>刀</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>杀个</rb><rt>sah-go</rt><rb>在内</rb><rt>dzæ-nen</rt></ruby>。<sup>23</sup> <u><ruby><rb>流便</rb><rt>Liu-bin</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>子孙个</rb><rt>ts-seng-go</rt><rb>地界</rb><rt>di-ka</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>约但</rb><rt>Iah-dæn</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>河</rb><rt>ʽo</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>河</rb><rt>ʽo</rt><rb>边沿</rb><rt>pin-yin</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>Keh</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>流便</rb><rt>Liu-bin</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>子孙</rb><rt>ts-seng</rt><rb>照</rb><rt>tsiao</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>房份</rb><rt>vông-veng</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>得着个</rb><rt>teh-djoh-go</rt><rb>产业</rb><rt>tsʽæn-nyih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>城子</rb><rt>dzing-ts</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>属</rb><rt>joh</rt><rb>其个</rb><rt>gyi-go</rt><rb>乡村</rb><rt>hyiang-tsʽeng</rt></ruby>。

<sup>24</sup> <u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>分</rb><rt>feng</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>迦得</rb><rt>Kyüô-teh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>支派</rb><rt>ts-pʽa</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>迦得</rb><rt>Kyüô-teh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>子孙</rb><rt>ts-seng</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>照</rb><rt>tsiao</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>房份</rb><rt>vông-veng</rt></ruby>。<sup>25</sup> <ruby><rb>其拉个</rb><rt>Gyi-lah-go</rt><rb>地界</rb><rt>di-ka</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>雅设</rb><rt>Yüô-shih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>基列</u>个</rb><rt>Kyi-lih-go</rt><rb>各</rb><rt>koh</rt><rb>城子</rb><rt>dzing-ts</rt></ruby>；<u><ruby><rb>亚扪</rb><rt>Üô-meng</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人个</rb><rt>nying-go</rt><rb>一半</rb><rt>ih-pun</rt><rb>地</rb><rt>di</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>拉巴</rb><rt>Lah-pô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>面前个</rb><rt>min-zin-go</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>亚罗珥</rb><rt>Üô-lo-r</rt></ruby></u>；<sup>26</sup> <ruby><rb>还有</rb><rt>wa-yiu</rt><rb>从</rb><rt>dzong</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>希实本</rb><rt>Hyi-jih-peng</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>拉末</rb><rt>Lah-meh</rt></ruby></u>、<u><ruby><rb>蜜是巴</rb><rt>Mih-z-pô</rt></ruby></u>、<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>比多宁</rb><rt>Pi-to-nying</rt></ruby></u>；<ruby><rb>还有</rb><rt>wa-yiu</rt><rb>从</rb><rt>dzong</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>玛哈念</rb><rt>Mô-ha-nyiæn</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>底璧</u>个</rb><rt>Ti-pih-go</rt><rb>地界</rb><rt>di-ka</rt></ruby>；<sup>27</sup> <ruby><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>山岙个</rb><rt>sæn-ao-go</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>伯亚兰</rb><rt>Pah-üô-læn</rt></ruby></u>、<u><ruby><rb>伯宁拉</rb><rt>Pah-nying-lah</rt></ruby></u>、<u><ruby><rb>疏割</rb><rt>Su-keh</rt></ruby></u>、<u><ruby><rb>撒分</rb><rt>Sah-feng</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>希实本</rb><rt>Hyi-jih-peng</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>王</rb><rt>wông</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>西宏</rb><rt>Si-ʽong</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>余多个</rb><rt>yü-to-go</rt><rb>地</rb><rt>di</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>还有</rb><rt>wa-yiu</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>约但</rb><rt>Iah-dæn</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>河边</rb><rt>ʽo-pin</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>基尼烈</rb><rt>Kyi-nyi-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>海</rb><rt>hæ</rt><rb>极边</rb><rt>gyih-pin</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>约但</rb><rt>Iah-dæn</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>东边</rb><rt>tong-pin</rt></ruby>。<sup>28</sup> <ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>Keh</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>迦得</rb><rt>Kyüô-teh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>子孙</rb><rt>ts-seng</rt><rb>照</rb><rt>tsiao</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>房份</rb><rt>vông-veng</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>得着个</rb><rt>teh-djoh-go</rt><rb>产业</rb><rt>tsʽæn-nyih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>城子</rb><rt>dzing-ts</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>属</rb><rt>joh</rt><rb>其个</rb><rt>gyi-go</rt><rb>乡村</rb><rt>hyiang-tsʽeng</rt></ruby>。

<sup>29</sup> <u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>分</rb><rt>feng</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>玛拿西</rb><rt>Mô-nô-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>半</rb><rt>pun</rt><rb>支派</rb><rt>ts-pʽa</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>玛拿西</rb><rt>Mô-nô-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>半</rb><rt>pun</rt><rb>支派</rb><rt>ts-pʽa</rt><rb>照</rb><rt>tsiao</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>房份</rb><rt>vông-veng</rt></ruby>。<sup>30</sup> <ruby><rb>其拉个</rb><rt>Gyi-lah-go</rt><rb>地界</rb><rt>di-ka</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>从</rb><rt>dzong</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>玛哈念</rb><rt>Mô-ha-nyiæn</rt></ruby></u>、<u><ruby><rb>巴珊</rb><rt>Pô-sæn</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>全地</rb><rt>djün-di</rt></ruby>、<u><ruby><rb>巴珊</rb><rt>Pô-sæn</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>王</rb><rt>wông</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>噩</rb><rt>Ngoh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>全国</rb><rt>djün-koh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>还有</rb><rt>wa-yiu</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>巴珊</rb><rt>Pô-sæn</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>地个</rb><rt>di-go</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>睚珥</rb><rt>Yiæ-r</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>各</rb><rt>koh</rt><rb>城子</rb><rt>dzing-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>共</rb><rt>gong</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>六十</rb><rt>loh-jih</rt><rb>座</rb><rt>zo</rt></ruby>；<sup>31</sup> <u><ruby><rb>基列</rb><rt>Kyi-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>一半</rb><rt>ih-pun</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>亚大录</rb><rt>Üô-da-loh</rt></ruby></u>、<u><ruby><rb>以得来</rb><rt>Yi-teh-læ</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>巴珊</rb><rt>Pô-sæn</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>王</rb><rt>wông</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>噩</rb><rt>Ngoh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>国份里个</rb><rt>koh-veng-li-go</rt><rb>城子</rb><rt>dzing-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>赐拨</rb><rt>s-peh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>玛拿西</rb><rt>Mô-nô-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>儿子</rb><rt>ng-ts</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>玛吉</u>个</rb><rt>Mô-kyih-go</rt><rb>子孙</rb><rt>ts-seng</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>玛吉</rb><rt>Mô-kyih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>一半</rb><rt>ih-pun</rt><rb>子孙</rb><rt>ts-seng</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>照</rb><rt>tsiao</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>房份</rb><rt>vông-veng</rt></ruby>。

<sup>32</sup> <ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>Keh</rt><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>约但</rb><rt>Iah-dæn</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>河</rb><rt>ʽo</rt><rb>东边</rb><rt>tong-pin</rt><rb>对着</rb><rt>te-djoh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶利哥</rb><rt>Yiæ-li-ko</rt></ruby></u> <ruby><rb><u>摩押</u>个</rb><rt>Mo-iah-go</rt><rb>平垟里</rb><rt>bing-yiang-li</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>分</rb><rt>feng</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>产业个</rb><rt>tsʽæn-nyih-go</rt></ruby>。<sup>33</sup> <ruby><rb>但是</rb><rt>Dæn-z</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>呒得</rb><rt>m-neh</rt><rb>分</rb><rt>feng</rt><rb>产业</rb><rt>tsʽæn-nyih</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>利未</rb><rt>Li-vi</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>支派</rb><rt>ts-pʽa</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>因为</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>耶和华</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>以色列</u>个</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih-go</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>其拉个</rb><rt>gyi-lah-go</rt><rb>产业</rb><rt>tsʽæn-nyih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>像</rb><rt>ziang</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt></ruby>。

---

### Is. XIV.

<u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>YI-SEH-LIH</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>子孙</rb><rt>ts-seng</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>迦<u>南</rb><rt>Kyüô-nen</rt><rb>地</rb><rt>di</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>得着个</rb><rt>teh-djoh-go</rt><rb>产业</rb><rt>tsʽæn-nyih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>祭司</rb><rt>tsi-s</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以利亚撒</rb><rt>Yi-li-üô-sah</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>嫩</u>个</rb><rt>Neng-go</rt><rb>儿子</rb><rt>ng-ts</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>约书亚</rb><rt>Iah-shü-üô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>支派个</rb><rt>ts-pʽa-go</rt><rb>众</rb><rt>cong</rt><rb>牧伯</rb><rt>moh-pah</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>分</rb><rt>feng</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>其拉个</rb><rt>gyi-lah-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>像</rb><rt>ziang</rt><rb>下文</rb><rt>ʽô-veng</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt></ruby>。<sup>2</sup> <ruby><rb>其拉个</rb><rt>Gyi-lah-go</rt><rb>产业</rb><rt>tsʽæn-nyih</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>抽签</rb><rt>tsʽiu-tsʽin</rt><rb>分个</rb><rt>feng-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>照</rb><rt>tsiao</rt><rb>耶和华</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>托</rb><rt>tʽoh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>为了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt><rb>九</rb><rt>kyiu</rt><rb>支派</rb><rt>ts-pʽa</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>半</rb><rt>pun</rt><rb>支派</rb><rt>ts-pʽa</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>吩咐个</rb><rt>feng-fu-go</rt></ruby>。<sup>3</sup> <ruby><rb>因为</rb><rt>Ing-we</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>已经</rb><rt>yi-kying</rt><rb>分</rb><rt>feng</rt><rb>产业</rb><rt>tsʽæn-nyih</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>两</rb><rt>liang</rt><rb>支派</rb><rt>ts-pʽa</rt><rb>半</rb><rt>pun</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>约但</rb><rt>Iah-dæn</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>河外</rb><rt>ʽo-nga</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>只是</rb><rt>tsih-z</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>中央</rb><rt>cong-nyiang</rt><rb>呒得</rb><rt>m-neh</rt><rb>分</rb><rt>feng</rt><rb>产业</rb><rt>tsʽæn-nyih</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>利未</rb><rt>Li-vi</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>。<sup>4</sup> <ruby><rb>因为</rb><rt>Ing-we</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>约瑟</rb><rt>Iah-seh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>子孙</rb><rt>ts-seng</rt><rb>分</rb><rt>feng</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>两</rb><rt>liang</rt><rb>支派</rb><rt>ts-pʽa</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>玛拿西</rb><rt>Mô-nô-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以法莲</rb><rt>Yi-fah-lin</rt></ruby></u>；<u><ruby><rb>利未</rb><rt>Li-vi</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>呒得</rb><rt>m-neh</rt><rb>分</rb><rt>feng</rt><rb>地</rb><rt>di</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>只有</rb><rt>tsih-yiu</rt><rb>赐拨</rb><rt>s-peh</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>城子</rb><rt>dzing-ts</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>庉</rb><rt>deng</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>还有</rb><rt>wa-yiu</rt><rb>为了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt><rb>牲口</rb><rt>sang-kʽeo</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>财物</rb><rt>dzæ-veh</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>赐拨</rb><rt>s-peh</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>城外个</rb><rt>dzing-nga-go</rt><rb>田地</rb><rt>din-di</rt></ruby>。<sup>5</sup> <ruby><rb>照</rb><rt>Tsiao</rt><rb>耶和华</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>吩咐</rb><rt>feng-fu</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u>，<u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>子孙</rb><rt>ts-seng</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>把</rb><rt>pô</rt><rb>地</rb><rt>di</rt><rb>分开</rb><rt>feng-kʽæ</rt></ruby>。

<sup>6</sup> <ruby><rb>箇遭</rb><rt>Keh-tsao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>犹大</rb><rt>Yiu-da</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>子孙</rb><rt>ts-seng</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>吉甲</rb><rt>Kyih-kyiah</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>见</rb><rt>kyin</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>约书亚</rb><rt>Iah-shü-üô</rt></ruby></u>；<u><ruby><rb>基尼洗</rb><rt>Kyi-nyi-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶孚尼</rb><rt>Yiæ-fu-nyi</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>儿子</rb><rt>ng-ts</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>迦勒</rb><rt>Kyüô-lah</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>耶和华</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>迦铁巴尼亚</rb><rt>Kyüô-tʽih-pô-nyi-üô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>神明个</rb><rt>Jing-ming-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>讲到</rb><rt>kông-tao</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>吩咐个</rb><rt>feng-fu-go</rt><rb>事干</rb><rt>z-ken</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>晓得</rb><rt>hyiao-teh</rt></ruby>。<sup>7</sup> <ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>四十</rb><rt>s-jih</rt><rb>岁</rb><rt>shü</rt><rb>时候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt><rb>耶和华个</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô-go</rt><rb>奴仆</rb><rt>nu-boh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>从</rb><rt>dzong</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>迦铁巴尼亚</rb><rt>Kyüô-tʽih-pô-nyi-üô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>差</rb><rt>tsʽa</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>探看</rb><rt>tʽen-kʽen</rt><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>地</rb><rt>di</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>照</rb><rt>tsiao</rt><rb>我个</rb><rt>ngô-go</rt><rb>心意</rb><rt>sing-i</rt><rb>回报</rb><rt>we-pao</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>。<sup>8</sup> <ruby><rb>但是</rb><rt>Dæn-z</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>同去个</rb><rt>dong-kyʽi-go</rt><rb>兄弟</rb><rt>hyüong-di</rt><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>百姓</rb><rt>pah-sing</rt><rb>胆小</rb><rt>tæn-siao</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>只是</rb><rt>tsih-z</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>全全</rb><rt>djün-djün</rt><rb>依从</rb><rt>i-dzong</rt><rb>耶和华</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>我个</rb><rt>ngô-go</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt></ruby>。<sup>9</sup> <u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>箇日</rb><rt>keh-nyih</rt><rb>罚咒</rb><rt>vah-tsiu</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：『<ruby><rb>尔</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>既然</rb><rt>kyi-jün</rt><rb>全全</rb><rt>djün-djün</rt><rb>依从</rb><rt>i-dzong</rt><rb>耶和华</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>我个</rb><rt>ngô-go</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>脚</rb><rt>kyiah</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>踏过个</rb><rt>dah-ko-go</rt><rb>地</rb><rt>di</rt><rb>必定</rb><rt>pih-ding</rt><rb>归拨</rb><rt>kwe-peh</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>尔个</rb><rt>ng-go</rt><rb>子孙</rb><rt>ts-seng</rt><rb>永远</rb><rt>üong-yün</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>产业</rb><rt>tsʽæn-nyih</rt></ruby>。』<sup>10</sup> <ruby><rb>现在</rb><rt>Yin-dzæ</rt><rb>耶和华</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>照</rb><rt>tsiao</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>保佑</rb><rt>pao-yiu</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>存活</rb><rt>dzeng-weh</rt><rb>四十五</rb><rt>s-jih-ng</rt><rb>年</rb><rt>nyin</rt><rb>工夫</rb><rt>kong-fu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>从</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>耶和华</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>讲</rb><rt>kông</rt><rb>箇个</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>说话</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt></ruby>，<u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>走</rb><rt>tseo</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>旷野</rb><rt>kwʽông-iæ</rt><rb>时候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>今末</rb><rt>kyih-mih</rt><rb>八十五</rb><rt>pah-jih-ng</rt><rb>岁了</rb><rt>shü-de</rt></ruby>。 <sup>11</sup> <ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>今末</rb><rt>kyih-mih</rt><rb>仍旧</rb><rt>dzing-gyiu</rt><rb>康健</rb><rt>kʽông-gyin</rt><rb>像</rb><rt>ziang</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>差</rb><rt>tsʽa</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>箇日</rb><rt>keh-nyih</rt><rb>一样</rb><rt>ih-yiang</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>时候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt><rb>我个</rb><rt>ngô-go</rt><rb>力量</rb><rt>lih-liang</rt><rb>咋个</rb><rt>dza-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>现在</rb><rt>yin-dzæ</rt><rb>力量</rb><rt>lih-liang</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>咋个</rb><rt>dza-go</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>会</rb><rt>We</rt><rb>打仗</rb><rt>tang-tsiang</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>走来</rb><rt>tseo-læ</rt><rb>走去</rb><rt>tseo-kyʽi</rt></ruby>。<sup>12</sup> <ruby><rb>现在</rb><rt>Yin-dzæ</rt><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>箇座</rb><rt>keh-zo</rt><rb>山</rb><rt>sæn</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>耶和华</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>箇日</rb><rt>keh-nyih</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>讲个</rb><rt>kông-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>因为</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>箇日</rb><rt>keh-nyih</rt><rb>听闻</rb><rt>tʽing-meng</rt><rb>箇头</rb><rt>keh-deo</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>亚衲</rb><rt>Üô-neh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>族</rb><rt>dzoh</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>还有</rb><rt>wa-yiu</rt><rb>高大</rb><rt>kao-do</rt><rb>坚固个</rb><rt>kyin-kwu-go</rt><rb>城子</rb><rt>dzing-ts</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>如果</rb><rt>jü-ko</rt><rb>耶和华</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>来东</rb><rt>læ-tong</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>赶</rb><rt>ken</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>出</rb><rt>cʽih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>像</rb><rt>ziang</rt><rb>耶和华</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt></ruby>。」<sup>13</sup> <u><ruby><rb>约书亚</rb><rt>Iah-shü-üô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>祝福</rb><rt>coh-foh</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>将</rb><rt>tsiang</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>希伯仑</rb><rt>Hyi-pah-leng</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶孚尼</rb><rt>Yiæ-fu-nyi</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>儿子</rb><rt>ng-ts</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>迦勒</rb><rt>Kyüô-lah</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>产业</rb><rt>tsʽæn-nyih</rt></ruby>。<sup>14</sup> <ruby><rb>所以</rb><rt>Sô-yi</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>希伯仑</rb><rt>Hyi-pah-leng</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>基尼洗</rb><rt>Kyi-nyi-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶孚尼</rb><rt>Yiæ-fu-nyi</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>儿子</rb><rt>ng-ts</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>迦勒</rb><rt>Kyüô-lah</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>产业</rb><rt>tsʽæn-nyih</rt><rb>直到</rb><rt>dzih-tao</rt><rb>如今</rb><rt>jü-kying</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>因为</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>全全</rb><rt>djün-djün</rt><rb>依从</rb><rt>i-dzong</rt><rb>耶和华</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>以色列</u>个</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih-go</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt></ruby>。<sup>15</sup> <u><ruby><rb>希伯仑</rb><rt>Hyi-pah-leng</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>从前</rb><rt>dzong-zin</rt><rb>叫勒</rb><rt>kyiao-leh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>基列亚巴</rb><rt>Kyi-lih-üô-pô</rt></ruby></u><sup>\[1\]</sup>；<ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>亚巴</rb><rt>Üô-pô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>亚衲</rb><rt>Üô-neh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>族</rb><rt>dzoh</rt><rb>顶</rb><rt>ting</rt><rb>长大个</rb><rt>dziang-do-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>箇遭</rb><rt>Keh-tsao</rt><rb>地</rb><rt>di</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>太平</rb><rt>tʽa-bing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>呒得</rb><rt>m-neh</rt><rb>打仗</rb><rt>tang-tsiang</rt></ruby>。

<sup>\[1\] *Ziu-z* Üô-pô-go dzing-ts. 就是<u>亚巴</u>个城子。（原注）</sup>

---

### Is. XV.

<u><ruby><rb>犹大</rb><rt>YIU-DA</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>子孙个</rb><rt>ts-seng-go</rt><rb>支派</rb><rt>ts-pʽa</rt><rb>照</rb><rt>tsiao</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>房份</rb><rt>vông-veng</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>抽签</rb><rt>tsʽiu-tsʽin</rt><rb>分个</rb><rt>feng-go</rt><rb>地</rb><rt>di</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>极南个</rb><rt>gyih-nen-go</rt><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以东</rb><rt>Yi-tong</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>地界</rb><rt>di-ka</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>汛</rb><rt>Sing</rt><rb>旷野</rb><rt>kwʽông-iæ</rt><rb>南边</rb><rt>nen-pin</rt></ruby>。<sup>2</sup> <ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt><rb>南边个</rb><rt>nen-pin-go</rt><rb>地界</rb><rt>di-ka</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>盐</rb><rt>Yin</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>海</rb><rt>hæ</rt><rb>极南</rb><rt>gyih-nen</rt><rb>边沿</rb><rt>pin-yin</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>从</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>向</rb><rt>hyiang</rt><rb>南边个</rb><rt>nen-pin-go</rt><rb>海汊</rb><rt>hæ-tsʽô</rt><rb>起头</rb><rt>kyʽi-deo</rt></ruby>；<sup>3</sup> <ruby><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>亚克拉滨</rb><rt>Üô-kʽah-lah-ping</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>南边</rb><rt>nen-pin</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>汛</rb><rt>Sing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>上</rb><rt>zông</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>迦铁巴尼亚</rb><rt>Kyüô-tʽih-pô-nyi-üô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>南边</rb><rt>nen-pin</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>过到</rb><rt>ko-tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>希斯仑</rb><rt>Hyi-s-leng</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>上</rb><rt>zông</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>亚忒</rb><rt>Üô-tʽeh</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>转</rb><rt>cün</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>甲加</rb><rt>Kyiah-kyüô</rt></ruby></u>。<sup>4</sup> <ruby><rb>又</rb><rt>Yi</rt><rb>过到</rb><rt>ko-tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>押们</rb><rt>Iah-meng</rt></ruby></u>；<ruby><rb>出</rb><rt>cʽih</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>埃及</rb><rt>Yiæ-gyih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>河</rb><rt>ʽo</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>地界</rb><rt>di-ka</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>海</rb><rt>hæ</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>尽头</rb><rt>zing-deo</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>南边个</rb><rt>nen-pin-go</rt><rb>地界</rb><rt>di-ka</rt></ruby>。<sup>5</sup> <ruby><rb>东边</rb><rt>Tong-pin</rt><rb>地界</rb><rt>di-ka</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>盐</rb><rt>Yin</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>海</rb><rt>hæ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>直到</rb><rt>dzih-tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>约但</rb><rt>Iah-dæn</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>河口</rb><rt>ʽo-kʽeo</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>北边个</rb><rt>Poh-pin-go</rt><rb>地界</rb><rt>di-ka</rt><rb>从</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>海汊</rb><rt>hæ-tsʽô</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>约但</rb><rt>Iah-dæn</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>河口</rb><rt>ʽo-kʽeo</rt></ruby>；<sup>6</sup> <ruby><rb>地界</rb><rt>di-ka</rt><rb>上</rb><rt>zông</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>伯曷拉</rb><rt>Pah-ʽeh-lah</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>过</rb><rt>ko</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>伯亚拉巴</u>个</rb><rt>Pah-üô-lah-pô-go</rt><rb>北边</rb><rt>poh-pin</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>上</rb><rt>zông</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>流便</rb><rt>Liu-bin</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>儿子</rb><rt>ng-ts</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>波罕</u>个</rb><rt>Po-hen-go</rt><rb>磐石</rb><rt>bun-zah</rt></ruby>；<sup>7</sup> <ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>地界</rb><rt>di-ka</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>从</rb><rt>dzong</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>亚割</rb><rt>Üô-keh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>岙</rb><rt>ao</rt><rb>上去</rb><rt>zông-kyʽi</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>底璧</rb><rt>Ti-pih</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>向</rb><rt>hyiang</rt><rb>北</rb><rt>poh</rt><rb>对着</rb><rt>te-djoh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>吉甲</rb><rt>Kyih-kyiah</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>上</rb><rt>zông</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>亚都冥</rb><rt>Üô-tu-ming</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>对面</rb><rt>te-min</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>河个</rb><rt>ʽo-go</rt><rb>南边</rb><rt>nen-pin</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>地界</rb><rt>di-ka</rt><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>过去</rb><rt>ko-kyʽi</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>宴示麦</u><sup>\[1\]</sup>个</rb><rt>In-z-mah-go</rt><rb>水</rb><rt>shü</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>宴罗结</rb><rt>In-lo-kyih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>为止</rb><rt>we-ts</rt></ruby>；<sup>8</sup> <ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>地界</rb><rt>di-ka</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>上</rb><rt>zông</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>便欣嫩</rb><rt>Bin-hying-neng</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>岙</rb><rt>ao</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶布士</rb><rt>Yiæ-pu-z</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>南边</rb><rt>nen-pin</rt></ruby>，（<u><ruby><rb>耶布士</rb><rt>Yiæ-pu-z</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶路撒冷</rb><rt>Yiæ-lu-sah-leng</rt></ruby></u>，）<ruby><rb>地界</rb><rt>di-ka</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>上</rb><rt>zông</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>向</rb><rt>hyiang</rt><rb>西边个</rb><rt>Si-pin-go</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>便欣嫩</rb><rt>Bin-hying-neng</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>岙</rb><rt>ao</rt><rb>对面个</rb><rt>te-min-go</rt><rb>山顶头</rb><rt>sæn-ting-den</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>利乏音</rb><rt>Li-vah-ing</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>山岙</rb><rt>sæn-ao</rt><rb>极北个</rb><rt>gyih-poh-go</rt><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt></ruby>；<sup>9</sup> <ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>地界</rb><rt>di-ka</rt><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>从</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>山顶头</rb><rt>sæn-ting-den</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>尼弗多亚</u>个</rb><rt>Nyi-feh-to-üô-go</rt><rb>水源</rb><rt>shü-nyün</rt></ruby><sup>\[2\]</sup>，<ruby><rb>过到</rb><rt>ko-tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以弗仑</rb><rt>Yi-feh-leng</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>山里</rb><rt>sæn-li</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>城子</rb><rt>dzing-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>还有</rb><rt>wa-yiu</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>巴拉</rb><rt>Pô-lah</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>基列耶琳</rb><rt>Kyi-lih-yiæ-ling</rt></ruby></u>；<sup>10</sup> <ruby><rb>地界</rb><rt>di-ka</rt><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>从</rb><rt>dzong</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>巴拉</rb><rt>Pô-lah</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>向</rb><rt>hyiang</rt><rb>西</rb><rt>si</rt><rb>转</rb><rt>cün</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>西珥</rb><rt>Si-r</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>山</rb><rt>sæn</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>过</rb><rt>ko</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶琳</rb><rt>Yiæ-ling</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>山</rb><rt>sæn</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>北边</rb><rt>poh-pin</rt></ruby>，（<u><ruby><rb>耶琳</rb><rt>Yiæ-ling</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>基撒仑</rb><rt>Kyi-sah-leng</rt></ruby></u>，）<ruby><rb>落到</rb><rt>loh-tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>伯示麦</rb><rt>Pah-z-meh</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>过去</rb><rt>ko-kyʽi</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>亭拿</rb><rt>Ding-nô</rt></ruby></u>；<sup>11</sup> <ruby><rb>地界</rb><rt>di-ka</rt><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>出去</rb><rt>cʽih-kyʽi</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以革伦</rb><rt>Yi-keh-leng</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>北</rb><rt>poh</rt><rb>国</rb><rt>koh</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>实伦</rb><rt>Jih-leng</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>过到</rb><rt>ko-tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>巴拉</rb><rt>Pô-lah</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>山</rb><rt>sæn</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>押业</rb><rt>Iah-nyih</rt></ruby></u>；<ruby><rb>箇个</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>地界</rb><rt>di-ka</rt><rb>极头</rb><rt>gyih-deo</rt><rb>一直</rb><rt>ih-dzih</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>海</rb><rt>hæ</rt></ruby>。<sup>12</sup> <ruby><rb>西边</rb><rt>Si-pin</rt><rb>地界</rb><rt>di-ka</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>大</rb><rt>Do</rt><rb>海</rb><rt>hæ</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>边沿</rb><rt>pin-yin</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>Keh</rt><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>犹大</rb><rt>Yiu-da</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>子孙</rb><rt>ts-seng</rt><rb>团圈个</rb><rt>dön-kyʽün-go</rt><rb>地界</rb><rt>di-ka</rt><rb>照</rb><rt>tsiao</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>房份</rb><rt>vông-veng</rt></ruby>。

<sup>\[1\] 原文此处错印为 In-z-meh，后文为 In-z-mah</sup>

<sup>\[2\] 此处原书错印为 shü-nyü，下文均为 shü-nyün</sup>

<sup>13</sup> <u><ruby><rb>约书亚</rb><rt>Iah-shü-üô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>分</rb><rt>feng</rt><rb>地</rb><rt>di</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶孚尼</rb><rt>Yiæ-fu-nyi</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>儿子</rb><rt>ng-ts</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>迦勒</rb><rt>Kyüô-lah</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>犹大</rb><rt>Yiu-da</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>子孙</rb><rt>ts-seng</rt><rb>中央</rb><rt>cong-nyiang</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>照</rb><rt>tsiao</rt><rb>耶和华</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>吩咐</rb><rt>feng-fu</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>约书亚</u>个</rb><rt>Iah-shü-üô-go</rt><rb>命令</rb><rt>ming-ling</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>基列亚巴</rb><rt>Kyi-lih-üô-pô</rt></ruby></u><sup>\[1\]</sup>，<ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>亚巴</rb><rt>Üô-pô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>亚衲</rb><rt>Üô-neh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>阿爹</rb><rt>ah-tia</rt></ruby>，（<u><ruby><rb>基列亚巴</rb><rt>Kyi-lih-üô-pô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>希伯仑</rb><rt>Hyi-pah-leng</rt></ruby></u>。）<sup>14</sup> <u><ruby><rb>迦勒</rb><rt>Kyüô-lah</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>从</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>箇头</rb><rt>keh-deo</rt><rb>赶出</rb><rt>ken-cʽih</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>亚衲</rb><rt>Üô-neh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>山个</rb><rt>sæn-go</rt><rb>儿子</rb><rt>ng-ts</rt></ruby>，<u><ruby><rb>示筛</rb><rt>Z-s</rt></ruby></u>、<u><ruby><rb>亚希幔</rb><rt>Üô-hyi-mæn</rt></ruby></u>、<u><ruby><rb>达买</rb><rt>Dah-mæ</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>亚衲</rb><rt>Üô-neh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>子孙</rb><rt>ts-seng</rt></ruby>。<sup>15</sup> <ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>从</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>箇头</rb><rt>keh-deo</rt><rb>上去</rb><rt>zông-kyʽi</rt><rb>攻打</rb><rt>kong-tang</rt><rb>庉</rb><rt>deng</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>底璧</u>个</rb><rt>Ti-pih-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>；<u><ruby><rb>底璧</rb><rt>Ti-pih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>从前</rb><rt>dzong-zin</rt><rb>叫勒</rb><rt>kyiao-leh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>基列西弗</rb><rt>Kyi-lih-si-feh</rt></ruby></u>，<sup>16</sup> <u><ruby><rb>迦勒</rb><rt>Kyüô-lah</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>谁</rb><rt>Jü</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>攻打</rb><rt>kong-tang</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>基列西弗</rb><rt>Kyi-lih-si-feh</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>夺着</rb><rt>deh-djoh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>把</rb><rt>pô</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>囡</rb><rt>nön</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>押撒</rb><rt>Iah-sah</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>妻子</rb><rt>tsʽi-ts</rt></ruby>。」<sup>17</sup> <u><ruby><rb>迦勒</rb><rt>Kyüô-lah</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>兄弟</rb><rt>hyüong-di</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>基纳</u>个</rb><rt>Kyi-neh-go</rt><rb>儿子</rb><rt>ng-ts</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>阿得业</rb><rt>Ah-teh-nyih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>夺着</rb><rt>deh-djoh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>，<u><ruby><rb>迦勒</rb><rt>Kyüô-lah</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>把</rb><rt>pô</rt><rb>囡</rb><rt>nön</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>押撒</rb><rt>Iah-sah</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>妻子</rb><rt>tsʽi-ts</rt></ruby>。<sup>18</sup> <u><ruby><rb>押撒</rb><rt>Iah-sah</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>过门</rb><rt>ko-meng</rt><rb>时候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt><rb>讴</rb><rt>eo</rt><rb>丈夫</rb><rt>dziang-fu</rt><rb>向</rb><rt>hyiang</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>阿爹</rb><rt>ah-tia</rt><rb>讨</rb><rt>tʽao</rt><rb>一块</rb><rt>ih-kwʽe</rt><rb>地</rb><rt>di</rt></ruby>。<u><ruby><rb>押撒</rb><rt>Iah-sah</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>从</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>骡子</rb><rt>lo-ts</rt><rb>跳落来</rb><rt>tʽiao-loh-læ</rt></ruby>，<u><ruby><rb>迦勒</rb><rt>Kyüô-lah</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>尔</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>啥西</rb><rt>soh-si</rt></ruby>？」<sup>19</sup> <ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>赐</rb><rt>S</rt><rb>福气</rb><rt>foh-kyʽi</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>既然</rb><rt>kyi-jün</rt><rb>赐拨</rb><rt>s-peh</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>南边个</rb><rt>nen-pin-go</rt><rb>地</rb><rt>di</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>求</rb><rt>gyiu</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>赐拨</rb><rt>s-peh</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>水源</rb><rt>shü-nyün</rt></ruby>。」<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>赐拨</rb><rt>s-peh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>上下个</rb><rt>zông-ʽô-go</rt><rb>水源</rb><rt>shü-nyün</rt></ruby>。

<sup>\[1\] Kʽen Is. 14. 15. 看 Is. 14. 15.（原注）</sup>

<sup>20</sup> <ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>Keh</rt><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>犹大</rb><rt>Yiu-da</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>子孙</rb><rt>ts-seng</rt><rb>支派个</rb><rt>ts-pʽa-go</rt><rb>产业</rb><rt>tsʽæn-nyih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>照</rb><rt>tsiao</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>房份</rb><rt>vông-veng</rt></ruby>。

<sup>21</sup> <u><ruby><rb>犹大</rb><rt>Yiu-da</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>支派</rb><rt>ts-pʽa</rt><rb>顶</rb><rt>ting</rt><rb>远个</rb><rt>yün-go</rt><rb>城子</rb><rt>dzing-ts</rt><rb>靠近</rb><rt>kʽao-gying</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以东</rb><rt>Yi-tong</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>南边个</rb><rt>nen-pin-go</rt><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>甲薛</rb><rt>Kyiah-sih</rt></ruby></u>、<u><ruby><rb>以得</rb><rt>Yi-teh</rt></ruby></u>、<u><ruby><rb>雅姑珥</rb><rt>Yüô-kwu-r</rt></ruby></u>；<sup>22</sup> <u><ruby><rb>基拿</rb><rt>Kyi-nô</rt></ruby></u>、<u><ruby><rb>底摩拿</rb><rt>Ti-mo-nô</rt></ruby></u>、<u><ruby><rb>亚大挞</rb><rt>Üô-da-tʽeh</rt></ruby></u>；<sup>23</sup> <u><ruby><rb>基特</rb><rt>Kyi-deh</rt></ruby></u>、<u><ruby><rb>夏朔</rb><rt>Yüô-soh</rt></ruby></u>、<u><ruby><rb>易难</rb><rt>Yih-næn</rt></ruby></u>；<sup>24</sup> <u><ruby><rb>西弗</rb><rt>Si-feh</rt></ruby></u>、<ruby><rb>提练</rb><rt>Di-lin</rt></ruby>、<u><ruby><rb>比亚绿</rb><rt>Pi-üô-loh</rt></ruby></u>；<sup>25</sup>
