<u><ruby><rb>亚曼</rb><rt>Üô-mæn</rt></ruby></u>、<u><ruby><rb>示玛</rb><rt>Z-mô</rt></ruby></u>、<u><ruby><rb>摩拉大</rb><rt>Mo-lah-da</rt></ruby></u>；<sup>27</sup> <u><ruby><rb>哈萨迦大</rb><rt>Ha-sah-kyüô-da</rt></ruby></u>、<u><ruby><rb>黑实门</rb><rt>Heh-jih-meng</rt></ruby></u>、<u><ruby><rb>伯怕力</rb><rt>Pah-pʽô-lih</rt></ruby></u>；<sup>28</sup> <u><ruby><rb>哈萨书亚</rb><rt>Ha-sah-shü-üô</rt></ruby></u>、<u><ruby><rb>别是巴</rb><rt>Bih-z-pô</rt></ruby></u>、<u><ruby><rb>必若雅</rb><rt>Pih-ziah-yüô</rt></ruby></u>；<sup>29</sup> <u><ruby><rb>巴拉</rb><rt>Pô-lah</rt></ruby></u>、<u><ruby><rb>以因</rb><rt>Yi-ing</rt></ruby></u>、<u><ruby><rb>亚生</rb><rt>Üô-seng</rt></ruby></u>；<sup>30</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耳多腊</rb><rt>R-to-lah</rt></ruby></u>、<u><ruby><rb>基失</rb><rt>Kyi-shih</rt></ruby></u>、<u><ruby><rb>盍马</rb><rt>ʽEh-mô</rt></ruby></u>；<sup>31</sup> <u><ruby><rb>息腊</rb><rt>Sih-lah</rt></ruby></u>、<u><ruby><rb>麦玛拿</rb><rt>Meh-mô-nô</rt></ruby></u>、<u><ruby><rb>撒撒拿</rb><rt>Sah-sah-nô</rt></ruby></u>；<sup>32</sup> <u><ruby><rb>利巴弗</rb><rt>Li-pô-feh</rt></ruby></u>、<u><ruby><rb>实欣</rb><rt>Jih-hying</rt></ruby></u>、<u><ruby><rb>亚因</rb><rt>Üô-ing</rt></ruby></u>、<u><ruby><rb>临门</rb><rt>Ling-meng</rt></ruby></u>；<ruby><rb>共</rb><rt>gong</rt><rb>廿九</rb><rt>nyiæn-kyiu</rt><rb>座</rb><rt>zo</rt><rb>城子</rb><rt>dzing-ts</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其个</rb><rt>gyi-go</rt><rb>乡村</rb><rt>hyiang-tsʽeng</rt></ruby>。

<sup>33</sup> <ruby><rb>来</rb><rt>Læ</rt><rb>山下</rb><rt>sæn-ʽô</rt><rb>平地</rb><rt>bing-di</rt><rb>里</rb><rt>li</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以实陶</rb><rt>Yi-jih-dao</rt></ruby></u>、<u><ruby><rb>琐列</rb><rt>So-lih</rt></ruby></u>、<u><ruby><rb>亚实拿</rb><rt>Üô-jih-nô</rt></ruby></u>；<sup>34</sup> <u><ruby><rb>撒挪亚</rb><rt>Sah-nô-üô</rt></ruby></u>、<u><ruby><rb>隐干宁</rb><rt>Ing-ken-nying</rt></ruby></u>、<u><ruby><rb>大布亚</rb><rt>Da-pu-üô</rt></ruby></u>、<u><ruby><rb>以楠</rb><rt>Yi-nen</rt></ruby></u>；<sup>35</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶末</rb><rt>Yiæ-meh</rt></ruby></u>、<u><ruby><rb>亚土兰</rb><rt>Üô-tʽu-læn</rt></ruby></u>、<u><ruby><rb>梭哥</rb><rt>So-ko</rt></ruby></u>、<u><ruby><rb>亚西加</rb><rt>Üô-si-kyüô</rt></ruby></u>；<sup>36</sup> <u><ruby><rb>沙拉音</rb><rt>Sô-lah-ing</rt></ruby></u>、<u><ruby><rb>亚底大音</rb><rt>Üô-ti-da-ing</rt></ruby></u>、<u><ruby><rb>其底拉</rb><rt>Gyi-ti-lah</rt></ruby></u>、<u><ruby><rb>其底鲁田</rb><rt>Gyi-ti-lu-din</rt></ruby></u>；<ruby><rb>共</rb><rt>gong</rt><rb>十<u>四</rb><rt>jih-s</rt><rb>座</rb><rt>zo</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>城子</rb><rt>dzing-ts</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其个</rb><rt>gyi-go</rt><rb>乡村</rb><rt>hyiang-tsʽeng</rt></ruby>。<sup>37</sup> <u><ruby><rb>洗楠</rb><rt>Si-nen</rt></ruby></u>、<u><ruby><rb>哈大沙</rb><rt>Ha-da-sô</rt></ruby></u>、<u><ruby><rb>麦大甲</rb><rt>Mah-da-kyiah</rt></ruby></u>；<sup>38</sup> <u><ruby><rb>底连</rb><rt>Ti-lin</rt></ruby></u>、<u><ruby><rb>蜜是巴</rb><rt>Mih-z-pô</rt></ruby></u>、<u><ruby><rb>约帖</rb><rt>Iah-tʽiah</rt></ruby></u>；<sup>39</sup> <u><ruby><rb>拉吉</rb><rt>Lah-kyih</rt></ruby></u>、<u><ruby><rb>不甲</rb><rt>Peh-kyiah</rt></ruby></u>、<u><ruby><rb>厄伦</rb><rt>Ah-leng</rt></ruby></u>；<sup>40</sup> <u><ruby><rb>甲本</rb><rt>Kyiah-peng</rt></ruby></u>、<u><ruby><rb>拉曼</rb><rt>Lah-mæn</rt></ruby></u>、<u><ruby><rb>吉勒</rb><rt>Kyih-lah</rt></ruby></u>；<sup>41</sup> <u><ruby><rb>其得落</rb><rt>Gyi-teh-loh</rt></ruby></u>、<u><ruby><rb>伯大公</rb><rt>Pah-da-kong</rt></ruby></u>、<u><ruby><rb>拿玛</rb><rt>Nô-mô</rt></ruby></u>、<u><ruby><rb>玛基大</rb><rt>Mô-kyi-da</rt></ruby></u>；<ruby><rb>共</rb><rt>gong</rt><rb>十六</rb><rt>jih-loh</rt><rb>座</rb><rt>zo</rt><rb>城子</rb><rt>dzing-ts</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其个</rb><rt>gyi-go</rt><rb>乡村</rb><rt>hyiang-tsʽeng</rt></ruby>。

<sup>42</sup> <u><ruby><rb>立拿</rb><rt>Lih-nô</rt></ruby></u>、<u><ruby><rb>以帖</rb><rt>Yi-tʽiah</rt></ruby></u>、<u><ruby><rb>亚散</rb><rt>Üô-sæn</rt></ruby></u>；<sup>43</sup> <u><ruby><rb>益弗大</rb><rt>Ih-fah-da</rt></ruby></u>、<u><ruby><rb>亚实拿</rb><rt>Üô-jih-nô</rt></ruby></u>、<u><ruby><rb>尼悉</rb><rt>Nyi-sih</rt></ruby></u>；<sup>44</sup> <u><ruby><rb>基拉</rb><rt>Kyi-lah</rt></ruby></u>、<u><ruby><rb>亚革悉</rb><rt>Üô-keh-sih</rt></ruby></u>、<u><ruby><rb>米利沙</rb><rt>Mi-li-sô</rt></ruby></u>；<ruby><rb>共</rb><rt>gong</rt><rb>九座</rb><rt>kyiu-zo</rt><rb>城子</rb><rt>dzing-ts</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其个</rb><rt>gyi-go</rt><rb>乡村</rb><rt>hyiang-tsʽeng</rt></ruby>。

<sup>45</sup> <u><ruby><rb>以革伦</rb><rt>Yi-keh-leng</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>属</rb><rt>joh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以革伦</rb><rt>Yi-keh-leng</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>乡镇</rb><rt>hyiang-cing</rt><rb>村庄</rb><rt>tsʽeng-tsông</rt></ruby>；<sup>46</sup> <ruby><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>从</rb><rt>dzong</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以革伦</rb><rt>Yi-keh-leng</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>海</rb><rt>hæ</rt><rb>凡百</rb><rt>væn-pah</rt><rb>靠近</rb><rt>kʽao-gying</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>亚实突</u>个</rb><rt>Üô-jih-deh-go</rt><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>乡村</rb><rt>hyiang-tsʽeng</rt></ruby>。

<sup>47</sup> <u><ruby><rb>亚实突</rb><rt>Üô-jih-deh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>属</rb><rt>joh</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>亚实突</u>个</rb><rt>Üô-jih-deh-go</rt><rb>乡镇</rb><rt>hyiang-cing</rt><rb>村庄</rb><rt>tsʽeng-tsông</rt></ruby>；<u><ruby><rb>迦萨</rb><rt>Kyüô-sah</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>属</rb><rt>joh</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>迦萨</u>个</rb><rt>Kyüô-sah-go</rt><rb>乡镇</rb><rt>hyiang-cing</rt><rb>村庄</rb><rt>tsʽeng-tsông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>直到</rb><rt>dzih-tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>埃及</rb><rt>Yiæ-gyih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>河</rb><rt>ʽo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>还有</rb><rt>wa-yiu</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>大</rb><rt>Do</rt><rb>海</rb><rt>hæ</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>边沿</rb><rt>pin-yin</rt></ruby>。

<sup>48</sup> <ruby><rb>来</rb><rt>Læ</rt><rb>山地</rb><rt>sæn-di</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>沙密</rb><rt>Sô-mih</rt></ruby></u>、<u><ruby><rb>雅的</rb><rt>Yüô-tih</rt></ruby></u>、<u><ruby><rb>梭哥</rb><rt>So-ko</rt></ruby></u>；<sup>49</sup> <u><ruby><rb>大拿</rb><rt>Da-nô</rt></ruby></u>、<u><ruby><rb>基列撒拿</rb><rt>Kyi-lih-sah-nô</rt></ruby></u>，（<u><ruby><rb>基列撒拿</rb><rt>Kyi-lih-sah-nô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>底璧</rb><rt>Ti-pih</rt></ruby></u>；）<sup>50</sup> <u><ruby><rb>亚拿伯</rb><rt>Üô-nô-pah</rt></ruby></u>、<u><ruby><rb>以实提莫</rb><rt>Yi-jih-di-moh</rt></ruby></u>、<u><ruby><rb>亚念</rb><rt>Üô-nyiæn</rt></ruby></u>；<sup>51</sup> <u><ruby><rb>坷山</rb><rt>Kʽo-sæn</rt></ruby></u> <u><ruby><rb>何伦</rb><rt>ʽO-leng</rt></ruby></u>、<u><ruby><rb>其录</rb><rt>Gyi-loh</rt></ruby></u>；<ruby><rb>共</rb><rt>gong</rt><rb>十一</rb><rt>jih-ih</rt><rb>座</rb><rt>zo</rt><rb>城子</rb><rt>dzing-ts</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其个</rb><rt>gyi-go</rt><rb>乡村</rb><rt>hyiang-tsʽeng</rt></ruby>。

<sup>52</sup> <u><ruby><rb>亚拉</rb><rt>Üô-lah</rt></ruby></u>、<u><ruby><rb>土玛</rb><rt>Tʽu-mô</rt></ruby></u>、<u><ruby><rb>以珊</rb><rt>Yi-sæn</rt></ruby></u>；<sup>53</sup> <u><ruby><rb>雅农</rb><rt>Yüô-nong</rt></ruby></u>、<u><ruby><rb>伯大布亚</rb><rt>Pah-da-pu-üô</rt></ruby></u>、<u><ruby><rb>亚非迦</rb><rt>Üô-fi-kyüô</rt></ruby></u>；<sup>54</sup> <u><ruby><rb>训大</rb><rt>Hyüing-da</rt></ruby></u>、<u><ruby><rb>基列亚巴</rb><rt>Kyi-lih-üô-pô</rt></ruby></u>，（<u><ruby><rb>基列亚巴</rb><rt>Kyi-lih-üô-pô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>希伯仑</rb><rt>Hyi-pah-leng</rt></ruby></u>；）<ruby><rb>还有</rb><rt>wa-yiu</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>洗阿珥</rb><rt>Si-o-r</rt></ruby></u>；<ruby><rb>共</rb><rt>gong</rt><rb>九座</rb><rt>kyiu-zo</rt><rb>城子</rb><rt>dzing-ts</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其个</rb><rt>gyi-go</rt><rb>乡村</rb><rt>hyiang-tsʽeng</rt></ruby>。<sup>55</sup> <u><ruby><rb>玛云</rb><rt>Mô-yüing</rt></ruby></u>、<u><ruby><rb>迦密</rb><rt>Kyüô-mih</rt></ruby></u>、<u><ruby><rb>西弗</rb><rt>Si-feh</rt></ruby></u>、<u><ruby><rb>若大</rb><rt>Ziah-da</rt></ruby></u>；<sup>56</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶斯列</rb><rt>Yiæ-s-lih</rt></ruby></u>、<u><ruby><rb>约甸</rb><rt>Iah-din</rt></ruby></u>、<u><ruby><rb>撒挪亚</rb><rt>Sah-nô-üô</rt></ruby></u>；<sup>57</sup> <u><ruby><rb>该隐</rb><rt>Kæ-ing</rt></ruby></u>、<u><ruby><rb>其比亚</rb><rt>Gyi-pi-üô</rt></ruby></u>、<u><ruby><rb>亭拿</rb><rt>Ding-nô</rt></ruby></u>；<ruby><rb>共</rb><rt>gong</rt><rb>十座</rb><rt>jih-zo</rt><rb>城子</rb><rt>dzing-ts</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其个</rb><rt>gyi-go</rt><rb>乡村</rb><rt>hyiang-tsʽeng</rt></ruby>。

<sup>58</sup> <u><ruby><rb>哈忽</rb><rt>Ha-hweh</rt></ruby></u>、<u><ruby><rb>伯夙</rb><rt>Pah-soh</rt></ruby></u>、<u><ruby><rb>其特</rb><rt>Gyi-deh</rt></ruby></u>；<sup>59</sup> <u><ruby><rb>玛拉</rb><rt>Mô-lah</rt></ruby></u>、<u><ruby><rb>伯亚诺</rb><rt>Pah-üô-noh</rt></ruby></u>、<u><ruby><rb>利提君</rb><rt>Li-di-kyüing</rt></ruby></u>；<ruby><rb>共</rb><rt>gong</rt><rb>六座</rb><rt>loh-zo</rt><rb>城子</rb><rt>dzing-ts</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其个</rb><rt>gyi-go</rt><rb>乡村</rb><rt>hyiang-tsʽeng</rt></ruby>。

<sup>60</sup> <u><ruby><rb>基列巴力</rb><rt>Kyi-lih-pô-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>基列耶琳</rb><rt>Kyi-lih-yiæ-ling</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>拉巴</rb><rt>Lah-pô</rt></ruby></u>；<ruby><rb>两座</rb><rt>liang-zo</rt><rb>城子</rb><rt>dzing-ts</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其个</rb><rt>gyi-go</rt><rb>乡村</rb><rt>hyiang-tsʽeng</rt></ruby>。

<sup>61</sup> <ruby><rb>来</rb><rt>Læ</rt><rb>旷野</rb><rt>kwʽông-iæ</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>伯亚拉巴</rb><rt>Pah-üô-lah-pô</rt></ruby></u>、<u><ruby><rb>麦丁</rb><rt>Mah-ting</rt></ruby></u>、<u><ruby><rb>西迦迦</rb><rt>Si-kyüô-kyüô</rt></ruby></u>；<sup>62</sup> <u><ruby><rb>匿珊</rb><rt>Nyih-sæn</rt></ruby></u>、<u><ruby><rb>盐</rb><rt>Yin</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>城</rb><rt>dzing</rt></ruby>、<u><ruby><rb>隐其底</rb><rt>Ing-gyi-ti</rt></ruby></u>；<ruby><rb>共</rb><rt>gong</rt><rb>六</rb><rt>loh</rt><rb>座</rb><rt>zo</rt><rb>城子</rb><rt>dzing-ts</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其个</rb><rt>gyi-go</rt><rb>乡村</rb><rt>hyiang-tsʽeng</rt></ruby>。

<sup>63</sup> <ruby><rb>庉</rb><rt>Deng</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶路撒冷</rb><rt>Yiæ-lu-sah-leng</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶布士</rb><rt>Yiæ-pu-z</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>，<u><ruby><rb>犹大</rb><rt>Yiu-da</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>子孙</rb><rt>ts-seng</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>能够</rb><rt>neng-keo</rt><rb>赶</rb><rt>ken</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>出</rb><rt>cʽih</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>所以</rb><rt>sô-yi</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶布士</rb><rt>Yiæ-pu-z</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>犹大</rb><rt>Yiu-da</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>子孙</rb><rt>ts-seng</rt><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>庉</rb><rt>deng</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶路撒冷</rb><rt>Yiæ-lu-sah-leng</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>直到</rb><rt>dzih-tao</rt><rb>如今</rb><rt>jü-kying</rt></ruby>。

---

### Is. XVI.

<u><ruby><rb>约瑟</rb><rt>IAH-SEH</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>子孙</rb><rt>ts-seng</rt><rb>抽签</rb><rt>tsʽiu-tsʽin</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>得着个</rb><rt>teh-djoh-go</rt><rb>地</rb><rt>di</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>从</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>靠近</rb><rt>kʽao-gying</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶利哥</rb><rt>Yiæ-li-ko</rt></ruby></u> <u><ruby><rb>约但</rb><rt>Iah-dæn</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>河</rb><rt>ʽo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶利哥</rb><rt>Yiæ-li-ko</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>东边个</rb><rt>tong-pin-go</rt><rb>水源</rb><rt>shü-nyün</rt><rb>起</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>从</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>旷野</rb><rt>kwʽông-iæ</rt><rb>上</rb><rt>zông</rt><rb>山地</rb><rt>sæn-di</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>伯特利</rb><rt>Pah-deh-li</rt></ruby></u>；<sup>2</sup> <ruby><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>从</rb><rt>dzong</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>伯特利</rb><rt>Pah-deh-li</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>路斯</rb><rt>Lu-s</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>过到</rb><rt>ko-tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>亚其</rb><rt>Üô-gyi</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>地界个</rb><rt>di-ka-go</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>亚大录</rb><rt>Üô-da-loh</rt></ruby></u>；<sup>3</sup> <ruby><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>向</rb><rt>hyiang</rt><rb>西</rb><rt>si</rt><rb>落去</rb><rt>loh-kyʽi</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>押利底</rb><rt>Iah-li-ti</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人个</rb><rt>nying-go</rt><rb>地界</rb><rt>di-ka</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>下</rb><rt>ʽô</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>伯和仑</u>个</rb><rt>Pah-ʽo-leng-go</rt><rb>地界</rb><rt>di-ka</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>直到</rb><rt>dzih-tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>基色</rb><rt>Kyi-seh</rt></ruby></u>；<ruby><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>海</rb><rt>hæ</rt><rb>为</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>尽头</rb><rt>zing-deo</rt></ruby>。<sup>4</sup> <u><ruby><rb>约瑟</rb><rt>Iah-seh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>子孙</rb><rt>ts-seng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>玛拿西</rb><rt>Mô-nô-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以法莲</rb><rt>Yi-fah-lin</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>得着</rb><rt>teh-djoh</rt><rb>其拉个</rb><rt>gyi-lah-go</rt><rb>产业</rb><rt>tsʽæn-nyih</rt></ruby>。<sup>5</sup> <u><ruby><rb>以法莲</rb><rt>Yi-fah-lin</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>子孙个</rb><rt>ts-seng-go</rt><rb>地界</rb><rt>di-ka</rt><rb>照</rb><rt>tsiao</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>房份</rb><rt>vông-veng</rt><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt></ruby>：<ruby><rb>其拉个</rb><rt>gyi-lah-go</rt><rb>产业</rb><rt>tsʽæn-nyih</rt><rb>东边</rb><rt>tong-pin</rt><rb>地界</rb><rt>di-ka</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>亚大录亚达</rb><rt>Üô-da-loh-üô-deh</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>直到</rb><rt>dzih-tao</rt><rb>上</rb><rt>zông</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>伯和仑</rb><rt>Pah-ʽo-leng</rt></ruby></u>：<sup>6</sup> <ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>地界</rb><rt>di-ka</rt><rb>向</rb><rt>hyiang</rt><rb>西</rb><rt>si</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>密米大</rb><rt>Mih-mi-da</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>北边</rb><rt>poh-pin</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>向</rb><rt>hyiang</rt><rb>东</rb><rt>tong</rt><rb>绕转</rb><rt>nyiao-cün</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>大纳示罗</rb><rt>Da-neh-z-lo</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>过去</rb><rt>ko-kyʽi</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>雅挪哈</rb><rt>Yüô-nô-ha</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>东边</rb><rt>tong-pin</rt></ruby>：<sup>7</sup> <ruby><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>从</rb><rt>dzong</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>雅挪哈</rb><rt>Yüô-nô-ha</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>落去</rb><rt>loh-kyʽi</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>亚大录</rb><rt>Üô-da-loh</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>拿拉</rb><rt>Nô-lah</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>直到</rb><rt>dzih-tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶利哥</rb><rt>Yiæ-li-ko</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>约但</rb><rt>Iah-dæn</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>河</rb><rt>ʽo</rt><rb>为</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>尽头</rb><rt>zing-deo</rt></ruby>。<sup>8</sup> <ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>地界</rb><rt>di-ka</rt><rb>从</rb><rt>dzong</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>大布亚</rb><rt>Da-pu-üô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>向</rb><rt>hyiang</rt><rb>西</rb><rt>si</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>加拿</rb><rt>Kyüô-nô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>河</rb><rt>ʽo</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>海</rb><rt>hæ</rt><rb>为</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>尽头</rb><rt>zing-deo</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>Keh</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以法莲</rb><rt>Yi-fah-lin</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>子孙个</rb><rt>ts-seng-go</rt><rb>支派</rb><rt>ts-pʽa</rt><rb>照</rb><rt>tsiao</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>房份</rb><rt>vông-veng</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>得着个</rb><rt>teh-djoh-go</rt><rb>产业</rb><rt>tsʽæn-nyih</rt></ruby>。<sup>9</sup> <ruby><rb>另外</rb><rt>Ling-nga</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>玛拿西</rb><rt>Mô-nô-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>子孙个</rb><rt>ts-seng-go</rt><rb>产业</rb><rt>tsʽæn-nyih</rt><rb>中央</rb><rt>cong-nyiang</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>几座</rb><rt>kyi-zo</rt><rb>城子</rb><rt>dzing-ts</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>属</rb><rt>joh</rt><rb>其个</rb><rt>gyi-go</rt><rb>乡村</rb><rt>hyiang-tsʽeng</rt><rb>分出来</rb><rt>feng-cʽih-læ</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以法莲</rb><rt>Yi-fah-lin</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>子孙个</rb><rt>ts-seng-go</rt></ruby>。<sup>10</sup> <ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt><rb>呒得</rb><rt>m-neh</rt><rb>赶出</rb><rt>ken-cʽih</rt><rb>庉</rb><rt>deng</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>基色</u>个</rb><rt>Kyi-seh-go</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>迦南</rb><rt>Kyüô-nen</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>但是</rb><rt>dæn-z</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>迦南</rb><rt>Kyüô-nen</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>庉</rb><rt>deng</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以法莲</rb><rt>Yi-fah-lin</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>中央</rb><rt>cong-nyiang</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>如今</rb><rt>jü-kying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>当</rb><rt>tông</rt><rb>苦差个</rb><rt>kwʽu-tsʽa-go</rt><rb>奴仆</rb><rt>nu-boh</rt></ruby>。

---

### Is. XVII.

<u><ruby><rb>约瑟</rb><rt>IAH-SEH</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>长子</rb><rt>tsiang-ts</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>玛拿西</rb><rt>Mô-nô-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>抽签</rb><rt>tsʽiu-tsʽin</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>得着个</rb><rt>teh-djoh-go</rt><rb>地</rb><rt>di</rt><rb>像</rb><rt>ziang</rt><rb>下文</rb><rt>ʽô-veng</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt></ruby>。<u><ruby><rb>玛拿西</rb><rt>Mô-nô-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>长子</rb><rt>tsiang-ts</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>基列</u>个</rb><rt>Kyi-lih-go</rt><rb>阿爹</rb><rt>ah-tia</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>玛吉</rb><rt>Mô-kyih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>得着</rb><rt>teh-djoh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>基列</rb><rt>Kyi-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>巴珊</rb><rt>Pô-sæn</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>因为</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>一个</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>好佬</rb><rt>hao-lao</rt></ruby>。<sup>2</sup> <ruby><rb>还有</rb><rt>Wa-yiu</rt><rb>分</rb><rt>feng</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>玛拿西</rb><rt>Mô-nô-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>余多</rb><rt>yü-to</rt><rb>子孙</rb><rt>ts-seng</rt><rb>照</rb><rt>tsiao</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>房份</rb><rt>vông-veng</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>亚比以泄</rb><rt>Üô-pi-yi-sih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>子孙</rb><rt>ts-seng</rt></ruby>、<u><ruby><rb>希勒</rb><rt>Hyi-lah</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>子孙</rb><rt>ts-seng</rt></ruby>、<u><ruby><rb>亚士列</rb><rt>Üô-z-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>子孙</rb><rt>ts-seng</rt></ruby>、<u><ruby><rb>示剑</rb><rt>Z-kyin</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>子孙</rb><rt>ts-seng</rt></ruby>、<u><ruby><rb>希弗</rb><rt>Hyi-feh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>子孙</rb><rt>ts-seng</rt></ruby>、<u><ruby><rb>示米大</rb><rt>Z-mi-da</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>子孙</rb><rt>ts-seng</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>约瑟</rb><rt>Iah-seh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>儿子</rb><rt>ng-ts</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>玛拿西</u>个</rb><rt>Mô-nô-si-go</rt><rb>男丁</rb><rt>nen-ting</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>照</rb><rt>tsiao</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>房份</rb><rt>vông-veng</rt></ruby>。<sup>3</sup> <ruby><rb>但是</rb><rt>Dæn-z</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>玛拿西</rb><rt>Mô-nô-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>玄孙</rb><rt>yün-seng</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>玛吉</u>个</rb><rt>Mô-kyih-go</rt><rb>曾孙</rb><rt>tseng-seng</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb><u>基列</u>个</rb><rt>Kyi-lih-go</rt><rb>孙子</rb><rt>seng-ts</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>希弗</rb><rt>Hyi-feh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>儿子</rb><rt>ng-ts</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>西罗非哈</rb><rt>Si-lo-fi-ha</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>呒得</rb><rt>m-neh</rt><rb>儿子</rb><rt>ng-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>只有</rb><rt>tsih-yiu</rt><rb>囡</rb><rt>nön</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>其个</rb><rt>gyi-go</rt><rb>囡</rb><rt>nön</rt><rb>名字</rb><rt>ming-z</rt><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>玛拉</rb><rt>Mô-lah</rt></ruby></u>、<u><ruby><rb>挪亚</rb><rt>Nô-üô</rt></ruby></u>、<u><ruby><rb>曷拉</rb><rt>Eh-lah</rt></ruby></u>、<u><ruby><rb>密迦</rb><rt>Mih-kyüô</rt></ruby></u>、<u><ruby><rb>得撒</rb><rt>Teh-sah</rt></ruby></u>。<sup>4</sup> <ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt><rb>走拢</rb><rt>tseo-long</rt><rb>祭司</rb><rt>tsi-s</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以利亚撒</rb><rt>Yi-li-üô-sah</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>嫩</u>个</rb><rt>Neng-go</rt><rb>儿子</rb><rt>ng-ts</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>约书亚</rb><rt>Iah-shü-üô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>牧伯</rb><rt>moh-pah</rt><rb>面前</rb><rt>min-zin</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>耶和华</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>吩咐</rb><rt>feng-fu</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>赐拨</rb><rt>s-peh</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>产业</rb><rt>tsʽæn-nyih</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>兄弟</rb><rt>hyüong-di</rt><rb>中央</rb><rt>cong-nyiang</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>所以</rb><rt>sô-yi</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>约书亚</rb><rt>Iah-shü-üô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>照</rb><rt>tsiao</rt><rb>耶和华个</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô-go</rt><rb>吩咐</rb><rt>feng-fu</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>赐拨</rb><rt>s-peh</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>产业</rb><rt>tsʽæn-nyih</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>叔伯</rb><rt>shoh-pah</rt><rb>中央</rb><rt>cong-nyiang</rt></ruby>。」<sup>5</sup> <ruby><rb>除</rb><rt>Djü</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>约但</rb><rt>Iah-dæn</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>河</rb><rt>ʽo</rt><rb>箇边个</rb><rt>keh-pin-go</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>基列</rb><rt>Kyi-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>巴珊</rb><rt>Pô-sæn</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>地</rb><rt>di</rt><rb>之外</rb><rt>ts-nga</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>玛拿西</rb><rt>Mô-nô-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>抽签</rb><rt>tsʽiu-tsʽin</rt><rb>得着</rb><rt>teh-djoh</rt><rb>十股</rb><rt>jih-kwu</rt></ruby>；<sup>6</sup> <ruby><rb>因为</rb><rt>ing-we</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>玛拿西</rb><rt>Mô-nô-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>孙女</rb><rt>seng-nyü</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>玛拿西</rb><rt>Mô-nô-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>子孙</rb><rt>ts-seng</rt><rb>中央</rb><rt>cong-nyiang</rt><rb>得着</rb><rt>teh-djoh</rt><rb>产业</rb><rt>tsʽæn-nyih</rt></ruby>；<u><ruby><rb>基列</rb><rt>Kyi-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>地</rb><rt>di</rt><rb>归</rb><rt>kwe</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>玛拿西</rb><rt>Mô-nô-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>余多</rb><rt>yü-to</rt><rb>子孙</rb><rt>ts-seng</rt></ruby>。<sup>7</sup> <u><ruby><rb>玛拿西</rb><rt>Mô-nô-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>地界</rb><rt>di-ka</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>从</rb><rt>dzong</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>亚设</rb><rt>Üô-shih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>示剑</rb><rt>Z-kyin</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>面前个</rb><rt>min-zin-go</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>密米大</rb><rt>Mih-mi-da</rt></ruby></u>：<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>地界</rb><rt>di-ka</rt><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>弯</rb><rt>w̆æn</rt><rb>顺手边</rb><rt>jing-siu-pin</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>庉</rb><rt>deng</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>隐大布亚</rb><rt>Ing-da-pu-üô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人个</rb><rt>nying-go</rt><rb>地</rb><rt>di</rt></ruby>。<sup>8</sup> <u><ruby><rb>大布亚</rb><rt>Da-pu-üô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>地</rb><rt>di</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>归</rb><rt>kwe</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>玛拿西</rb><rt>Mô-nô-si</rt></ruby></u>；<ruby><rb>但是</rb><rt>dæn-z</rt><rb>靠近</rb><rt>kʽao-gying</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>玛拿西</rb><rt>Mô-nô-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>地界</rb><rt>di-ka</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>大布亚</rb><rt>Da-pu-üô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>城子</rb><rt>dzing-ts</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>归</rb><rt>kwe</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以法莲</rb><rt>Yi-fah-lin</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>子孙</rb><rt>ts-seng</rt></ruby>。<sup>9</sup> <ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>地界</rb><rt>di-ka</rt><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>落</rb><rt>loh</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>加拿</rb><rt>Kyüô-nô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>河</rb><rt>ʽo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>河个</rb><rt>ʽo-go</rt><rb>南边</rb><rt>nen-pin</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>箇星</rb><rt>Keh-sing</rt><rb>城子</rb><rt>dzing-ts</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>玛拿西</rb><rt>Mô-nô-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>城子</rb><rt>dzing-ts</rt><rb>中央</rb><rt>cong-nyiang</rt><rb>归</rb><rt>kwe</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以法莲</rb><rt>Yi-fah-lin</rt></ruby></u>，<u><ruby><rb>玛拿西</rb><rt>Mô-nô-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>地界</rb><rt>di-ka</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>河</rb><rt>ʽo</rt><rb>北边</rb><rt>poh-pin</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>海</rb><rt>hæ</rt><rb>为</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>尽头</rb><rt>zing-deo</rt></ruby>。<sup>10</sup> <ruby><rb>南边</rb><rt>Nen-pin</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>属</rb><rt>joh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以法莲</rb><rt>Yi-fah-lin</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>北边</rb><rt>poh-pin</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>属</rb><rt>joh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>玛拿西</rb><rt>Mô-nô-si</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>海</rb><rt>hæ</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>其个</rb><rt>gyi-go</rt><rb>边界</rb><rt>pin-ka</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>北边</rb><rt>poh-pin</rt><rb>接着</rb><rt>tsih-djoh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>亚设</rb><rt>Üô-shih</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>东边</rb><rt>tong-pin</rt><rb>接着</rb><rt>tsih-djoh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以萨迦</rb><rt>Yi-sah-kyüô</rt></ruby></u>。<sup>11</sup> <u><ruby><rb>玛拿西</rb><rt>Mô-nô-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以萨迦</rb><rt>Yi-sah-kyüô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>亚设</rb><rt>Üô-shih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>对着</rb><rt>te-djoh</rt><rb>城子</rb><rt>dzing-ts</rt><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>伯善</rb><rt>Pah-jün</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其个</rb><rt>gyi-go</rt><rb>乡村</rb><rt>hyiang-tsʽeng</rt></ruby>，<u><ruby><rb>以伯莲</rb><rt>Yi-pah-lin</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其个</rb><rt>gyi-go</rt><rb>乡村</rb><rt>hyiang-tsʽeng</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>庉</rb><rt>deng</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>多珥个</rb><rt>To-r-go</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>百姓</rb><rt>pah-sing</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其个</rb><rt>gyi-go</rt><rb>乡村</rb><rt>hyiang-tsʽeng</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>还有</rb><rt>wa-yiu</rt><rb>庉</rb><rt>deng</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>隐多珥</u>个</rb><rt>Ing-to-r-go</rt><rb>百姓</rb><rt>pah-sing</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其个</rb><rt>gyi-go</rt><rb>乡村</rb><rt>hyiang-tsʽeng</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>庉</rb><rt>deng</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>大纳</u>个</rb><rt>Da-neh-go</rt><rb>百姓</rb><rt>pah-sing</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其个</rb><rt>gyi-go</rt><rb>乡村</rb><rt>hyiang-tsʽeng</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>庉</rb><rt>deng</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>米吉多</u>个</rb><rt>Mi-kyih-to-go</rt><rb>百姓</rb><rt>pah-sing</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其个</rb><rt>gyi-go</rt><rb>乡村</rb><rt>hyiang-tsʽeng</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>三塔</rb><rt>sæn-tʽah</rt><rb>庉</rb><rt>deng</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>山顶个</rb><rt>sæn-teng-go</rt><rb>城子</rb><rt>dzing-ts</rt></ruby>。<sup>12</sup> <ruby><rb>但是</rb><rt>Dæn-z</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>玛拿西</rb><rt>Mô-nô-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>子孙</rb><rt>ts-seng</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>能够</rb><rt>neng-keo</rt><rb>赶出</rb><rt>ken-cʽih</rt><rb>庉</rb><rt>deng</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>城子个</rb><rt>dzing-ts-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>；<u><ruby><rb>迦南</rb><rt>Kyüô-nen</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>定坚</rb><rt>ding-kyin</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>庉</rb><rt>deng</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>箇个</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>地</rb><rt>di</rt></ruby>。<sup>13</sup> <ruby><rb>到了</rb><rt>Tao-leh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>强盛</rb><rt>gyiang-zing</rt><rb>时候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>迦南</rb><rt>Kyüô-nen</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>当</rb><rt>tông</rt><rb>苦差</rb><rt>kwʽu-tsʽa</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>呒得</rb><rt>m-neh</rt><rb>拢总</rb><rt>long-tsong</rt><rb>赶出</rb><rt>ken-cʽih</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>。<sup>14</sup> <u><ruby><rb>约瑟</rb><rt>Iah-seh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>子孙</rb><rt>ts-seng</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>约书亚</rb><rt>Iah-shü-üô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>耶和华</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>如今</rb><rt>jü-kying</rt><rb>赐</rb><rt>s</rt><rb>福气</rb><rt>foh-kyʽi</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>成为</rb><rt>dzing-we</rt><rb>一个</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>大族</rb><rt>do-dzoh</rt><rb>百姓</rb><rt>pah-sing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>咋会</rb><rt>dza-we</rt><rb>只</rb><rt>tsih</rt><rb>分</rb><rt>feng</rt><rb>一支</rb><rt>ih-ts</rt><rb>签</rb><rt>tsʽin</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>得着</rb><rt>teh-djoh</rt><rb>一股</rb><rt>ih-kwu</rt><rb>地</rb><rt>di</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>产业</rb><rt>tsʽæn-nyih</rt><rb>呢</rb><rt>ni</rt></ruby>？」<sup>15</sup> <u><ruby><rb>约书亚</rb><rt>Iah-shü-üô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>尔</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>如果</rb><rt>jü-ko</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>一个</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>大族</rb><rt>do-dzoh</rt></ruby>，<u><ruby><rb>以法莲</rb><rt>Yi-fah-lin</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>山地</rb><rt>sæn-di</rt><rb>于</rb><rt>ü</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>忒</rb><rt>tʽeh</rt><rb>狭窄</rb><rt>ʽah-tsah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>走上</rb><rt>tseo-zông</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>树木个</rb><rt>jü-moh-go</rt><rb>地里</rb><rt>di-li</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>比利洗</rb><rt>Pi-li-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>利乏音</u>个</rb><rt>Li-vah-ing-go</rt><rb>地</rb><rt>di</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>箇头</rb><rt>keh-deo</rt><rb>为了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt><rb>自</rb><rt>zi</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>斫掉</rb><rt>coh-diao</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>树木</rb><rt>jü-moh</rt></ruby>。」<sup>16</sup> <u><ruby><rb>约瑟</rb><rt>Iah-seh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>子孙</rb><rt>ts-seng</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>Keh</rt><rb>山地</rb><rt>sæn-di</rt><rb>于</rb><rt>ü</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>弗够</rb><rt>feh-keo</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>并且</rb><rt>ping-tsʽia</rt><rb>庉</rb><rt>deng</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>山岙</rb><rt>sæn-ao</rt><rb>里个</rb><rt>li-go</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>迦南</rb><rt>Kyüô-nen</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>庉</rb><rt>deng</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>伯善</rb><rt>Pah-jün</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>乡村个</rb><rt>hyiang-tsʽeng-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>还有</rb><rt>wa-yiu</rt><rb>庉</rb><rt>deng</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶斯列</rb><rt>Yiæ-s-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>山岙里</rb><rt>sæn-ao-li</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>铁个</rb><rt>tʽih-go</rt><rb>车子</rb><rt>tsʽô-ts</rt></ruby>。」<sup>17</sup> <u><ruby><rb>约书亚</rb><rt>Iah-shü-üô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>约瑟</rb><rt>Iah-seh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>族</rb><rt>dzoh</rt><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以法莲</rb><rt>Yi-fah-lin</rt></ruby></u> <u><ruby><rb>玛拿西</rb><rt>Mô-nô-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>尔</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>一个</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>大族</rb><rt>do-dzoh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>大</rb><rt>do</rt><rb>能干个</rb><rt>neng-ken-go</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>单只</rb><rt>tæn-tsih</rt><rb>得着</rb><rt>teh-djoh</rt><rb>一支</rb><rt>ih-ts</rt><rb>签个</rb><rt>tsʽin-go</rt><rb>地</rb><rt>di</rt></ruby>，<sup>18</sup> <ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>山地</rb><rt>sæn-di</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>必定</rb><rt>pih-ding</rt><rb>归</rb><rt>kwe</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>虽即</rb><rt>se-tsih</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>树林</rb><rt>jü-ling</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>斫</rb><rt>coh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>掉</rb><rt>diao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>直到</rb><rt>dzih-tao</rt><rb>尽头</rb><rt>zing-deo</rt><rb>归</rb><rt>kwe</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>因为</rb><rt>ing-we</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>迦南</rb><rt>Kyüô-nen</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>虽即</rb><rt>se-tsih</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>铁</rb><rt>tʽih</rt><rb>车子</rb><rt>tsʽô-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>强盛个</rb><rt>gyiang-zing-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>必定</rb><rt>pih-ding</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>赶</rb><rt>ken</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>出</rb><rt>cʽih</rt></ruby>。」

---

### Is. XVIII.

<u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>YI-SEH-LIH</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>全</rb><rt>djün</rt><rb>公会</rb><rt>kong-we</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>聚集</rb><rt>jü-jih</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>示罗</rb><rt>Z-lo</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>箇头</rb><rt>keh-deo</rt><rb>立</rb><rt>lih</rt><rb>公会</rb><rt>kong-we</rt><rb>帐篷</rb><rt>tsiang-bong</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>箇个</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>地</rb><rt>di</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>面前</rb><rt>min-zin</rt><rb>被</rb><rt>be</rt><rb>制服</rb><rt>cü-voh</rt><rb>了</rb><rt>de</rt></ruby>。<sup>2</sup> <u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>子孙</rb><rt>ts-seng</rt><rb>还有</rb><rt>wa-yiu</rt><rb>七个</rb><rt>tsʽih-go</rt><rb>支派</rb><rt>ts-pʽa</rt><rb>未曾</rb><rt>vi-dzeng</rt><rb>分</rb><rt>feng</rt><rb>产业</rb><rt>tsʽæn-nyih</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt></ruby>。<sup>3</sup> <u><ruby><rb>约书亚</rb><rt>Iah-shü-üô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>子孙</rb><rt>ts-seng</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>耶和华</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>列祖个</rb><rt>lih-tsu-go</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>赐拨</rb><rt>s-peh</rt><rb>尔个</rb><rt>ng-go</rt><rb>地</rb><rt>di</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>耽搁</rb><rt>tæn-koh</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>进去</rb><rt>tsing-kyʽi</rt><rb>得着</rb><rt>teh-djoh</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>产业</rb><rt>tsʽæn-nyih</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>几时</rb><rt>kyi-z</rt><rb>呢</rb><rt>ni</rt></ruby>？<sup>4</sup> <ruby><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>Ng-lah</rt><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>为了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt><rb>每</rb><rt>me</rt><rb>支派</rb><rt>ts-pʽa</rt><rb>挑选</rb><rt>tʽiao-shün</rt><rb>三个</rb><rt>sæn-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>差</rb><rt>tsʽa</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>起来</rb><rt>kyʽi-læ</rt><rb>走过</rb><rt>tseo-ko</rt><rb>箇个</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>地</rb><rt>di</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>照</rb><rt>tsiao</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>产业个</rb><rt>tsʽæn-nyih-go</rt><rb>地</rb><rt>di</rt><rb>写出来</rb><rt>sia-cʽih-læ</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>走转</rb><rt>tseo-cün</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>。<sup>5</sup> <ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>分</rb><rt>feng</rt><rb>七股生</rb><rt>tsʽih-kwu-sang</rt></ruby>；<u><ruby><rb>犹大</rb><rt>Yiu-da</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>庉</rb><rt>deng</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>境界</rb><rt>kying-ka</rt><rb>里向</rb><rt>li-hyiang</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>南边</rb><rt>nen-pin</rt></ruby>，<u><ruby><rb>约瑟</rb><rt>Iah-seh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>一家</rb><rt>ih-kô</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>庉</rb><rt>deng</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>境界</rb><rt>kying-ka</rt><rb>里向</rb><rt>li-hyiang</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>北边</rb><rt>poh-pin</rt></ruby>。<sup>6</sup> <ruby><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>Ng-lah</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>把</rb><rt>pô</rt><rb>地</rb><rt>di</rt><rb>分</rb><rt>feng</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>七股</rb><rt>tsʽih-kwu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>写</rb><rt>sia</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>出来</rb><rt>cʽih-læ</rt><rb>带拨</rb><rt>ta-peh</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>此地</rb><rt>tsʽ-di</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>耶和华</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>神明个</rb><rt>Jing-ming-go</rt><rb>面前</rb><rt>min-zin</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>抽签</rb><rt>tsʽiu-tsʽin</rt></ruby>。<sup>7</sup> <ruby><rb>因为</rb><rt>Ing-we</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>利未</rb><rt>Li-vi</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>中央</rb><rt>cong-nyiang</rt><rb>呒得</rb><rt>m-neh</rt><rb>受</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>产业</rb><rt>tsʽæn-nyih</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>耶和华</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>祭司个</rb><rt>tsi-s-go</rt><rb>职份</rb><rt>tsih-veng</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>其拉个</rb><rt>gyi-lah-go</rt><rb>产业</rb><rt>tsʽæn-nyih</rt></ruby>；<u><ruby><rb>迦得</rb><rt>Kyüô-teh</rt></ruby></u>、<u><ruby><rb>流便</rb><rt>Liu-bin</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>玛拿西</rb><rt>Mô-nô-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>半</rb><rt>pun</rt><rb>支派</rb><rt>ts-pʽa</rt><rb>产业</rb><rt>tsʽæn-nyih</rt><rb>已经</rb><rt>yi-kying</rt><rb>得着</rb><rt>teh-djoh</rt><rb>了</rb><rt>de</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>约但</rb><rt>Iah-dæn</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>东边</rb><rt>tong-pin</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>耶和华个</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô-go</rt><rb>奴仆</rb><rt>nu-boh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>赐拨</rb><rt>s-peh</rt><rb>其拉个</rb><rt>gyi-lah-go</rt></ruby>。」<sup>8</sup> <ruby><rb>箇星</rb><rt>Keh-sing</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>动身</rb><rt>dong-sing</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<u><ruby><rb>约书亚</rb><rt>Iah-shü-üô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>吩咐</rb><rt>feng-fu</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>写</rb><rt>sia</rt><rb>地理</rb><rt>di-li</rt><rb>书个</rb><rt>shü-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>去</rb><rt>Kyʽi</rt><rb>走过</rb><rt>tseo-ko</rt><rb>箇个</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>地</rb><rt>di</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>写</rb><rt>sia</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>地理</rb><rt>di-li</rt><rb>书</rb><rt>shü</rt><rb>再</rb><rt>tsæ</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>之地</rb><rt>ts-di</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>示罗</rb><rt>Z-lo</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt><rb>耶和华</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>面前</rb><rt>min-zin</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>抽签</rb><rt>tsʽiu-tsʽin</rt></ruby>。」<sup>9</sup> <ruby><rb>箇星</rb><rt>Keh-sing</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>走过</rb><rt>tseo-ko</rt><rb>箇个</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>地</rb><rt>di</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>照</rb><rt>tsiao</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>各</rb><rt>koh</rt><rb>城子</rb><rt>dzing-ts</rt><rb>写出来</rb><rt>sia-cʽih-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>分</rb><rt>feng</rt><rb>七股</rb><rt>tsʽih-kwu</rt><rb>写</rb><rt>sia</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>书里</rb><rt>shü-li</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>归到</rb><rt>kyü-tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>示罗</rb><rt>Z-lo</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>营寨</rb><rt>ying-za</rt><rb>见</rb><rt>kyin</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>约书亚</rb><rt>Iah-shü-üô</rt></ruby></u>。<sup>10</sup> <u><ruby><rb>约书亚</rb><rt>Iah-shü-üô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>示罗</rb><rt>Z-lo</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>耶和华</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>面前</rb><rt>min-zin</rt><rb>待</rb><rt>dæ</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>抽签</rb><rt>tsʽiu-tsʽin</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>箇头</rb><rt>keh-deo</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>约书亚</rb><rt>Iah-shü-üô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>分</rb><rt>feng</rt><rb>地</rb><rt>di</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>子孙</rb><rt>ts-seng</rt><rb>照</rb><rt>tsiao</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>族份</rb><rt>dzoh-veng</rt></ruby>。

<sup>11</sup> <u><ruby><rb>便雅悯</rb><rt>Bin-yüô-ming</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>子孙个</rb><rt>ts-seng-go</rt><rb>支派</rb><rt>ts-pʽa</rt><rb>照</rb><rt>tsiao</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>房份</rb><rt>vông-veng</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>抽签个</rb><rt>tsʽiu-tsʽin-go</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>地界</rb><rt>di-ka</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>犹大</rb><rt>Yiu-da</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>子孙</rb><rt>ts-seng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>约瑟</rb><rt>Iah-seh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>子孙</rb><rt>ts-seng</rt><rb>当中</rb><rt>tông-cong</rt></ruby>。<sup>12</sup> <ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt><rb>北边</rb><rt>poh-pin</rt><rb>地界</rb><rt>di-ka</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>从</rb><rt>dzong</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>约但</rb><rt>Iah-dæn</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>河</rb><rt>ʽo</rt><rb>起</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>走上</rb><rt>tseo-zông</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶利哥</rb><rt>Yiæ-li-ko</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>北边</rb><rt>poh-pin</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>上</rb><rt>zông</rt><rb>山</rb><rt>sæn</rt><rb>向</rb><rt>hyiang</rt><rb>西</rb><rt>si</rt><rb>直到</rb><rt>dzih-tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>伯大文</rb><rt>Pah-da-veng</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>旷野</rb><rt>kwʽông-iæ</rt><rb>为</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>尽头</rb><rt>zing-deo</rt></ruby>。<sup>13</sup> <ruby><rb>其个</rb><rt>Gyi-go</rt><rb>地界</rb><rt>di-ka</rt><rb>从</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>箇头</rb><rt>keh-deo</rt><rb>过去</rb><rt>ko-kyʽi</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>路斯</rb><rt>Lu-s</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>路斯</u>个</rb><rt>Lu-s-go</rt><rb>南边</rb><rt>nen-pin</rt></ruby>；（<u><ruby><rb>路斯</rb><rt>Lu-s</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>伯特利</rb><rt>Pah-deh-li</rt></ruby></u>；）<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>地界</rb><rt>di-ka</rt><rb>落去</rb><rt>loh-kyʽi</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>亚大录亚达</rb><rt>Üô-da-loh-üô-deh</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>靠近</rb><rt>kʽao-gying</rt><rb>下</rb><rt>ʽô</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>伯和仑</rb><rt>Pah-ʽo-leng</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>南边个</rb><rt>nen-pin-go</rt><rb>山</rb><rt>sæn</rt></ruby>。<sup>14</sup> <ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>地界</rb><rt>di-ka</rt><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>弯转</rb><rt>w̆æn-cün</rt><rb>向</rb><rt>hyiang</rt><rb>西南边</rb><rt>si-nen-pin</rt><rb>从</rb><rt>dzong</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>伯和仑</rb><rt>Pah-ʽo-leng</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>南边</rb><rt>nen-pin</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>山</rb><rt>sæn</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>其个</rb><rt>gyi-go</rt><rb>尽头</rb><rt>zing-deo</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>基列巴力</rb><rt>Kyi-lih-pô-lih</rt></ruby></u>，（<u><ruby><rb>基列巴力</rb><rt>Kyi-lih-pô-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>基列耶琳</rb><rt>Kyi-lih-yiæ-ling</rt></ruby></u>）<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>属</rb><rt>joh</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>犹大</u>个</rb><rt>Yiu-da-go</rt><rb>城子</rb><rt>dzing-ts</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>西边</rb><rt>si-pin</rt><rb>地界</rb><rt>di-ka</rt></ruby>。<sup>15</sup> <ruby><rb>南边</rb><rt>Nen-pin</rt><rb>地界</rb><rt>di-ka</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>从</rb><rt>dzong</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>基列耶琳</u>个</rb><rt>Kyi-lih-yiæ-ling-go</rt><rb>尽头</rb><rt>zing-deo</rt><rb>向</rb><rt>hyiang</rt><rb>西</rb><rt>si</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>尼弗多亚</u>个</rb><rt>Nyi-feh-to-üô-go</rt><rb>水源</rb><rt>shü-nyün</rt></ruby>：<sup>16</sup> <ruby><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>落</rb><rt>loh</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>便欣嫩</rb><rt>Bin-hying-neng</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>岙</rb><rt>ao</rt><rb>相对个</rb><rt>siang-te-go</rt><rb>山脚</rb><rt>sæn-kyiah</rt><rb>尽头</rb><rt>zing-deo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>利乏音</rb><rt>Li-vah-ing</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>岙</rb><rt>ao</rt><rb>北边个</rb><rt>poh-pin-go</rt><rb>山</rb><rt>sæn</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>落</rb><rt>loh</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>便欣嫩</rb><rt>Bin-hying-neng</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>岙</rb><rt>ao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶布士</rb><rt>Yiæ-pu-z</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>城个</rb><rt>dzing-go</rt><rb>南边</rb><rt>nen-pin</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>宴罗结</rb><rt>In-lo-kyih</rt></ruby></u>；<sup>17</sup> <ruby><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>向</rb><rt>hyiang</rt><rb>北</rb><rt>poh</rt><rb>直出</rb><rt>dzih-cʽih</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>宴示麦</rb><rt>In-z-mah</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>直出</rb><rt>dzih-cʽih</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>上</rb><rt>zông</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>亚都冥</rb><rt>Üô-tu-ming</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>相对个</rb><rt>siang-te-go</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>基利绿</rb><rt>Kyi-li-loh</rt></ruby></u>；<ruby><rb>落去</rb><rt>loh-kyʽi</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>流便</rb><rt>Liu-bin</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>儿子</rb><rt>ng-ts</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>波罕</u>个</rb><rt>Po-hen-go</rt><rb>磐石</rb><rt>bun-zah</rt></ruby>；<sup>18</sup> <ruby><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>从</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>平垟</rb><rt>Bing-yiang</rt><rb>北边</rb><rt>poh-pin</rt><rb>过去</rb><rt>ko-kyʽi</rt><rb>落</rb><rt>loh</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>平垟</rb><rt>bing-yiang</rt></ruby>；<sup>19</sup> <ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>地界</rb><rt>di-ka</rt><rb>尽头</rb><rt>zing-deo</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>伯曷拉</rb><rt>Pah-ʽeh-lah</rt></ruby></u><sup>\[1\]</sup><ruby><rb>北边</rb><rt>poh-pin</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>直到</rb><rt>dzih-tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>盐</rb><rt>Yin</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>海个</rb><rt>hæ-go</rt><rb>北边</rb><rt>poh-pin</rt><rb>汊</rb><rt>tsʽô</rt></ruby>，<u><ruby><rb>约但</rb><rt>Iah-dæn</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>河</rb><rt>ʽo</rt><rb>极</rb><rt>gyih</rt><rb>南边</rb><rt>nen-pin</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>南边个</rb><rt>nen-pin-go</rt><rb>地界</rb><rt>di-ka</rt></ruby>。<sup>20</sup> <ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>东边</rb><rt>tong-pin</rt><rb>地界</rb><rt>di-ka</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>约但</rb><rt>Iah-dæn</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>河</rb><rt>ʽo</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>便雅悯</rb><rt>Bin-yüô-ming</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>子孙个</rb><rt>ts-seng-go</rt><rb>产业</rb><rt>tsʽæn-nyih</rt><rb>四围</rb><rt>s-we</rt><rb>地界</rb><rt>di-ka</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>照</rb><rt>tsiao</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>房份</rb><rt>vông-veng</rt></ruby>。<sup>21</sup> <u><ruby><rb>便雅悯</rb><rt>Bin-yüô-ming</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>子孙个</rb><rt>ts-seng-go</rt><rb>支派</rb><rt>ts-pʽa</rt><rb>照</rb><rt>tsiao</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>房份个</rb><rt>vông-veng-go</rt><rb>城子</rb><rt>dzing-ts</rt><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶利哥</rb><rt>Yiæ-li-ko</rt></ruby></u>、<u><ruby><rb>伯曷拉</rb><rt>Pah-ʽeh-lah</rt></ruby></u>、<u><ruby><rb>基悉</rb><rt>Kyi-sih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>山岙</rb><rt>sæn-ao</rt></ruby>；<sup>22</sup> <u><ruby><rb>伯亚拉巴</rb><rt>Pah-üô-lah-pô</rt></ruby></u>、<u><ruby><rb>洗玛脸</rb><rt>Si-mô-lin</rt></ruby></u>、<u><ruby><rb>伯特利</rb><rt>Pah-deh-li</rt></ruby></u>；<sup>23</sup> <u><ruby><rb>亚文</rb><rt>Üô-veng</rt></ruby></u>、<u><ruby><rb>巴拉</rb><rt>Pô-lah</rt></ruby></u>、<u><ruby><rb>阿弗拉</rb><rt>O-fah-lah</rt></ruby></u>；<sup>24</sup> <u><ruby><rb>基法哈摩耐</rb><rt>Kyi-fah-ha-mo-næ</rt></ruby></u>、<u><ruby><rb>阿弗尼</rb><rt>O-fah-nyi</rt></ruby></u>、<u><ruby><rb>迦巴</rb><rt>Kyüô-pô</rt></ruby></u>；<ruby><rb>共</rb><rt>gong</rt><rb>十二</rb><rt>jih-nyi</rt><rb>座</rb><rt>zo</rt><rb>城子</rb><rt>dzing-ts</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其个</rb><rt>gyi-go</rt><rb>乡村</rb><rt>hyiang-tsʽeng</rt></ruby>。<sup>25</sup> <ruby><rb>还有</rb><rt>Wa-yiu</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>基遍</rb><rt>Kyi-pin</rt></ruby></u>、<u><ruby><rb>拉玛</rb><rt>Lah-mô</rt></ruby></u>、<u><ruby><rb>比录</rb><rt>Pi-loh</rt></ruby></u>；<sup>26</sup> <u><ruby><rb>蜜是巴</rb><rt>Mih-z-pô</rt></ruby></u>、<u><ruby><rb>基非拉</rb><rt>Kyi-fi-lah</rt></ruby></u><sup>\[2\]</sup>、<u><ruby><rb>摩撒</rb><rt>Mo-sah</rt></ruby></u>；<sup>27</sup> <u><ruby><rb>利金</rb><rt>Li-kying</rt></ruby></u>、<u><ruby><rb>耳别</rb><rt>R-bih</rt></ruby></u>、<u><ruby><rb>大剌拉</rb><rt>Da-lah-lah</rt></ruby></u>；<sup>28</sup> <u><ruby><rb>洗拉</rb><rt>Si-lah</rt></ruby></u>、<u><ruby><rb>以法弗</rb><rt>Yi-fah-feh</rt></ruby></u>、<u><ruby><rb>耶布士</rb><rt>Yiæ-pu-z</rt></ruby></u>；（<u><ruby><rb>耶布士</rb><rt>Yiæ-pu-z</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶路撒冷</rb><rt>Yiæ-lu-sah-leng</rt></ruby></u>；）<u><ruby><rb>其比亚</rb><rt>Gyi-pi-üô</rt></ruby></u>、<u><ruby><rb>基列</rb><rt>Kyi-lih</rt></ruby></u>；<ruby><rb>共</rb><rt>gong</rt><rb>十<u>四</rb><rt>jih-s</rt><rb>座</rb><rt>zo</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>城子</rb><rt>dzing-ts</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其个</rb><rt>gyi-go</rt><rb>乡村</rb><rt>hyiang-tsʽeng</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>Keh</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>便雅悯</rb><rt>Bin-yüô-ming</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>子孙</rb><rt>ts-seng</rt><rb>照</rb><rt>tsiao</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>房份</rb><rt>vông-veng</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>得着个</rb><rt>teh-djoh-go</rt><rb>产业</rb><rt>tsʽæn-nyih</rt></ruby>。

### Is. XIX.

<ruby><rb>第二支</rb><rt>DI-NYI-TS</rt><rb>签</rb><rt>tsʽin</rt><rb>归</rb><rt>kwe</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>西缅</rb><rt>Si-min</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>西缅</rb><rt>Si-min</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>子孙个</rb><rt>ts-seng-go</rt><rb>支派</rb><rt>ts-pʽa</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>照</rb><rt>tsiao</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>房份</rb><rt>vông-veng</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>其拉个</rb><rt>gyi-lah-go</rt><rb>产业</rb><rt>tsʽæn-nyih</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>犹大</rb><rt>Yiu-da</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>子孙</rb><rt>ts-seng</rt><rb>产业个</rb><rt>tsʽæn-nyih-go</rt><rb>中央</rb><rt>cong-nyiang</rt></ruby>。<sup>2</sup> <ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>得着</rb><rt>teh-djoh</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>产业个</rb><rt>tsʽæn-nyih-go</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>别是巴</rb><rt>Bih-z-pô</rt></ruby></u>、<u><ruby><rb>示巴</rb><rt>Z-pô</rt></ruby></u>、<u><ruby><rb>摩拉大</rb><rt>Mo-lah-da</rt></ruby></u>；<sup>3</sup> <u><ruby><rb>哈萨书亚</rb><rt>Ha-sah-shü-üô</rt></ruby></u>、<u><ruby><rb>巴拉</rb><rt>Pô-lah</rt></ruby></u>、<u><ruby><rb>亚生</rb><rt>Üô-seng</rt></ruby></u>；<sup>4</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耳多腊</rb><rt>R-to-lah</rt></ruby></u>、<u><ruby><rb>伯突</rb><rt>Pah-deh</rt></ruby></u>、<u><ruby><rb>盍马</rb><rt>ʽEh-mô</rt></ruby></u>；<sup>5</sup> <u><ruby><rb>息腊</rb><rt>Sih-lah</rt></ruby></u>、<u><ruby><rb>伯玛嘉不</rb><rt>Pah-mô-kyüô-peh</rt></ruby></u>、<u><ruby><rb>哈萨苏撒</rb><rt>Ha-sah-su-sah</rt></ruby></u>；<sup>6</sup> <u><ruby><rb>伯利包得</rb><rt>Pah-li-pao-teh</rt></ruby></u>、<u><ruby><rb>沙鲁险</rb><rt>Sô-lu-hyin</rt></ruby></u>；<ruby><rb>共</rb><rt>gong</rt><rb>十三</rb><rt>jih-sæn</rt><rb>座</rb><rt>zo</rt><rb>城子</rb><rt>dzing-ts</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其个</rb><rt>gyi-go</rt><rb>乡村</rb><rt>hyiang-tsʽeng</rt></ruby>；<sup>7</sup> <ruby><rb>还有</rb><rt>wa-yiu</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>亚因</rb><rt>Üô-ing</rt></ruby></u>、<u><ruby><rb>哩门</rb><rt>Li-meng</rt></ruby></u>、<u><ruby><rb>以帖</rb><rt>Yi-tʽiah</rt></ruby></u>、<u><ruby><rb>亚散</rb><rt>Üô-sæn</rt></ruby></u>；<ruby><rb>共</rb><rt>gong</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>四座</rb><rt>s-zo</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>城子</rb><rt>dzing-ts</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其个</rb><rt>gyi-go</rt><rb>乡村</rb><rt>hyiang-tsʽeng</rt></ruby>；<sup>8</sup> <ruby><rb>还有</rb><rt>wa-yiu</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>城子</rb><rt>dzing-ts</rt><rb>团圈个</rb><rt>dön-kyʽün-go</rt><rb>乡村</rb><rt>hyiang-tsʽeng</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>直到</rb><rt>dzih-tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>巴拉比珥</rb><rt>Pô-lah-pi-r</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>南地</u>个</rb><rt>Nen-di-go</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>拉末</rb><rt>Lah-meh</rt></ruby></u>。<ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>Keh</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>西缅</rb><rt>Si-min</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>子孙个</rb><rt>ts-seng-go</rt><rb>支派个</rb><rt>ts-pʽa-go</rt><rb>产业</rb><rt>tsʽæn-nyih</rt><rb>照</rb><rt>tsiao</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>房份</rb><rt>vông-veng</rt></ruby>。<sup>9</sup> <u><ruby><rb>西缅</rb><rt>Si-min</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>子孙个</rb><rt>ts-seng-go</rt><rb>产业</rb><rt>tsʽæn-nyih</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>犹大</rb><rt>Yiu-da</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>子孙</rb><rt>ts-seng</rt><rb>产业</rb><rt>tsʽæn-nyih</rt><rb>中央</rb><rt>cong-nyiang</rt><rb>得着个</rb><rt>teh-djoh-go</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>因为</rb><rt>ing-we</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>犹大</rb><rt>Yiu-da</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>子孙</rb><rt>ts-seng</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>得着个</rb><rt>teh-djoh-go</rt><rb>过于</rb><rt>ko-ü</rt><rb>多</rb><rt>to</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>所以</rb><rt>sô-yi</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>西缅</rb><rt>Si-min</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>子孙</rb><rt>ts-seng</rt><rb>得着</rb><rt>teh-djoh</rt><rb>产业</rb><rt>tsʽæn-nyih</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>中央</rb><rt>cong-nyiang</rt></ruby>。

<sup>10</sup> <ruby><rb>第三支</rb><rt>Di-sæn-ts</rt><rb>签</rb><rt>tsʽin</rt><rb>归</rb><rt>kwe</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>西布伦</rb><rt>Si-pu-leng</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>子孙</rb><rt>ts-seng</rt><rb>照</rb><rt>tsiao</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>房份</rb><rt>vông-veng</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>其拉个</rb><rt>gyi-lah-go</rt><rb>地界</rb><rt>di-ka</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>撒立</rb><rt>Sah-lih</rt></ruby></u>；<sup>11</sup> <ruby><rb>其拉个</rb><rt>gyi-lah-go</rt><rb>地界</rb><rt>di-ka</rt><rb>向</rb><rt>hyiang</rt><rb>西</rb><rt>si</rt><rb>上去</rb><rt>zông-kyʽi</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>玛拉喇</rb><rt>Mô-lah-lah</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>接着</rb><rt>tsih-djoh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>大巴设</rb><rt>Da-pô-shih</rt></ruby></u>；<ruby><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>接着</rb><rt>tsih-djoh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>约念</rb><rt>Iah-nyiæn</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>面前个</rb><rt>min-zin-go</rt><rb>河</rb><rt>ʽo</rt></ruby>，<sup>12</sup> <ruby><rb>从</rb><rt>Dzong</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>撒立</rb><rt>Sah-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>转去</rb><rt>cün-kyʽi</rt><rb>向</rb><rt>hyiang</rt><rb>东</rb><rt>tong</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>日头</rb><rt>nyih-deo</rt><rb>出去</rb><rt>cʽih-kyʽi</rt><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>吉绿大泊</u>个</rb><rt>Kyih-loh-da-boh-go</rt><rb>地界</rb><rt>di-ka</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>直出</rb><rt>dzih-cʽih</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>大比拉</rb><rt>Da-pi-lah</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>上去</rb><rt>zông-kyʽi</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶非亚</rb><rt>Yiæ-fi-üô</rt></ruby></u>；<sup>13</sup> <ruby><rb>从</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>箇头</rb><rt>keh-deo</rt><rb>向</rb><rt>hyiang</rt><rb>东</rb><rt>tong</rt><rb>过去</rb><rt>ko-kyʽi</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>基大希伯</rb><rt>Kyi-da-hyi-pah</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以大加汛</rb><rt>Yi-da-kyüô-sing</rt></ruby></u>；<ruby><rb>直出</rb><rt>dzih-cʽih</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>接着</rb><rt>tsih-djoh</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>尼亚</u>个</rb><rt>Nyi-üô-go</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>哩门</rb><rt>Li-meng</rt></ruby></u>；<sup>14</sup> <ruby><rb>还有</rb><rt>wa-yiu</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>迦大</rb><rt>Kyüô-da</rt></ruby></u>、<u><ruby><rb>拿哈拉</rb><rt>Nô-ha-lah</rt></ruby></u>、<u><ruby><rb>伸仑</rb><rt>Sing-leng</rt></ruby></u>、<u><ruby><rb>以大拉</rb><rt>Yi-da-lah</rt></ruby></u>、<u><ruby><rb>伯利恒</rb><rt>Pah-li-ʽeng</rt></ruby></u>；<ruby><rb>共</rb><rt>gong</rt><rb>十二</rb><rt>jih-nyi</rt><rb>座</rb><rt>zo</rt><rb>城子</rb><rt>dzing-ts</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其个</rb><rt>gyi-go</rt><rb>乡村</rb><rt>hyiang-tsʽeng</rt></ruby>。<sup>16</sup> <sup>17</sup> <ruby><rb>箇星</rb><rt>Keh-sing</rt><rb>城子</rb><rt>dzing-ts</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其个</rb><rt>gyi-go</rt><rb>乡村</rb><rt>hyiang-tsʽeng</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>亚设</rb><rt>Üô-shih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>子孙</rb><rt>ts-seng</rt><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>支派</rb><rt>ts-pʽa</rt><rb>照</rb><rt>tsiao</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>房份</rb><rt>vông-veng</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>得着个</rb><rt>teh-djoh-go</rt><rb>产业</rb><rt>tsʽæn-nyih</rt></ruby>。

<sup>32</sup> <ruby><rb>第六支</rb><rt>Di-loh-ts</rt><rb>签</rb><rt>tsʽin</rt><rb>归拨</rb><rt>kwe-peh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>纳大利</rb><rt>Neh-da-li</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>纳大利</rb><rt>Neh-da-li</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>子孙个</rb><rt>ts-seng-go</rt><rb>支派</rb><rt>ts-pʽa</rt><rb>照</rb><rt>tsiao</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>房份</rb><rt>vông-veng</rt></ruby>。<sup>33</sup> <ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>地界</rb><rt>di-ka</rt><rb>转弯</rb><rt>cün-w̆æn</rt><rb>向</rb><rt>hyiang</rt><rb>西</rb><rt>si</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>押纳大泊</rb><rt>Iah-neh-da-boh</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>从</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>箇头</rb><rt>keh-deo</rt><rb>过去</rb><rt>ko-kyʽi</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>户割</rb><rt>Wu-keh</rt></ruby></u>；<ruby><rb>南边</rb><rt>nen-pin</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>接着</rb><rt>tsih-djoh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>西布伦</rb><rt>Si-pu-leng</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>西边</rb><rt>si-pin</rt><rb>接着</rb><rt>tsih-djoh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>亚设</rb><rt>Üô-shih</rt></ruby></u>；<ruby><rb>向着</rb><rt>hyiang-djoh</rt><rb>日头</rb><rt>nyih-deo</rt><rb>出去</rb><rt>cʽih-kyʽi</rt><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt><rb>接着</rb><rt>tsih-djoh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>约但</rb><rt>Iah-dæn</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>河边个</rb><rt>ʽo-pin-go</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>犹大</rb><rt>Yiu-da</rt></ruby></u>。<sup>35</sup> <ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>坚固</rb><rt>kyin-kwu</rt><rb>城子</rb><rt>dzing-ts</rt><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>西丁</rb><rt>Si-ting</rt></ruby></u>、<u><ruby><rb>西珥</rb><rt>Si-r</rt></ruby></u>、<u><ruby><rb>哈抹</rb><rt>Ha-mah</rt></ruby></u>、<u><ruby><rb>拉甲</rb><rt>Lah-kyiah</rt></ruby></u>、<u><ruby><rb>基尼烈</rb><rt>Kyi-nyi-lih</rt></ruby></u>；<sup>36</sup> <ruby><rb>箇星</rb><rt>Keh-sing</rt><rb>城子</rb><rt>dzing-ts</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其个</rb><rt>gyi-go</rt><rb>乡村</rb><rt>hyiang-tsʽeng</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>纳大利</rb><rt>Neh-da-li</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>子孙</rb><rt>ts-seng</rt><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>支派</rb><rt>ts-pʽa</rt><rb>照</rb><rt>tsiao</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>房份</rb><rt>vông-veng</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>得着个</rb><rt>teh-djoh-go</rt><rb>产业</rb><rt>tsʽæn-nyih</rt></ruby>。

<sup>40</sup> <ruby><rb>后头</rb><rt>ʽEo-deo</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>但</rb><rt>Dæn</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>子孙个</rb><rt>ts-seng-go</rt><rb>地界</rb><rt>di-ka</rt><rb>开辟</rb><rt>kʽæ-bih</rt><rb>起来</rb><rt>kyʽi-læ</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>因为</rb><rt>ing-we</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>但</u>个</rb><rt>Dæn-go</rt><rb>子孙</rb><rt>ts-seng</rt><rb>走上</rb><rt>tseo-zông</rt><rb>攻打</rb><rt>kong-tang</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>利善</rb><rt>Li-jün</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>夺着</rb><rt>deh-djoh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>刀</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>攻打</rb><rt>kong-tang</rt><rb>得着</rb><rt>teh-djoh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>庉</rb><rt>deng</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>箇头</rb><rt>keh-deo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>取</rb><rt>cʽü</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>利善</rb><rt>Li-jün</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>地名</rb><rt>di-ming</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>但</rb><rt>Dæn</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>照</rb><rt>tsiao</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>祖宗</rb><rt>tsu-tsong</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>但</u>个</rb><rt>Dæn-go</rt><rb>名字</rb><rt>ming-z</rt></ruby>。<sup>48</sup> <ruby><rb>箇星</rb><rt>Keh-sing</rt><rb>城子</rb><rt>dzing-ts</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其个</rb><rt>gyi-go</rt><rb>乡村</rb><rt>hyiang-tsʽeng</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>但</rb><rt>Dæn</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>子孙</rb><rt>ts-seng</rt><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>支派</rb><rt>ts-pʽa</rt><rb>照</rb><rt>tsiao</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>房份</rb><rt>vông-veng</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>得着个</rb><rt>teh-djoh-go</rt><rb>产业</rb><rt>tsʽæn-nyih</rt></ruby>。

<sup>49</sup> <ruby><rb>是介</rb><rt>Z-ka</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>照</rb><rt>tsiao</rt><rb>地界</rb><rt>di-ka</rt><rb>分</rb><rt>feng</rt><rb>地</rb><rt>di</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>产业</rb><rt>tsʽæn-nyih</rt><rb>已经</rb><rt>yi-kying</rt><rb>完毕</rb><rt>wun-pih</rt></ruby>；<u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>赐</rb><rt>s</rt><rb>产业</rb><rt>tsʽæn-nyih</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>嫩</u>个</rb><rt>Neng-go</rt><rb>儿子</rb><rt>ng-ts</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>约书亚</rb><rt>Iah-shü-üô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>中央</rb><rt>cong-nyiang</rt></ruby>，<sup>50</sup> <ruby><rb>照</rb><rt>tsiao</rt><rb>耶和华个</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô-go</rt><rb>命令</rb><rt>ming-ling</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>赐拨</rb><rt>s-peh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>其自</rb><rt>gyi-zi</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>讨个</rb><rt>tʽao-go</rt><rb>城子</rb><rt>dzing-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以法莲</rb><rt>Yi-fah-lin</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>山地个</rb><rt>sæn-di-go</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>亭讷</rb><rt>Ding-neh</rt></ruby></u> <u><ruby><rb>西拉</rb><rt>si-lah</rt></ruby></u>：<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>建造</rb><rt>kyin-zao</rt><rb>城子</rb><rt>dzing-ts</rt><rb>居住</rb><rt>kyü-djü</rt></ruby>。

<sup>51</sup> <ruby><rb>箇星</rb><rt>Keh-sing</rt><rb>产业</rb><rt>tsʽæn-nyih</rt><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>祭司</rb><rt>tsi-s</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以利亚撒</rb><rt>Yi-li-üô-sah</rt></ruby></u>、<ruby><rb><u>嫩</u>个</rb><rt>Neng-go</rt><rb>儿子</rb><rt>ng-ts</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>约书亚</rb><rt>Iah-shü-üô</rt></ruby></u>、<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>各</rb><rt>koh</rt><rb>支派个</rb><rt>ts-pʽa-go</rt><rb>族长</rb><rt>dzoh-tsiang</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>示罗</rb><rt>Z-lo</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>公会</rb><rt>kong-we</rt><rb>帐篷</rb><rt>tsiang-bong</rt><rb>门口</rb><rt>meng-kʽeo</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>耶和华</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>面前</rb><rt>min-zin</rt><rb>抽签</rb><rt>tsʽiu-tsʽin</rt><rb>分</rb><rt>feng</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>产业个</rb><rt>tsʽæn-nyih-go</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>是介</rb><rt>Z-ka</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>分</rb><rt>feng</rt><rb>地个</rb><rt>di-go</rt><rb>事干</rb><rt>z-ken</rt><rb>完毕了</rb><rt>wun-pih-de</rt></ruby>。

### Is. XX.

<ruby><rb>耶和华</rb><rt>YIÆ-ʽO-WÔ</rt><rb>吩咐</rb><rt>feng-fu</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>约书亚</rb><rt>Iah-shü-üô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：<sup>2</sup> 「<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>子孙</rb><rt>ts-seng</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：『<ruby><rb>好</rb><rt>Hao</rt><rb>为了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt><rb>自</rb><rt>zi</rt><rb>设立</rb><rt>shih-lih</rt><rb>逃命个</rb><rt>dao-ming-go</rt><rb>城子</rb><rt>dzing-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>托</rb><rt>tʽoh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>讲个</rb><rt>kông-go</rt></ruby>；<sup>3</sup> <ruby><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>偶然</rb><rt>ngeo-jün</rt><rb>无意中</rb><rt>vu-i-cong</rt><rb>杀人个</rb><rt>sah-nying-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>逃到</rb><rt>dao-tao</rt><rb>箇头</rb><rt>keh-deo</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>城子</rb><rt>dzing-ts</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>逃避</rb><rt>dao-bi</rt><rb>报仇个</rb><rt>pao-dziu-go</rt><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt></ruby>。<sup>4</sup> <ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>必要</rb><rt>pih-iao</rt><rb>逃到</rb><rt>dao-tao</rt><rb>一座</rb><rt>ih-zo</rt><rb>逃命</rb><rt>dao-ming</rt><rb>城</rb><rt>dzing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>立</rb><rt>lih</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>城门口</rb><rt>dzing-meng-kʽeo</rt><rb>把</rb><rt>pô</rt><rb>其个</rb><rt>gyi-go</rt><rb>案件</rb><rt>en-gyin</rt><rb>告诉</rb><rt>kao-su</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>城里个</rb><rt>dzing-li-go</rt><rb>长老</rb><rt>tsiang-lao</rt><rb>听</rb><rt>tʽing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>收</rb><rt>siu</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>进</rb><rt>tsing</rt><rb>城</rb><rt>dzing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>归</rb><rt>kwe</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>一𡍲</rb><rt>ih-tʽah</rt><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>庉</rb><rt>deng</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>中央</rb><rt>cong-nyiang</rt></ruby>。<sup>5</sup> <ruby><rb>若是</rb><rt>Ziah-z</rt><rb>报仇个</rb><rt>pao-dziu-go</rt><rb>追赶</rb><rt>tse-ken</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>弗可</rb><rt>feh-kʽo</rt><rb>把</rb><rt>pô</rt><rb>杀人个</rb><rt>sah-nying-go</rt><rb>交代</rb><rt>kao-dæ</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>手里</rb><rt>siu-li</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>偶然</rb><rt>ngeo-jün</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>打</rb><rt>tang</rt><rb>邻舍家</rb><rt>ling-sô-kô</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>因为</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>从前</rb><rt>dzong-zin</rt><rb>呒得</rb><rt>m-neh</rt><rb>恶愫</rb><rt>u-su</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>。<sup>6</sup> <ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>庉</rb><rt>deng</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>箇座</rb><rt>keh-zo</rt><rb>城子</rb><rt>dzing-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等到</rb><rt>teng-tao</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>公会</rb><rt>kong-we</rt><rb>面前</rb><rt>min-zin</rt><rb>受</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>判断</rb><rt>pʽun-tön</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等到</rb><rt>teng-tao</rt><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>时候个</rb><rt>z-ʽeo-go</rt><rb>祭司长</rb><rt>tsi-s-tsiang</rt><rb>死</rb><rt>si</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>箇遭</rb><rt>keh-tsao</rt><rb>杀人个</rb><rt>sah-nying-go</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>归</rb><rt>kyü</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>本城</rb><rt>peng-dzing</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>自</rb><rt>zi</rt><rb>屋里</rb><rt>oh-li</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>逃出个</rb><rt>dao-cʽih-go</rt><rb>城子</rb><rt>dzing-ts</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>。』」<sup>7</sup> <ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>设立</rb><rt>shih-lih</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>纳大利</rb><rt>Neh-da-li</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>山地</rb><rt>sæn-di</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>加利利</u>个</rb><rt>Kyüô-li-li-go</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>基特</rb><rt>Kyi-deh</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以法莲</rb><rt>Yi-fah-lin</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>山地个</rb><rt>sæn-di-go</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>示剑</rb><rt>Z-kyin</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>犹大</rb><rt>Yiu-da</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>山地个</rb><rt>sæn-di-go</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>基列亚巴</rb><rt>Kyi-lih-üô-pô</rt></ruby></u>。（<u><ruby><rb>基列亚巴</rb><rt>Kyi-lih-üô-pô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>希伯仑</rb><rt>Hyi-pah-leng</rt></ruby></u>。）<sup>8</sup> <ruby><rb>还有</rb><rt>Wa-yiu</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>约但</rb><rt>Iah-dæn</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>河外</rb><rt>ʽo-nga</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶利哥</rb><rt>Yiæ-li-ko</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>东边</rb><rt>tong-pin</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>平垟</rb><rt>bing-yiang</rt><rb>里</rb><rt>li</rt><rb>设立</rb><rt>shih-lih</rt><rb>旷野</rb><rt>kwʽông-iæ</rt><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>比悉</rb><rt>Pi-sih</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>属于</rb><rt>joh-ü</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>流便</rb><rt>Liu-bin</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>支派</rb><rt>ts-pʽa</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>基列</u>个</rb><rt>Kyi-lih-go</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>拉末</rb><rt>Lah-mah</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>属于</rb><rt>joh-ü</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>迦得</rb><rt>Kyüô-teh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>支派</rb><rt>ts-pʽa</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>连</rb><rt>lin</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>巴珊</rb><rt>Pô-sæn</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>地方个</rb><rt>di-fông-go</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>坷兰</rb><rt>Kʽo-læn</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>属于</rb><rt>joh-ü</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>玛拿西</rb><rt>Mô-nô-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>支派</rb><rt>ts-pʽa</rt></ruby>。<sup>9</sup> <ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>Keh</rt><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>为了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt><rb>众</rb><rt>cong</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>子孙</rb><rt>ts-seng</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>庉</rb><rt>deng</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>中央</rb><rt>cong-nyiang</rt><rb>外来</rb><rt>nga-læ</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>立个</rb><rt>lih-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>偶然</rb><rt>ngeo-jün</rt><rb>杀人个</rb><rt>sah-nying-go</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>逃到</rb><rt>dao-tao</rt><rb>箇头</rb><rt>keh-deo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>报仇个</rb><rt>pao-dziu-go</rt><rb>打煞</rb><rt>tang-sah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等到</rb><rt>teng-tao</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>立</rb><rt>lih</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>公会</rb><rt>kong-we</rt><rb>面前</rb><rt>min-zin</rt></ruby>。

### Is. XXI.

<ruby><rb>介</rb><rt>KA</rt><rb>时候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>利未</u>个</rb><rt>Li-vi-go</rt><rb>族长</rb><rt>dzoh-tsiang</rt><rb>走拢</rb><rt>tseo-long</rt><rb>祭司</rb><rt>tsi-s</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以利亚撒</rb><rt>Yi-li-üô-sah</rt></ruby></u>、<ruby><rb><u>嫩</u>个</rb><rt>Neng-go</rt><rb>儿子</rb><rt>ng-ts</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>约书亚</rb><rt>Iah-shü-üô</rt></ruby></u>、<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>子孙</rb><rt>ts-seng</rt><rb>支派</rb><rt>ts-pʽa</rt><rb>族长个</rb><rt>dzoh-tsiang-go</rt><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt></ruby>；<sup>2</sup> <ruby><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>迦南</u>个</rb><rt>Kyüô-nen-go</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>示罗</rb><rt>Z-lo</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>讲</rb><rt>kông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>耶和华</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>托</rb><rt>tʽoh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>吩咐</rb><rt>feng-fu</rt><rb>赐拨</rb><rt>s-peh</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>几个</rb><rt>kyi-go</rt><rb>城子</rb><rt>dzing-ts</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>庉</rb><rt>deng</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>城外个</rb><rt>dzing-nga-go</rt><rb>地</rb><rt>di</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>养</rb><rt>yiang</rt><rb>牲口</rb><rt>sang-kʽeo</rt></ruby>。」<sup>3</sup> <u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>子孙</rb><rt>ts-seng</rt><rb>把</rb><rt>pô</rt><rb>以下</rb><rt>yi-ʽô</rt><rb>写的个</rb><rt>sia-tih-go</rt><rb>城子</rb><rt>dzing-ts</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其拉个</rb><rt>gyi-lah-go</rt><rb>地</rb><rt>di</rt><rb>从</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>自个</rb><rt>zi-go</rt><rb>产业</rb><rt>tsʽæn-nyih</rt><rb>中央</rb><rt>cong-nyiang</rt><rb>赐拨</rb><rt>s-peh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>利未</rb><rt>Li-vi</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>照</rb><rt>tsiao</rt><rb>耶和华</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>吩咐个</rb><rt>feng-fu-go</rt></ruby>。

<sup>4</sup> <ruby><rb>为了</rb><rt>We-leh</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>哥辖</u>个</rb><rt>Ko-ʽeh-go</rt><rb>房份</rb><rt>vông-veng</rt><rb>抽签</rb><rt>tsʽiu-tsʽin</rt></ruby>；<u><ruby><rb>利未</rb><rt>Li-vi</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>祭司</rb><rt>tsi-s</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>亚伦</u>个</rb><rt>Üô-leng-go</rt><rb>子孙</rb><rt>ts-seng</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>从</rb><rt>dzong</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>犹大</rb><rt>Yiu-da</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>支派</rb><rt>ts-pʽa</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>从</rb><rt>dzong</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>西缅</rb><rt>Si-min</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>支派</rb><rt>ts-pʽa</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>还有</rb><rt>wa-yiu</rt><rb>从</rb><rt>dzong</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>便雅悯</rb><rt>Bin-yüô-ming</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>支派</rb><rt>ts-pʽa</rt><rb>抽签</rb><rt>tsʽiu-tsʽin</rt><rb>得着</rb><rt>teh-djoh</rt><rb>十三</rb><rt>jih-sæn</rt><rb>座</rb><rt>zo</rt><rb>城子</rb><rt>dzing-ts</rt></ruby>。

<sup>5</sup> <u><ruby><rb>哥辖</rb><rt>Ko-ʽeh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>余多</rb><rt>yü-to</rt><rb>子孙</rb><rt>ts-seng</rt><rb>抽签</rb><rt>tsʽiu-tsʽin</rt><rb>从</rb><rt>dzong</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以法莲</rb><rt>Yi-fah-lin</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>支派个</rb><rt>ts-pʽa-go</rt><rb>房份</rb><rt>vông-veng</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>从</rb><rt>dzong</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>但</rb><rt>Dæn</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>支派</rb><rt>ts-pʽa</rt><rb>还有</rb><rt>wa-yiu</rt><rb>从</rb><rt>dzong</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>玛拿西</rb><rt>Mô-nô-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>半</rb><rt>pun</rt><rb>支派</rb><rt>ts-pʽa</rt><rb>得着</rb><rt>teh-djoh</rt><rb>十座</rb><rt>jih-zo</rt><rb>城子</rb><rt>dzing-ts</rt></ruby>。

<sup>6</sup> <u><ruby><rb>革顺</rb><rt>Kah-jing</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>子孙</rb><rt>ts-seng</rt><rb>抽签</rb><rt>tsʽiu-tsʽin</rt><rb>从</rb><rt>dzong</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以萨迦</rb><rt>Yi-sah-kyüô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>支派个</rb><rt>ts-pʽa-go</rt><rb>房份</rb><rt>vông-veng</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>从</rb><rt>dzong</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>亚设</rb><rt>Üô-shih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>支派</rb><rt>ts-pʽa</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>纳大利</rb><rt>Neh-da-li</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>支派</rb><rt>ts-pʽa</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>还有</rb><rt>wa-yiu</rt><rb>从</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>住</rb><rt>djü</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>巴珊</rb><rt>Pô-sæn</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>地</rb><rt>di</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>玛拿西</rb><rt>Mô-nô-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>半</rb><rt>pun</rt><rb>支派</rb><rt>ts-pʽa</rt><rb>得着</rb><rt>teh-djoh</rt><rb>十三</rb><rt>jih-sæn</rt><rb>座</rb><rt>zo</rt><rb>城子</rb><rt>dzing-ts</rt></ruby>。

<sup>7</sup> <u><ruby><rb>米拉利</rb><rt>Mi-lah-li</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>子孙</rb><rt>ts-seng</rt><rb>照</rb><rt>tsiao</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>房份</rb><rt>vông-veng</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>从</rb><rt>dzong</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>流便</rb><rt>Liu-bin</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>支派</rb><rt>ts-pʽa</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>从</rb><rt>dzong</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>迦得</rb><rt>Kyüô-teh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>支派</rb><rt>ts-pʽa</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>从</rb><rt>dzong</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>西布伦</rb><rt>Si-pu-leng</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>支派</rb><rt>ts-pʽa</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>得着</rb><rt>teh-djoh</rt><rb>十二</rb><rt>jih-nyi</rt><rb>座</rb><rt>zo</rt><rb>城子</rb><rt>dzing-ts</rt></ruby>。

<sup>8</sup> <u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>抽签</rb><rt>tsʽiu-tsʽin</rt><rb>分</rb><rt>feng</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>城子</rb><rt>dzing-ts</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其个</rb><rt>gyi-go</rt><rb>地</rb><rt>di</rt><rb>赐拨</rb><rt>s-peh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>利未</rb><rt>Li-vi</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>照</rb><rt>tsiao</rt><rb>耶和华</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>托</rb><rt>tʽoh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>吩咐个</rb><rt>feng-fu-go</rt></ruby>。<sup>9</sup> <ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt><rb>从</rb><rt>dzong</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>犹大</rb><rt>Yiu-da</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>西缅</u>个</rb><rt>Si-min-go</rt><rb>子孙</rb><rt>ts-seng</rt><rb>支派</rb><rt>ts-pʽa</rt><rb>把</rb><rt>pô</rt><rb>名字</rb><rt>ming-z</rt><rb>写</rb><rt>sia</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>下面个</rb><rt>ʽô-min-go</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>城子</rb><rt>dzing-ts</rt><rb>赐拨</rb><rt>s-peh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>利未</rb><rt>Li-vi</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>：<sup>10</sup> <ruby><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>城子</rb><rt>dzing-ts</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>赐拨</rb><rt>s-peh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>亚伦</rb><rt>Üô-leng</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>子孙</rb><rt>ts-seng</rt></ruby>，<u><ruby><rb>哥辖</rb><rt>Ko-ʽeh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>房份</rb><rt>vông-veng</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>利未</rb><rt>Li-vi</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>子孙</rb><rt>ts-seng</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>因为</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>第一支</rb><rt>di-ih-ts</rt><rb>签</rb><rt>tsʽin</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>其拉个</rb><rt>gyi-lah-go</rt></ruby>。<sup>11</sup> <ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>Ziu</rt><rb>把</rb><rt>pô</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>犹大</rb><rt>Yiu-da</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>山地个</rb><rt>sæn-di-go</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>基列亚巴</rb><rt>Kyi-lih-üô-pô</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>城外个</rb><rt>dzing-nga-go</rt><rb>地</rb><rt>di</rt><rb>赐拨</rb><rt>s-peh</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>亚巴</rb><rt>Üô-pô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>亚衲</u>个</rb><rt>Üô-neh-go</rt><rb>阿爹</rb><rt>ah-tia</rt></ruby>。（<u><ruby><rb>基列亚巴</rb><rt>Kyi-lih-üô-pô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>希伯仑</rb><rt>Hyi-pah-leng</rt></ruby></u>。）<sup>12</sup> <ruby><rb>但是</rb><rt>Dæn-z</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>城子个</rb><rt>dzing-ts-go</rt><rb>田地</rb><rt>din-di</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>乡村</rb><rt>hyiang-tsʽeng</rt><rb>赐拨</rb><rt>s-peh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶孚尼</rb><rt>Yiæ-fu-nyi</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>儿子</rb><rt>ng-ts</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>迦勒</rb><rt>Kyüô-lah</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>产业</rb><rt>tsʽæn-nyih</rt></ruby>。

<sup>13</sup>
