<ruby><rb>从</rb><rt>Dzong</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>迦得</rb><rt>Kyüô-teh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>支派</rb><rt>ts-pʽa</rt><rb>得着</rb><rt>teh-djoh</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>基列</u>个</rb><rt>Kyi-lih-go</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>拉末</rb><rt>Lah-mah</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>杀人个</rb><rt>sah-nying-go</rt><rb>逃命</rb><rt>dao-ming</rt><rb>城</rb><rt>dzing</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>城外个</rb><rt>dzing-nga-go</rt><rb>地</rb><rt>di</rt></ruby>；<u><ruby><rb>玛哈念</rb><rt>Mô-ha-nyiæn</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>城外个</rb><rt>dzing-nga-go</rt><rb>地</rb><rt>di</rt></ruby>；<sup>39</sup> <u><ruby><rb>希实本</rb><rt>Hyi-jih-peng</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>城外个</rb><rt>dzing-nga-go</rt><rb>地</rb><rt>di</rt></ruby>；<u><ruby><rb>雅设</rb><rt>Yüô-shih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>城外个</rb><rt>dzing-nga-go</rt><rb>地</rb><rt>di</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>共</rb><rt>gong</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>四座</rb><rt>s-zo</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>城子</rb><rt>dzing-ts</rt></ruby>。<sup>40</sup> <ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>Keh</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>利未</rb><rt>Li-vi</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>余多</rb><rt>yü-to</rt><rb>房份</rb><rt>vông-veng</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>米拉利</rb><rt>Mi-lah-li</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>子孙</rb><rt>ts-seng</rt><rb>照</rb><rt>tsiao</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>房份</rb><rt>vông-veng</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>得着个</rb><rt>teh-djoh-go</rt><rb>拢总</rb><rt>long-tsong</rt><rb>城子</rb><rt>dzing-ts</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>共</rb><rt>gong</rt><rb>抽签</rb><rt>tsʽiu-tsʽin</rt><rb>得着</rb><rt>teh-djoh</rt><rb>十二</rb><rt>jih-nyi</rt><rb>座</rb><rt>zo</rt><rb>城子</rb><rt>dzing-ts</rt></ruby>。

<sup>41</sup> <u><ruby><rb>利未</rb><rt>Li-vi</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>子孙个</rb><rt>ts-seng-go</rt><rb>产业</rb><rt>tsʽæn-nyih</rt><rb>中央</rb><rt>cong-nyiang</rt><rb>拢总</rb><rt>long-tsong</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>得着个</rb><rt>teh-djoh-go</rt><rb>城子</rb><rt>dzing-ts</rt><rb>共</rb><rt>gong</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>四十八</rb><rt>s-jih-pah</rt><rb>座</rb><rt>zo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>城外个</rb><rt>dzing-nga-go</rt><rb>地</rb><rt>di</rt></ruby>。<sup>42</sup> <ruby><rb>箇星</rb><rt>Keh-sing</rt><rb>城子</rb><rt>dzing-ts</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>城外个</rb><rt>dzing-nga-go</rt><rb>地</rb><rt>di</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>各</rb><rt>koh</rt><rb>座</rb><rt>zo</rt><rb>城</rb><rt>dzing</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt></ruby>。

<sup>43</sup> <ruby><rb>是介</rb><rt>Z-ka</rt><rb>耶和华</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>把</rb><rt>pô</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>一切</rb><rt>ih-tsʽih</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>罚咒</rb><rt>vah-tsiu</rt><rb>应许</rb><rt>ing-hyü</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>祖宗个</rb><rt>tsu-tsong-go</rt><rb>地</rb><rt>di</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>赐拨</rb><rt>s-peh</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>得着</rb><rt>teh-djoh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>产业</rb><rt>tsʽæn-nyih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>庉</rb><rt>deng</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>箇头</rb><rt>keh-deo</rt></ruby>。<sup>44</sup> <ruby><rb>耶和华</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>赐拨</rb><rt>s-peh</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>四面</rb><rt>s-min</rt><rb>安耽</rb><rt>en-tæn</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>照</rb><rt>tsiao</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>凡百</rb><rt>væn-pah</rt><rb>向</rb><rt>hyiang</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>列祖</rb><rt>lih-tsu</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>罚咒个</rb><rt>vah-tsiu-go</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>拢总</rb><rt>long-tsong</rt><rb>仇敌</rb><rt>dziu-dih</rt><rb>呒得</rb><rt>m-neh</rt><rb>一个</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>面前</rb><rt>min-zin</rt><rb>立勒住</rb><rt>lih-leh-djü</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>因为</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>耶和华</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>把</rb><rt>pô</rt><rb>拢总</rb><rt>long-tsong</rt><rb>仇敌</rb><rt>dziu-dih</rt><rb>交代</rb><rt>kao-dæ</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>手里</rb><rt>siu-li</rt></ruby>。<sup>45</sup> <ruby><rb>耶和华</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>向</rb><rt>hyiang</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>凡百</rb><rt>væn-pah</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>应许个</rb><rt>ing-hyü-go</rt><rb>好处</rb><rt>hao-cʽü</rt><rb>呒得</rb><rt>m-neh</rt><rb>缺</rb><rt>kyʽüih</rt><rb>一样</rb><rt>ih-yiang</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>拢总</rb><rt>long-tsong</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>应验</rb><rt>ing-nyiæn</rt></ruby>。

### Is. XXII.

<ruby><rb>箇遭</rb><rt>KEH-TSAO</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>约书亚</rb><rt>Iah-shü-üô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>召</rb><rt>dziao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>流便</rb><rt>Liu-bin</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>、<u><ruby><rb>迦得</rb><rt>Kyüô-teh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>、<u><ruby><rb>玛拿西</rb><rt>Mô-nô-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>半</rb><rt>pun</rt><rb>支派个</rb><rt>ts-pʽa-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：<sup>2</sup> 「<ruby><rb>耶和华个</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô-go</rt><rb>奴仆</rb><rt>nu-boh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>凡百</rb><rt>væn-pah</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>吩咐</rb><rt>feng-fu</rt><rb>尔拉个</rb><rt>ng-lah-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>已经</rb><rt>yi-kying</rt><rb>保守</rb><rt>pao-siu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>听从</rb><rt>tʽing-dzong</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>凡百</rb><rt>væn-pah</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>吩咐</rb><rt>feng-fu</rt><rb>尔拉个</rb><rt>ng-lah-go</rt><rb>说话</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt></ruby>；<sup>3</sup> <ruby><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>许多</rb><rt>hyü-to</rt><rb>日子</rb><rt>nyih-ts</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>呒得</rb><rt>m-neh</rt><rb>离开</rb><rt>li-kʽæ</rt><rb>兄弟</rb><rt>hyüong-di</rt><rb>直到</rb><rt>dzih-tao</rt><rb>如今</rb><rt>jü-kying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>保守</rb><rt>pao-siu</rt><rb>耶和华</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>神明个</rb><rt>Jing-ming-go</rt><rb>命令</rb><rt>ming-ling</rt></ruby>。<sup>4</sup> <ruby><rb>现在</rb><rt>Yin-dzæ</rt><rb>耶和华</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>尔个</rb><rt>ng-go</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>已经</rb><rt>yi-kying</rt><rb>赐</rb><rt>s</rt><rb>平安</rb><rt>bing-en</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>尔个</rb><rt>ng-go</rt><rb>兄弟</rb><rt>hyüong-di</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>像</rb><rt>ziang</rt><rb>耶和华</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>话过</rb><rt>wô-ko</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>所以</rb><rt>sô-yi</rt><rb>现在</rb><rt>yin-dzæ</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>归到</rb><rt>kyü-tao</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>帐篷</rb><rt>tsiang-bong</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>得着</rb><rt>teh-djoh</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>产业个</rb><rt>tsʽæn-nyih-go</rt><rb>地</rb><rt>di</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>耶和华个</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô-go</rt><rb>奴仆</rb><rt>nu-boh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>赐拨</rb><rt>s-peh</rt><rb>尔拉个</rb><rt>ng-lah-go</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>约但</rb><rt>Iah-dæn</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>河外</rb><rt>ʽo-nga</rt></ruby>。<sup>5</sup> <ruby><rb>但是</rb><rt>Dæn-z</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>巴结</rb><rt>pô-kyih</rt><rb>小心</rb><rt>siao-sing</rt><rb>按</rb><rt>en</rt><rb>耶和华个</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô-go</rt><rb>奴仆</rb><rt>nu-boh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>吩咐</rb><rt>feng-fu</rt><rb>尔拉个</rb><rt>ng-lah-go</rt><rb>命令</rb><rt>ming-ling</rt><rb>律法</rb><rt>lih-fah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>爱敬</rb><rt>æ-kying</rt><rb>耶和华</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>尔个</rb><rt>ng-go</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>走</rb><rt>tseo</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>一切</rb><rt>ih-tsʽih</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>路</rb><rt>lu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>守</rb><rt>siu</rt><rb>其个</rb><rt>Gyi-go</rt><rb>命令</rb><rt>ming-ling</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>联合</rb><rt>lin-ʽeh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>尽心</rb><rt>dzing-sing</rt><rb>尽灵</rb><rt>dzing-ling</rt><rb>服侍</rb><rt>voh-z</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt></ruby>。」<sup>6</sup> <u><ruby><rb>约书亚</rb><rt>Iah-shü-üô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>祝福</rb><rt>coh-foh</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>归去</rb><rt>kyü-kyʽi</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>归到</rb><rt>kyü-tao</rt><rb>自</rb><rt>zi</rt><rb>帐篷</rb><rt>tsiang-bong</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>。

<sup>7</sup> <u><ruby><rb>玛拿西</rb><rt>Mô-nô-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>一半</rb><rt>ih-pun</rt><rb>支派</rb><rt>ts-pʽa</rt><rb>摩<u>西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>产业</rb><rt>tsʽæn-nyih</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>巴珊</rb><rt>Pô-sæn</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>但是</rb><rt>dæn-z</rt><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>一半</rb><rt>ih-pun</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>约书亚</rb><rt>Iah-shü-üô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>赐拨</rb><rt>s-peh</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>产业</rb><rt>tsʽæn-nyih</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>兄弟</rb><rt>hyüong-di</rt><rb>中央</rb><rt>cong-nyiang</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>约但</rb><rt>Iah-dæn</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>河</rb><rt>ʽo</rt><rb>西边</rb><rt>si-pin</rt></ruby>。<u><ruby><rb>约书亚</rb><rt>Iah-shü-üô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>自</rb><rt>zi</rt><rb>帐篷</rb><rt>tsiang-bong</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>时候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>祝福</rb><rt>coh-foh</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt></ruby>，<sup>8</sup> <ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>Ng-lah</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>带</rb><rt>ta</rt><rb>许多</rb><rt>hyü-to</rt><rb>财物</rb><rt>dzæ-veh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>许多</rb><rt>hyü-to</rt><rb>牲口</rb><rt>sang-kʽeo</rt><rb>金银</rb><rt>kying-nying</rt><rb>铜铁</rb><rt>dong-tʽih</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>许多</rb><rt>hyü-to</rt><rb>衣裳</rb><rt>i-zông</rt><rb>归</rb><rt>kyü</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>帐篷</rb><rt>tsiang-bong</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>该</rb><rt>kæ</rt><rb>把</rb><rt>pô</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>掳掠</rb><rt>lo-liah</rt><rb>仇敌个</rb><rt>dziu-dih-go</rt><rb>财物</rb><rt>dzæ-veh</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>兄弟</rb><rt>hyüong-di</rt><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>分开</rb><rt>feng-kʽæ</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>9</sup> <u><ruby><rb>流便</rb><rt>Liu-bin</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>迦得</u>个</rb><rt>Kyüô-teh-go</rt><rb>子孙</rb><rt>ts-seng</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>玛拿西</rb><rt>Mô-nô-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>半</rb><rt>pun</rt><rb>支派个</rb><rt>ts-pʽa-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>离开</rb><rt>li-kʽæ</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>子孙</rb><rt>ts-seng</rt><rb>从</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>迦<u>南</rb><rt>Kyüô-nen</rt><rb>地</u>个</rb><rt>di-go</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>示罗</rb><rt>Z-lo</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>归到</rb><rt>kyü-tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>基列</rb><rt>Kyi-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>地</rb><rt>di</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>得着个</rb><rt>teh-djoh-go</rt><rb>地</rb><rt>di</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>照</rb><rt>tsiao</rt><rb>耶和华</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>托</rb><rt>tʽoh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>吩咐</rb><rt>feng-fu</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>得着个</rb><rt>teh-djoh-go</rt></ruby>。<sup>10</sup> <ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt><rb>一</rb><rt>ih</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>约但</rb><rt>Iah-dæn</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>河</rb><rt>ʽo</rt><rb>相近个</rb><rt>siang-gying-go</rt><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>属于</rb><rt>joh-ü</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>迦南</u>个</rb><rt>Kyüô-nen-go</rt><rb>地</rb><rt>di</rt></ruby>，<u><ruby><rb>流便</rb><rt>Liu-bin</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>、<u><ruby><rb>迦得</rb><rt>Kyüô-teh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>、<u><ruby><rb>玛拿西</rb><rt>Mô-nô-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>半</rb><rt>pun</rt><rb>支派个</rb><rt>ts-pʽa-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>约但</rb><rt>Iah-dæn</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>河边</rb><rt>ʽo-pin</rt><rb>筑</rb><rt>coh</rt><rb>一座</rb><rt>ih-zo</rt><rb>坛</rb><rt>dæn</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇座</rb><rt>keh-zo</rt><rb>坛</rb><rt>dæn</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>顶</rb><rt>ting</rt><rb>大</rb><rt>do</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>远远</rb><rt>yün-yün</rt><rb>可以</rb><rt>kʽo-yi</rt><rb>看见</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt></ruby>。<sup>11</sup> <u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>子孙</rb><rt>ts-seng</rt><rb>听闻</rb><rt>tʽing-meng</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>，<u><ruby><rb>流便</rb><rt>Liu-bin</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>迦得</u>个</rb><rt>Kyüô-teh-go</rt><rb>子孙</rb><rt>ts-seng</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>玛拿西</rb><rt>Mô-nô-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>半</rb><rt>pun</rt><rb>支派</rb><rt>ts-pʽa</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>迦<u>南</rb><rt>Kyüô-nen</rt><rb>地</u>个</rb><rt>di-go</rt><rb>极边</rb><rt>gyih-pin</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>约但</rb><rt>Iah-dæn</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>河</rb><rt>ʽo</rt><rb>相近</rb><rt>siang-gying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>属</rb><rt>joh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>子孙</rb><rt>ts-seng</rt><rb>箇边个</rb><rt>keh-pin-go</rt><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>一座</rb><rt>ih-zo</rt><rb>坛</rb><rt>dæn</rt><rb>筑好</rb><rt>coh-hao</rt></ruby>。<sup>12</sup> <u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>子孙</rb><rt>ts-seng</rt><rb>一</rb><rt>ih</rt><rb>听闻</rb><rt>tʽing-meng</rt></ruby>，<u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>全</rb><rt>djün</rt><rb>公会</rb><rt>kong-we</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>聚集</rb><rt>jü-jih</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>示罗</rb><rt>Z-lo</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>上去</rb><rt>zông-kyʽi</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>打仗</rb><rt>tang-tsiang</rt></ruby>。

<sup>13</sup> <u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>子孙</rb><rt>ts-seng</rt><rb>差</rb><rt>tsʽa</rt><rb>祭司</rb><rt>tsi-s</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以利亚撒</rb><rt>Yi-li-üô-sah</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>儿子</rb><rt>ng-ts</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>非尼哈</rb><rt>Fi-nyi-ha</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>基列</rb><rt>Kyi-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>地</rb><rt>di</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>流便</rb><rt>Liu-bin</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>迦得</u>个</rb><rt>Kyüô-teh-go</rt><rb>子孙</rb><rt>ts-seng</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>玛拿西</rb><rt>Mô-nô-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>半</rb><rt>pun</rt><rb>支派个</rb><rt>ts-pʽa-go</rt><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt></ruby>；<sup>14</sup> <ruby><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>差</rb><rt>tsʽa</rt><rb>十个</rb><rt>jih-go</rt><rb>牧伯</rb><rt>moh-pah</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>为了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>每</rb><rt>me</rt><rb>支派</rb><rt>ts-pʽa</rt><rb>差</rb><rt>tsʽa</rt><rb>一个</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>牧伯</rb><rt>moh-pah</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>房份个</rb><rt>vông-veng-go</rt><rb>族长</rb><rt>dzoh-tsiang</rt></ruby>。<sup>15</sup> <ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt><rb>走到</rb><rt>tseo-tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>基列</rb><rt>Kyi-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>地</rb><rt>di</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>流便</rb><rt>Liu-bin</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>子孙</rb><rt>ts-seng</rt></ruby>、<u><ruby><rb>迦得</rb><rt>Kyüô-teh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>子孙</rb><rt>ts-seng</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>玛拿西</rb><rt>Mô-nô-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>半</rb><rt>pun</rt><rb>支派个</rb><rt>ts-pʽa-go</rt><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：<sup>16</sup> 「<ruby><rb>耶和华个</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô-go</rt><rb>全</rb><rt>djün</rt><rb>公会</rb><rt>kong-we</rt><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：『<ruby><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>Ng-lah</rt><rb>向</rb><rt>hyiang</rt><rb>耶和华</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>犯</rb><rt>væn</rt><rb>啥个</rb><rt>soh-go</rt><rb>罪</rb><rt>ze</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>今朝</rb><rt>kying-tsiao</rt><rb>扭转</rb><rt>nyin-cün</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>依从</rb><rt>i-dzong</rt><rb>耶和华</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>今朝</rb><rt>kying-tsiao</rt><rb>筑</rb><rt>coh</rt><rb>祭坛</rb><rt>tsi-dæn</rt><rb>违背</rb><rt>we-be</rt><rb>耶和华</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>呢</rb><rt>ni</rt></ruby>？<sup>17</sup> <ruby><rb><u>比珥</u>个</rb><rt>Pi-r-go</rt><rb>罪</rb><rt>ze</rt><rb>于</rb><rt>ü</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>还</rb><rt>wa</rt><rb>欠</rb><rt>kyʽin</rt><rb>大</rb><rt>do</rt><rb>吗</rb><rt>ma</rt></ruby>？<ruby><rb>虽即</rb><rt>se-tsih</rt><rb>瘟疫</rb><rt>w̆eng-yüoh</rt><rb>临着</rb><rt>ling-djoh</rt><rb>耶和华个</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô-go</rt><rb>公会</rb><rt>kong-we</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇个</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>如今</rb><rt>jü-kying</rt><rb>还</rb><rt>wa</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>能够</rb><rt>neng-keo</rt><rb>洁净</rb><rt>kyih-zing</rt></ruby>。<sup>18</sup> <ruby><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>Ng-lah</rt><rb>今朝</rb><rt>kying-tsiao</rt><rb>还要</rb><rt>wa-iao</rt><rb>扭转</rb><rt>nyin-cün</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>依从</rb><rt>i-dzong</rt><rb>耶和华</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>吗</rb><rt>ma</rt></ruby>？<ruby><rb>今朝</rb><rt>Kying-tsiao</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>违背</rb><rt>we-be</rt><rb>耶和华</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>明朝</rb><rt>ming-tsiao</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>向</rb><rt>hyiang</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>公会</rb><rt>kong-we</rt><rb>𢛟惶</rb><rt>ô-wông</rt></ruby>。<sup>19</sup> <ruby><rb>如果</rb><rt>Jü-ko</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>得着个</rb><rt>teh-djoh-go</rt><rb>地</rb><rt>di</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>洁净</rb><rt>kyih-zing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>走过</rb><rt>tseo-ko</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>耶和华</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>得着个</rb><rt>teh-djoh-go</rt><rb>地</rb><rt>di</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>耶和华个</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô-go</rt><rb>帐篷</rb><rt>tsiang-bong</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>庉个</rb><rt>deng-go</rt><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>得着</rb><rt>teh-djoh</rt><rb>地</rb><rt>di</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>只是</rb><rt>tsih-z</rt><rb>弗要</rb><rt>feh-iao</rt><rb>违背</rb><rt>we-be</rt><rb>耶和华</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>违背</rb><rt>we-be</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>耶和华</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>神明个</rb><rt>Jing-ming-go</rt><rb>坛</rb><rt>dæn</rt><rb>之外</rb><rt>ts-nga</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>再</rb><rt>tsæ</rt><rb>筑</rb><rt>coh</rt><rb>坛</rb><rt>dæn</rt></ruby>。<sup>20</sup> <ruby><rb>从前</rb><rt>Dzong-zin</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>西拉</rb><rt>Si-lah</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>儿子</rb><rt>ng-ts</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>亚干</rb><rt>Üô-ken</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>弗是</rb><rt>feh-z</rt><rb>为了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt><rb>当该</rb><rt>tông-kæ</rt><rb>毁灭</rb><rt>hwe-mih</rt><rb>东西</rb><rt>tong-si</rt><rb>犯罪</rb><rt>væn-ze</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>𢛟惶</rb><rt>ô-wông</rt><rb>临到</rb><rt>ling-tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>众</rb><rt>cong</rt><rb>公会</rb><rt>kong-we</rt><rb>吗</rb><rt>ma</rt></ruby>？<ruby><rb>箇个</rb><rt>Keh-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>单只</rb><rt>tæn-tsih</rt><rb>自</rb><rt>zi</rt><rb>灭亡</rb><rt>mih-vông</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>其个</rb><rt>gyi-go</rt><rb>罪恶</rb><rt>ze-ôh</rt></ruby>。』」

<sup>21</sup> <ruby><rb>箇遭</rb><rt>Keh-tsao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>流便</rb><rt>Liu-bin</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>子孙</rb><rt>ts-seng</rt></ruby>、<u><ruby><rb>迦得</rb><rt>Kyüô-teh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>子孙</rb><rt>ts-seng</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>玛拿西</rb><rt>Mô-nô-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>半</rb><rt>pun</rt><rb>支派</rb><rt>ts-pʽa</rt><rb>回答</rb><rt>we-teh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>宗族个</rb><rt>tsong-dzoh-go</rt><rb>牧伯</rb><rt>moh-pah</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：<sup>22</sup> 「<ruby><rb>万</rb><rt>Væn</rt><rb>神之</rb><rt>jing-ts</rt><rb>神</rb><rt>Jing</rt><rb>耶和华</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>万</rb><rt>væn</rt><rb>神之</rb><rt>jing-ts</rt><rb>神</rb><rt>Jing</rt><rb>耶和华</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>晓得</rb><rt>hyiao-teh</rt></ruby>，<u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>必定</rb><rt>pih-ding</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>晓得</rb><rt>hyiao-teh</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>如果</rb><rt>jü-ko</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>违背</rb><rt>we-be</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>或者</rb><rt>ʽôh-tsia</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>得罪</rb><rt>teh-ze</rt><rb>耶和华个</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô-go</rt><rb>意思</rb><rt>i-s</rt></ruby>，（<ruby><rb>今朝</rb><rt>kying-tsiao</rt><rb>弗要</rb><rt>feh-iao</rt><rb>救</rb><rt>kyiu</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt></ruby>，）<sup>23</sup> <ruby><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>筑</rb><rt>coh</rt><rb>坛</rb><rt>dæn</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>骗过</rb><rt>pʽin-ko</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>依从</rb><rt>i-dzong</rt><rb>耶和华</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>或者</rb><rt>ʽôh-tsia</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>箇座</rb><rt>keh-zo</rt><rb>坛上</rb><rt>dæn-zông</rt><rb>献</rb><rt>hyin</rt><rb>燔祭</rb><rt>væn-tsi</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>素祭</rb><rt>su-tsi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>或者</rb><rt>ʽôh-tsia</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>献</rb><rt>hyin</rt><rb>谢恩祭</rb><rt>zia-eng-tsi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>但愿</rb><rt>dæn-nyün</rt><rb>耶和华</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>自</rb><rt>zi</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>刑罚</rb><rt>ying-vah</rt><rb>箇个</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>罪</rb><rt>ze</rt></ruby>：<sup>24</sup> <ruby><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>还</rb><rt>wa</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>为了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt><rb>小心</rb><rt>siao-sing</rt><rb>特意</rb><rt>deh-i</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>箇个</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>事干</rb><rt>z-ken</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>因为</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：『<ruby><rb>将来</rb><rt>Tsiang-læ</rt><rb>日子</rb><rt>nyih-ts</rt><rb>恐怕</rb><rt>kʽong-pʽô</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>子孙</rb><rt>ts-seng</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>子孙</rb><rt>ts-seng</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：<ruby><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>Ng-lah</rt><rb>于</rb><rt>ü</rt><rb>耶和华</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>啥个</rb><rt>soh-go</rt><rb>相关</rb><rt>siang-kwæn</rt></ruby>？<sup>25</sup> <ruby><rb>耶和华</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>约但</rb><rt>Iah-dæn</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>河</rb><rt>ʽo</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>址界</rb><rt>ts-ka</rt><rb>把</rb><rt>pô</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>流便</rb><rt>Liu-bin</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>子孙</rb><rt>ts-seng</rt></ruby>、<u><ruby><rb>迦得</rb><rt>Kyüô-teh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>子孙</rb><rt>ts-seng</rt><rb>分开</rb><rt>feng-kʽæ</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>在于</rb><rt>dzæ-ü</rt><rb>耶和华</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>呒分</rb><rt>m-veng</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>甚至于</rb><rt>zing-ts-ü</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>子孙</rb><rt>ts-seng</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>子孙</rb><rt>ts-seng</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>怕惧</rb><rt>pʽô-gyü</rt><rb>耶和华</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt></ruby>。』<sup>26</sup> <ruby><rb>所以</rb><rt>Sô-yi</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>预备</rb><rt>yü-be</rt><rb>筑</rb><rt>coh</rt><rb>一座</rb><rt>ih-zo</rt><rb>坛</rb><rt>dæn</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>弗是</rb><rt>feh-z</rt><rb>为了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt><rb>献</rb><rt>hyin</rt><rb>燔祭</rb><rt>væn-tsi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>弗是</rb><rt>feh-z</rt><rb>为了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt><rb>祭物</rb><rt>tsi-veh</rt></ruby>：<sup>27</sup> <ruby><rb>不过</rb><rt>peh-ko</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>后世个</rb><rt>ʽeo-shü-go</rt><rb>对证</rb><rt>te-tsing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>燔祭</rb><rt>væn-tsi</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>祭物</rb><rt>tsi-veh</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>谢恩祭</rb><rt>zia-eng-tsi</rt><rb>服侍</rb><rt>voh-z</rt><rb>耶和华</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>省勒</rb><rt>sæn-leh</rt><rb>后来</rb><rt>ʽeo-læ</rt><rb>日子</rb><rt>nyih-ts</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>子孙</rb><rt>ts-seng</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>子孙</rb><rt>ts-seng</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：『<ruby><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>Ng-lah</rt><rb>于</rb><rt>ü</rt><rb>耶和华</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>呒分个</rb><rt>m-veng-go</rt></ruby>。』<sup>28</sup> <ruby><rb>所以</rb><rt>Sô-yi</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>以后</rb><rt>Yi-ʽeo</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>或者</rb><rt>ʽôh-tsia</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>后世</rb><rt>ʽeo-shü</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：『<ruby><rb>好</rb><rt>Hao</rt><rb>看</rb><rt>kʽen</rt><rb>耶和华</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>坛个</rb><rt>dæn-go</rt><rb>样式</rb><rt>yiang-shih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>祖宗</rb><rt>tsu-tsong</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>做个</rb><rt>tso-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>弗是</rb><rt>feh-z</rt><rb>为了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt><rb>燔祭</rb><rt>væn-tsi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>弗是</rb><rt>feh-z</rt><rb>为了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt><rb>祭物</rb><rt>tsi-veh</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>中央</rb><rt>cong-nyiang</rt><rb>一个</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>对证</rb><rt>te-tsing</rt></ruby>。』<sup>29</sup> <ruby><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>Ah-lah</rt><rb>违背</rb><rt>we-be</rt><rb>耶和华</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>今末</rb><rt>kyih-mih</rt><rb>扭转</rb><rt>nyin-cün</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>依从</rb><rt>i-dzong</rt><rb>耶和华</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>耶和华</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>帐篷</rb><rt>tsiang-bong</rt><rb>面前个</rb><rt>min-zin-go</rt><rb>坛</rb><rt>dæn</rt><rb>之外</rb><rt>ts-nga</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>再</rb><rt>tsæ</rt><rb>筑</rb><rt>coh</rt><rb>一座</rb><rt>ih-zo</rt><rb>坛</rb><rt>dæn</rt><rb>为了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>献</rb><rt>hyin</rt><rb>燔祭</rb><rt>væn-tsi</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>素祭</rb><rt>su-tsi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>或者</rb><rt>ʽôh-tsia</rt><rb>别个</rb><rt>bih-go</rt><rb>祭物</rb><rt>tsi-veh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>断然</rb><rt>tön-jün</rt><rb>呒得</rb><rt>m-neh</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>意思</rb><rt>i-s</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>30</sup> <ruby><rb>祭司</rb><rt>Tsi-s</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>非尼哈</rb><rt>Fi-nyi-ha</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>公会个</rb><rt>kong-we-go</rt><rb>牧伯</rb><rt>moh-pah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>来间个</rb><rt>læ-kæn-go</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>众</rb><rt>cong</rt><rb>族长</rb><rt>dzoh-tsiang</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>听闻</rb><rt>tʽing-meng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>流便</rb><rt>Liu-bin</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>子孙</rb><rt>ts-seng</rt></ruby>、<u><ruby><rb>迦得</rb><rt>Kyüô-teh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>子孙</rb><rt>ts-seng</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>玛拿西</rb><rt>Mô-nô-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>子孙个</rb><rt>ts-seng-go</rt><rb>说话</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>和然</rb><rt>ʽo-jün</rt><rb>欢喜</rb><rt>hwun-hyi</rt></ruby>。<sup>31</sup> <ruby><rb>祭司</rb><rt>Tsi-s</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以利亚撒</rb><rt>Yi-li-üô-sah</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>儿子</rb><rt>ng-ts</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>非尼哈</rb><rt>Fi-nyi-ha</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>流便</rb><rt>Liu-bin</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>子孙</rb><rt>ts-seng</rt></ruby>、<u><ruby><rb>迦得</rb><rt>Kyüô-teh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>子孙</rb><rt>ts-seng</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>玛拿西</rb><rt>Mô-nô-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>子孙</rb><rt>ts-seng</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>Ah-lah</rt><rb>今朝</rb><rt>kying-tsiao</rt><rb>晓得</rb><rt>hyiao-teh</rt><rb>耶和华</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>果然</rb><rt>ko-jün</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>阿拉个</rb><rt>ah-lah-go</rt><rb>中央</rb><rt>cong-nyiang</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>因为</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>呒得</rb><rt>m-neh</rt><rb>向</rb><rt>hyiang</rt><rb>耶和华</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>犯</rb><rt>væn</rt><rb>箇个</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>罪</rb><rt>ze</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>现在</rb><rt>yin-dzæ</rt><rb>救</rb><rt>kyiu</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>子孙</rb><rt>ts-seng</rt><rb>脱出</rb><rt>tʽeh-cʽih</rt><rb>耶和华个</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô-go</rt><rb>手</rb><rt>siu</rt></ruby>。」<sup>32</sup> <ruby><rb>祭司</rb><rt>Tsi-s</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以利亚撒</rb><rt>Yi-li-üô-sah</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>儿子</rb><rt>ng-ts</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>非尼哈</rb><rt>Fi-nyi-ha</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>牧伯</rb><rt>moh-pah</rt><rb>从</rb><rt>dzong</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>基列</rb><rt>Kyi-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>地</rb><rt>di</rt></ruby>，<u><ruby><rb>流便</rb><rt>Liu-bin</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>子孙</rb><rt>ts-seng</rt></ruby>、<u><ruby><rb>迦得</rb><rt>Kyüô-teh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>子孙个</rb><rt>ts-seng-go</rt><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>归</rb><rt>kyü</rt><rb>迦<u>南</rb><rt>Kyüô-nen</rt><rb>地</rb><rt>di</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>子孙</rb><rt>ts-seng</rt><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt><rb>回报</rb><rt>we-pao</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt></ruby>。<sup>33</sup> <ruby><rb>箇个</rb><rt>Keh-go</rt><rb>事干</rb><rt>z-ken</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>子孙</rb><rt>ts-seng</rt><rb>欢喜</rb><rt>hwun-hyi</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>祝谢</rb><rt>coh-zia</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>再</rb><rt>tsæ</rt><rb>提起</rb><rt>di-kyʽi</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>上去</rb><rt>zông-kyʽi</rt><rb>攻打</rb><rt>kong-tang</rt><rb>毁灭</rb><rt>hwe-mih</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>流便</rb><rt>Liu-bin</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>子孙</rb><rt>ts-seng</rt></ruby>、<u><ruby><rb>迦得</rb><rt>Kyüô-teh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>子孙</rb><rt>ts-seng</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>庉个</rb><rt>deng-go</rt><rb>地</rb><rt>di</rt></ruby>。<sup>34</sup> <u><ruby><rb>流便</rb><rt>Liu-bin</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>迦得</rb><rt>Kyüô-teh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>子孙</rb><rt>ts-seng</rt><rb>取</rb><rt>cʽü</rt><rb>箇座</rb><rt>keh-zo</rt><rb>坛个</rb><rt>dæn-go</rt><rb>名字</rb><rt>ming-z</rt><rb>叫勒</rb><rt>kyiao-leh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>益突</rb><rt>Ih-deh</rt></ruby></u><sup>\[1\]</sup>，<ruby><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>因为</rb><rt>Ing-we</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>中央</rb><rt>cong-nyiang</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>对证</rb><rt>te-tsing</rt><rb>耶和华</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>是个</rb><rt>z-go</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>\[1\] *Ziu-z* Te-tsing. 就是「对证」。（原注）</sup>

### Is. XXIII.

<ruby><rb>过了</rb><rt>KO-LEH</rt><rb>许多</rb><rt>hyü-to</rt><rb>日子</rb><rt>nyih-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>耶和华</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>已经</rb><rt>yi-kying</rt><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>脱出</rb><rt>tʽeh-cʽih</rt><rb>四面</rb><rt>s-min</rt><rb>仇敌</rb><rt>dziu-dih</rt><rb>享受</rb><rt>hyiang-ziu</rt><rb>平安</rb><rt>bing-en</rt></ruby>；<u><ruby><rb>约书亚</rb><rt>Iah-shü-üô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>老了</rb><rt>lao-de</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>年纪</rb><rt>nyin-kyi</rt><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>大</rb><rt>do</rt></ruby>；<sup>2</sup> <u><ruby><rb>约书亚</rb><rt>Iah-shü-üô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>召</rb><rt>dziao</rt><rb>众</rb><rt>cong</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其拉个</rb><rt>gyi-lah-go</rt><rb>长老</rb><rt>tsiang-lao</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>族长</rb><rt>dzoh-tsiang</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>士师</rb><rt>z-s</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>官府</rb><rt>kwun-fu</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：<sup>3</sup> 「<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>老了</rb><rt>lao-de</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>年纪</rb><rt>nyin-kyi</rt><rb>大了</rb><rt>do-de</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>已经</rb><rt>yi-kying</rt><rb>看见过</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin-ko</rt><rb>耶和华</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>为了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>缘故</rb><rt>yün-kwu</rt><rb>向</rb><rt>hyiang</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>国份</rb><rt>koh-veng</rt><rb>凡百</rb><rt>væn-pah</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>做个</rb><rt>tso-go</rt><rb>事干</rb><rt>z-ken</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>因为</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>耶和华</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>替</rb><rt>tʽi</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>打仗</rb><rt>tang-tsiang</rt><rb>过了</rb><rt>ko-de</rt></ruby>。<sup>4</sup> <ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>从</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>河</rb><rt>ʽo</rt><rb>箇边</rb><rt>keh-pin</rt><rb>带</rb><rt>ta</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>祖宗</rb><rt>tsu-tsong</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>亚伯拉罕</rb><rt>Üô-pah-lah-hen</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>领</rb><rt>ling</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>走遍</rb><rt>tseo-pin</rt><rb>迦<u>南</rb><rt>Kyüô-nen</rt><rb>地</rb><rt>di</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>子孙</rb><rt>ts-seng</rt><rb>多起来</rb><rt>to-kyʽi-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>赐拨</rb><rt>s-peh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以撒</rb><rt>Yi-sah</rt></ruby></u>。<sup>4</sup> <ruby><rb>若是</rb><rt>Ziah-z</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>dziu</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>看来</rb><rt>kʽen-læ</rt><rb>服侍</rb><rt>voh-z</rt><rb>耶和华</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>今朝</rb><rt>kying-tsiao</rt><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>拣选</rb><rt>kæn-shün</rt><rb>服侍</rb><rt>voh-z</rt><rb>谁</rb><rt>jü</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>还是</rb><rt>wa-z</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>祖宗</rb><rt>tsu-tsong</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>河</rb><rt>ʽo</rt><rb>箇边</rb><rt>keh-pin</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>服侍个</rb><rt>voh-z-go</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>jing-ming</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>还</rb><rt>wa</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>庉</rb><rt>deng</rt><rb>爱</rb><rt>æ</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>地里个</rb><rt>di-li-go</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>亚摩利</rb><rt>Üô-mo-li</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人个</rb><rt>nying-go</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>jing-ming</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>不过</rb><rt>peh-ko</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>一家</rb><rt>ih-kô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>服侍</rb><rt>voh-z</rt><rb>耶和华</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt></ruby>。」<sup>16</sup> <ruby><rb>百姓</rb><rt>Pah-sing</rt><rb>回答</rb><rt>we-teh</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>弃掉</rb><rt>Kyʽi-diao</rt><rb>耶和华</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>服侍</rb><rt>voh-z</rt><rb>别个</rb><rt>bih-go</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>jing-ming</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>断断</rb><rt>tön-tön</rt><rb>呒得</rb><rt>m-neh</rt><rb>箇个</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>意思</rb><rt>i-s</rt></ruby>；<sup>17</sup> <ruby><rb>因为</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>耶和华</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>阿拉个</rb><rt>ah-lah-go</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>领</rb><rt>ling</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>祖宗</rb><rt>tsu-tsong</rt><rb>出</rb><rt>cʽih</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>埃及</rb><rt>Yiæ-gyih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>地</rb><rt>di</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>走出</rb><rt>tseo-cʽih</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>奴仆个</rb><rt>nu-boh-go</rt><rb>屋里</rb><rt>oh-li</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>做出</rb><rt>tso-cʽih</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>大</rb><rt>do</rt><rb>兆头</rb><rt>ziao-deo</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>眼前</rb><rt>ngæn-zin</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>保护</rb><rt>pao-wu</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>凡百</rb><rt>væn-pah</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>去个</rb><rt>kyʽi-go</rt><rb>路</rb><rt>lu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>凡百</rb><rt>væn-pah</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>走过个</rb><rt>tseo-ko-go</rt><rb>百姓</rb><rt>pah-sing</rt><rb>中央</rb><rt>cong-nyiang</rt></ruby>；<sup>18</sup> <ruby><rb>耶和华</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>面前</rb><rt>min-zin</rt><rb>赶出</rb><rt>ken-cʽih</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>众</rb><rt>cong</rt><rb>百姓</rb><rt>pah-sing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>庉</rb><rt>deng</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>地个</rb><rt>di-go</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>亚摩利</rb><rt>Üô-mo-li</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>所以</rb><rt>sô-yi</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>服侍</rb><rt>voh-z</rt><rb>耶和华</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>因为</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>阿拉个</rb><rt>ah-lah-go</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt></ruby>。」<sup>19</sup> <u><ruby><rb>约书亚</rb><rt>Iah-shü-üô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>百姓</rb><rt>pah-sing</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>Ng-lah</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>能够</rb><rt>neng-keo</rt><rb>服侍</rb><rt>voh-z</rt><rb>耶和华</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>因为</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>耶和华</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>圣善</rb><rt>Sing-jün</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>嫉邪个</rb><rt>dzih-zia-go</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>弗会</rb><rt>feh-we</rt><rb>赦掉</rb><rt>sô-diao</rt><rb>尔拉个</rb><rt>ng-lah-go</rt><rb>过犯</rb><rt>ko-væn</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>尔拉个</rb><rt>ng-lah-go</rt><rb>罪</rb><rt>ze</rt></ruby>。<sup>20</sup> <ruby><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>Ng-lah</rt><rb>若是</rb><rt>ziah-z</rt><rb>弃掉</rb><rt>kyʽi-diao</rt><rb>耶和华</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>服侍</rb><rt>voh-z</rt><rb>别个</rb><rt>bih-go</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>jing-ming</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>耶和华</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>赐</rb><rt>s</rt><rb>福气</rb><rt>foh-kyʽi</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>之后</rb><rt>ts-ʽeo</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>反转</rb><rt>fæn-cün</rt><rb>降</rb><rt>kông</rt><rb>祸祟</rb><rt>ʽo-se</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>除灭</rb><rt>djü-mih</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt></ruby>。」<sup>21</sup> <ruby><rb>百姓</rb><rt>Pah-sing</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>约书亚</rb><rt>Iah-shü-üô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>弗是介</rb><rt>Feh-z-ka</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>必定</rb><rt>pih-ding</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>服侍</rb><rt>voh-z</rt><rb>耶和华</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt></ruby>。」<sup>22</sup> <u><ruby><rb>约书亚</rb><rt>Iah-shü-üô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>百姓</rb><rt>pah-sing</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>Ng-lah</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>自个</rb><rt>zi-go</rt><rb>对证</rb><rt>te-tsing</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>拣择</rb><rt>kæn-dzeh</rt><rb>耶和华</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>服侍</rb><rt>voh-z</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt></ruby>。」<ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>Ah-lah</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>对证</rb><rt>te-tsing</rt></ruby>。」<sup>23</sup> <u><ruby><rb>约书亚</rb><rt>Iah-shü-üô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>现在</rb><rt>Yin-dzæ</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>该</rb><rt>kæ</rt><rb>除掉</rb><rt>djü-diao</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>中央</rb><rt>cong-nyiang</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>有个</rb><rt>yiu-go</rt><rb>别个</rb><rt>bih-go</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>jing-ming</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>一心</rb><rt>ih-sing</rt><rb>向着</rb><rt>hyiang-djoh</rt><rb>耶和华</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>以色列</u>个</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih-go</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt></ruby>。」<sup>24</sup> <ruby><rb>百姓</rb><rt>Pah-sing</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>约书亚</rb><rt>Iah-shü-üô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>Ah-lah</rt><rb>必定</rb><rt>pih-ding</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>服侍</rb><rt>voh-z</rt><rb>耶和华</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>阿拉个</rb><rt>ah-lah-go</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>听从</rb><rt>tʽing-dzong</rt><rb>其个</rb><rt>Gyi-go</rt><rb>说话</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt></ruby>。」<sup>25</sup> <ruby><rb>所以</rb><rt>Sô-yi</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>约书亚</rb><rt>Iah-shü-üô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>箇日</rb><rt>keh-nyih</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>百姓</rb><rt>pah-sing</rt><rb>立约</rb><rt>lih-iah</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>示剑</rb><rt>Z-kyin</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>为了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>立定</rb><rt>lih-ding</rt><rb>规条</rb><rt>kwe-diao</rt><rb>律例</rb><rt>lih-li</rt></ruby>。

<sup>26</sup> <u><ruby><rb>约书亚</rb><rt>Iah-shü-üô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>把</rb><rt>pô</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>说话</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt><rb>写</rb><rt>sia</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>神明个</rb><rt>Jing-ming-go</rt><rb>律法</rb><rt>lih-fah</rt><rb>书里</rb><rt>shü-li</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>再</rb><rt>tsæ</rt><rb>驮</rb><rt>do</rt><rb>一块</rb><rt>ih-kwʽe</rt><rb>大大</rb><rt>do-do</rt><rb>石头</rb><rt>zah-deo</rt><rb>放</rb><rt>fông</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>箇头</rb><rt>keh-deo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>耶和华</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>帐篷</rb><rt>tsiang-bong</rt><rb>旁边个</rb><rt>bông-pin-go</rt><rb>橡树</rb><rt>ziang-jü</rt><rb>下</rb><rt>ʽô</rt></ruby>。<sup>27</sup> <u><ruby><rb>约书亚</rb><rt>Iah-shü-üô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>众</rb><rt>cong</rt><rb>百姓</rb><rt>pah-sing</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>箇块</rb><rt>Keh-kwʽe</rt><rb>石头</rb><rt>zah-deo</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>见证</rb><rt>kyin-tsing</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>因为</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>听闻</rb><rt>tʽing-meng</rt><rb>耶和华</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>讲过</rb><rt>kông-ko</rt><rb>凡百</rb><rt>væn-pah</rt><rb>说话</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>所以</rb><rt>sô-yi</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>见证</rb><rt>kyin-tsing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>恐怕</rb><rt>kʽong-pʽô</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>弃掉</rb><rt>kyʽi-diao</rt><rb>尔拉个</rb><rt>ng-lah-go</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt></ruby>。」<sup>28</sup> <u><ruby><rb>约书亚</rb><rt>Iah-shü-üô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>散开</rb><rt>sæn-kʽæ</rt><rb>百姓</rb><rt>pah-sing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>各人</rb><rt>koh-nying</rt><rb>归</rb><rt>kwe</rt><rb>自个</rb><rt>zi-go</rt><rb>产业</rb><rt>tsʽæn-nyih</rt><rb>地</rb><rt>di</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>。

<sup>29</sup> <ruby><rb>箇星</rb><rt>Keh-sing</rt><rb>事干</rb><rt>z-ken</rt><rb>之后</rb><rt>ts-ʽeo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>耶和华个</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô-go</rt><rb>奴仆</rb><rt>nu-boh</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>嫩</u>个</rb><rt>Neng-go</rt><rb>儿子</rb><rt>ng-ts</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>约书亚</rb><rt>Iah-shü-üô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>死了</rb><rt>si-de</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>年纪</rb><rt>nyin-kyi</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>一百</rb><rt>ih-pah</rt><rb>十</rb><rt>jih</rt><rb>岁</rb><rt>shü</rt></ruby>。<sup>30</sup> <ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt><rb>安葬</rb><rt>en-tsông</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>产业个</rb><rt>tsʽæn-nyih-go</rt><rb>地界</rb><rt>di-ka</rt><rb>里向</rb><rt>li-hyiang</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以法莲</rb><rt>Yi-fah-lin</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>山地</rb><rt>sæn-di</rt></ruby>，<u><ruby><rb>迦实</rb><rt>Kyüô-jih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>山个</rb><rt>sæn-go</rt><rb>北边</rb><rt>poh-pin</rt></ruby>，<u><ruby><rb>亭讷</rb><rt>Ding-neh</rt></ruby></u> <u><ruby><rb>西拉</rb><rt>si-lah</rt></ruby></u>。<sup>31</sup> <u><ruby><rb>约书亚</rb><rt>Iah-shü-üô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>在世</rb><rt>dzæ-shü</rt><rb>时候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>约书亚</rb><rt>Iah-shü-üô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>死后</rb><rt>si-ʽeo</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>长老</rb><rt>tsiang-lao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>晓得</rb><rt>hyiao-teh</rt><rb>耶和华</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>为了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>一切</rb><rt>ih-tsʽih</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>做个</rb><rt>tso-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>还</rb><rt>wa</rt><rb>在世</rb><rt>dzæ-shü</rt><rb>时候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt></ruby>，<u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>服侍</rb><rt>voh-z</rt><rb>耶和华</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt></ruby>。<sup>32</sup> <u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>子孙</rb><rt>ts-seng</rt><rb>从</rb><rt>dzong</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>埃及</rb><rt>Yiæ-gyih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>带上来</rb><rt>ta-zông-læ</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>约瑟</u>个</rb><rt>Iah-seh-go</rt><rb>骨头</rb><rt>kweh-deo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>葬</rb><rt>tsông</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>示剑</rb><rt>Z-kyin</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>雅各</rb><rt>Yüô-koh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>从前</rb><rt>dzong-zin</rt><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>一百</rb><rt>ih-pah</rt><rb>块</rb><rt>kwʽe</rt><rb>银子</rb><rt>nying-ts</rt><rb>向</rb><rt>hyiang</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>示剑</rb><rt>Z-kyin</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>阿爹</rb><rt>ah-tia</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>哈抹</rb><rt>Ha-meh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>子孙</rb><rt>ts-seng</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>买来个</rb><rt>ma-læ-go</rt><rb>箇块</rb><rt>keh-kwʽe</rt><rb>地</rb><rt>di</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>约瑟</rb><rt>Iah-seh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>子孙个</rb><rt>ts-seng-go</rt><rb>产业</rb><rt>tsʽæn-nyih</rt></ruby>。<sup>33</sup> <u><ruby><rb>亚伦</rb><rt>Üô-leng</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>儿子</rb><rt>ng-ts</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以利亚撒</rb><rt>Yi-li-üô-sah</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>死了</rb><rt>si-de</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>葬</rb><rt>tsông</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>儿子</rb><rt>ng-ts</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>非尼哈</u>个</rb><rt>Fi-nyi-ha-go</rt><rb>山上</rb><rt>sæn-zông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以法莲</rb><rt>Yi-fah-lin</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>山地</rb><rt>sæn-di</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>得着个</rb><rt>teh-djoh-go</rt></ruby>。

---
